[Q6] Select the most appropriate option to fill in Blank (1).

(b) has
(c) had
(d) hasbeen
[ANS] b

has The given sentence is in the present perfect tense and the subject 'Kodagu district administration' is singular. 'Singular Subject + has + V3' is the correct grammatical structure for it. Hence, 'has' is the most appropriate answer.

(c) agriculture
(d) seri culture
[ANS] b

apiculture 'Apiculture'means the activity of keeping bees to produce honey. The given passage states that the festival is aimed at encouraging apiculture in Kodagu in view of the worldwide demand for Coorg honey. Hence, 'apiculture' is the most appropriate answer.

done to Past participle is the third form of the verb and denotes a completed action. It is used to describe a noun to provide additional information about it. Similarly, in the given passage, it provides additional information about the noun 'harm'. Hence, 'done(V3) to' is the most appropriate answer.

(d) Flagrant 'Flagrant' means an action considered wrong or immoral, conspicuously or obviously offensive. Hence, 'hectic' is the most appropriate answer. (a) Indulge 'Indulge' means to allow oneself to enjoy the pleasure of something. [Q814] Passage: Forests are essential for the survival of life on Earth. They (1) ______ oxygen, regulate the climate, and provide habitats for countless species. However, rapid deforestation is destroying these vital ecosystems.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) consume
(b) produce
(c) destroy
(d) extract
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Contextually, forests release or produce oxygen through photosynthesis.
उदा. संदर्भ के अनुसार, जंगल प्रकाश संश्लेषण के through ऑक्सीजन का उत्पादन (produce) करते हैं।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q815] Passage: Reading books is a wonderful habit. It broadens our horizons and (2) ______ our imagination. Good books act as our best friends and guides in life.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) diminishes
(b) restricts
(c) enhances
(d) weakens
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Reading books enhances (improves) imagination, making it the correct positive word.
उदा. किताबें पढ़ने से हमारी कल्पना शक्ति बढ़ती है (enhances), अतः यह सही विकल्प है।
[RULE] Vocabulary & Tone

---

[Q816] Passage: Pollution has become a major threat to humanity. Industrial waste and vehicle emissions are heavily (3) ______ the air and water resources.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) purifying
(b) cleaning
(c) polluting
(d) protecting
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Industrial waste and vehicle emissions pollute air and water.
उदा. औद्योगिक कचरा और वाहनों से निकलने वाला धुआं हवा और पानी को दूषित (polluting) कर रहा है।
[RULE] Contextual Fit

---

[Q817] Passage: Success does not come over night. It requires consistent effort, dedication, and the ability to (4) ______ failures without losing hope.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) overcome
(b) welcome
(c) ignore
(d) invite
[ANS] a
[SOL]
We 'overcome' failures to achieve success.
उदा. सफलता पाने के लिए हमें असफलताओं से पार पाना (overcome) होता है।
[RULE] Collocation

---

[Q818] Passage: Traveling is not just about visiting new places; it is also about experiencing different cultures. When we travel, we learn to (5) ______ diversity.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) appreciate
(b) criticize
(c) reject
(d) ignore
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Traveling helps people appreciate cultural diversity.
उदा. यात्रा करना हमें सांस्कृतिक विविधता की सराहना (appreciate) करना सिखाता है।
[RULE] Contextual Meaning

---

[Q819] Passage: Honesty is the best policy. An honest person is trusted by every one. He never resorts to unfair means to achieve his goals, which brings him (6) ______ peace of mind.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) lasting
(b) temporary
(c) fleeting
(d) brief
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Honesty brings long-lasting or permanent peace of mind.
उदा. ईमानदारी से मानसिक शांति स्थायी (lasting) मिलती है।
[RULE] Adjective-Noun Collocation

---

[Q820] Passage: Sports play a vital role in student life. They teach us team work discipline, and stamina. More over, regular physical activity keeps us physically (7) ______.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) unfit
(b) lazy
(c) active
(d) sluggish
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Regular physical activity keeps us active and healthy.
उदा. नियमित शारीरिक गतिविधि हमें सक्रिय (active) और स्वस्थ रखती है।
[RULE] Logical Continuity

---

[Q821] Passage: Technology has transformed our lives completely. Today, communication is instantaneous due to smartphones and the internet, bridging distances and (8) ______ the world closer.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) bringing
(b) taking
(c) pushing
(d) keeping
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase is 'bringing the world closer'.
उदा. सही वाक्यांश 'bringing the world closer' (दुनिया को करीब लाना) है।
[RULE] Fixed Phrase / Idiomatic Usage

---

[Q822] Passage: Water is precious. With depleting ground water levels, it is our prime duty to conserve water. We must (9) ______ rain water harvesting methods in every house hold.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) abandon
(b) adopt
(c) neglect
(d) discard
[ANS] b
[SOL]
To save water, we need to 'adopt' rainwater harvesting.
उदा. पानी बचाने के लिए हमें वर्षा जल संचयन को अपनाना (adopt) चाहिए।
[RULE] Vocabulary in Context

---

[Q823] Passage: Time and tide wait for none. Once time is lost, it can never be (10) ______ back. Therefore, we should utilize every single moment productively.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) taken
(b) brought
(c) gotten
(d) pulled
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The passive form 'brought back' is idiomatically correct here.
उदा. खोया हुआ समय वापस नहीं लाया जा सकता, इसलिए 'brought back' सही है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs / Collocation

---

[Q824] Books are our best friends. They (1) us company when we are lonely.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) give
(b) keep
(c) provide
(d) bring
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The standard collocation is 'keep company', which means to stay with someone so that they are not alone.
उदा. 'Keep company' एक निश्चित collocation है जिसका अर्थ किसी का साथ देना होता है।
[RULE] Collocations

---

[Q825] A good book can always (2) us valuable knowledge and guidance.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) lend
(b) borrow
(c) impart
(d) transfer
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'Impart' means to pass on information, knowledge, or skills. Books impart knowledge.
उदा. 'Impart' का प्रयोग ज्ञान या सूचना देने के अर्थ में किया जाता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Word Choice

---

[Q826] Unlike bad friends, books never (3) us down in times of difficulty.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) let
(b) put
(c) pull
(d) bring
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrasal verb 'let down' means to disappoint someone.
उदा. 'Let down' एक phrasal verb है जिसका अर्थ निराश करना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q827] Reading good books helps in (4) our personality and broadens our out look
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) developing
(b) making
(c) creating
(d) building
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Developing' is the most appropriate word used with personality, mindset, or skills.
उदा. व्यक्तित्व (personality) के विकास के लिए 'developing' सबसे उपयुक्त शब्द है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q828] Therefore, we should cultivate the habit of reading regularly (5) our own benefit.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) with
(d) by
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The preposition 'for' indicates the purpose or recipient of a benefit (for our benefit).
उदा. लाभ या उद्देश्य को दर्शाने के लिए preposition 'for' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Prepositions

---

[Q829] Pollution is a serious problem that (1) all living beings on Earth.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) affects
(b) effects
(c) destroys
(d) spoils
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Affects' is a verb meaning to influence or produce a change in something.
उदा. 'Affects' एक verb है जिसका अर्थ प्रभावित करना है, जबकि 'effect' noun है।
[RULE] Confusing Words (Affect vs Effect)

---

[Q830] Industrial waste and vehicle smoke are the main sources (2) air pollution.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) in
(b) of
(c) from
(d) at
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The noun 'sources' is followed by the preposition 'of' to indicate origin.
उदा. 'Sources of' का प्रयोग किसी चीज़ के स्रोत को बताने के लिए किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q831] Governments across the world are taking steps to (3) the rising pollution levels.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) increase
(b) curb
(c) boost
(d) promote
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Curb' means to restrain or control something undesirable, like pollution.
उदा. 'Curb' का अर्थ किसी बुरी चीज़ (जैसे प्रदूषण) को नियंत्रित करना या रोकना होता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary

---

[Q832] Citizens must also contribute (4) planting more trees in their neighborhoods.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) by
(b) through
(c) in
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Contribute by + gerund' shows the method or manner in which a contribution is made.
उदा. 'Contribute by' का प्रयोग यह बताने के लिए होता है कि किस तरीके से योगदान दिया जा रहा है।
[RULE] Prepositions & Gerunds

---

[Q833] Only collective efforts can ensure a clean and healthy environment (5) future generations.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) towards
(d) on
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'For future generations' indicates intended beneficiaries or future recipients.
उदा. भविष्य की पीढ़ियों के लिए अर्थ देने हेतु preposition 'for' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Prepositions

---

[Q834] She _______ the news yesterday and was shocked. [1]
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) receive
(b) received
(c) receiving
(d) receives
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule: Simple Past Tense. For regular verbs, add –ed to the base form.
उदाहरण: उसने समाचार सुना। (She heard the news.)
[RULE] Simple Past Tense

---

[Q835] By the time the meeting ended, the team _______ 15 reports. [1]
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) finished
(b) has finished
(c) had finished
(d) will finish
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Rule: Past Perfect Tense. Use ‘had’ + past participle to express an action completed before another past event.
उदाहरण: उसने बैठक से पहले रिपोर्ट पूरी कर ली थी।
[RULE] Past Perfect Tense

---

[Q836] The new policy _______ on all employees next month. [1]
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) will be implemented
(b) is implementing
(c) will implement
(d) implements
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Passive Voice in Future Tense. Use ‘will be’ + past participle for future passive.
उदाहरण: नीति अगले महीने सभी कर्मचारियों पर लागू होगी।
[RULE] Passive Voice - Future

---

[Q837] He could _______ the ball if he had practiced. [1]
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) kick
(b) kicked
(c) kicking
(d) to kick
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Modal Verb ‘could’ + base verb to express past ability.
उदाहरण: वह गेंद को किक कर सकता था।
[RULE] Modal Verb (could) + Base Verb

---

[Q838] My car is _______ than yours. [1]
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) fast
(b) faster
(c) fastest
(d) fastestly
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule: Comparative Adjective. Add –er for one-step comparison.
उदाहरण: मेरी कार आपकी कार से तेज है।
[RULE] Comparative Adjective

---

[Q839] She _______ to finish the assignment before the dead line [1]
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) will
(b) has
(c) had
(d) would
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Future Simple. Use ‘will’ + base verb for predictions or promises.
उदाहरण: वह समय पर कार्य पूरा करेगी।
[RULE] Future Simple Tense

---

[Q840] They _______ the project at the moment. [1]
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) work
(b) working
(c) worked
(d) to work
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule: Present Continuous. Use ‘am/is/are’ + verb+ing to talk about actions happening now.
उदाहरण: वे परियोजना पर काम कर रहे हैं।
[RULE] Present Continuous Tense

---

[Q841] While I _______ the book, the phone rang. [1]
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) read
(b) was reading
(c) reading
(d) have read
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule: Past Continuous. Use ‘was/were’ + verb+ing to describe an action in progress in the past.
उदाहरण: मैं किताब पढ़ रहा था, तभी फोन बजा।
[RULE] Past Continuous Tense

---

[Q842] If he _______ earlier, he would have caught the train. [1]
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) left
(b) had left
(c) leaves
(d) leaving
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule: Third Conditional. Use ‘had’ + past participle in the if-clause and ‘would have’ + past participle in the main clause.
उदाहरण: यदि वह पहले निकल गया होता, तो वह ट्रेन पकड़ पाता।
[RULE] Third Conditional

---

[Q843] He asked me _______ the train schedule. [1]
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to know
(b) know
(c) knowing
(d) knows
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Infinitive of purpose after verbs of request. Use ‘to’ + base verb.
उदाहरण: उसने मुझसे ट्रेन समय जानने के लिए कहा।
[RULE] Infinitive after Verb of Request

---

[Q844] I enjoy _______ books on history. [1]
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reading
(b) read
(c) to read
(d) readed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Gerund after verbs of enjoyment. Use verb+ing.
उदाहरण: मुझे इतिहास की किताबें पढ़ना पसंद है।
[RULE] Gerund after Verb of Enjoyment

---

[Q845] The authorities announced that the new policy would [1] the use of plastic bags.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) ban
(b) baned
(c) banned
(d) banning
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When a modal verb like 'would' is used, the base form of the main verb follows it.
मॉडल क्रिया के बाद मूल क्रिया का रूप प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Modal + Base Verb

---

[Q846] She has been living in this city for [1] years.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) five
(b) fifth
(c) fives
(d) fiven
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Cardinal numbers are used with time expressions, not ordinal numbers.
समय के साथ कार्डिनल संख्या का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Cardinal Numbers with Time

---

[Q847] The scientist, who was born in 1955, [1] the Nobel Prize in 2006.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) received
(b) receives
(c) receiving
(d) receive
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past simple is used for a completed action in the past.
भूतकाल की सरल क्रिया का उपयोग पूर्ण हुई क्रिया के लिए किया जाता है।
[RULE] Past Simple

---

[Q848] Despite the heavy rain, the match was not [1].
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) canceled
(b) cancelling
(c) cancelled
(d) cancel
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past participle is used after a form of 'to be' for a passive construction.
पैसिव में 'to be' के बाद पास्ट पार्टिसिपल का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Passive Voice with Past Participle

---

[Q849] He was the first person to [1] the mountain.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) climbed
(b) climbs
(c) climb
(d) climbing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past simple follows 'first person to' to indicate a completed action.
'first person to' के बाद भूतकाल का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Past Simple after 'First Person To'

---

[Q850] The committee will meet at [1] hour.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) six
(b) sixth
(c) sixth hour
(d) Skeptical
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Cardinal numbers are used when referring to time.
समय बताने के लिए कार्डिनल संख्या का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Cardinal Numbers with Time

---

[Q851] I would have gone to the party if I [1] enough time.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had
(b) has
(c) having
(d) have
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past perfect is used after 'would have' in a conditional clause.
मॉडल + 'would have' के बाद पर्फेक्ट टेंस का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conditional Past Perfect

---

[Q852] The children were delighted to see the [1] fireworks.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) bright
(b) brightest
(c) brightly
(d) brightness
[ANS] a
[SOL]
An adjective is used before a noun to describe it.
संज्ञा से पहले गुणवाचक विशेषण का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Adjective Before Noun

---

[Q853] She is known for her [1] sense of humor.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) quick
(b) quickly
(c) quicker
(d) quickness
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Adjectives modify nouns; adverbs modify verbs, adjectives, or other adverbs.
विशेषण संज्ञा को संशोधित करते हैं, जबकि क्रिया विशेषण क्रिया, विशेषण या अन्य क्रिया विशेषण को संशोधित करते हैं।
[RULE] Adjective vs Adverb

---

[Q854] The company will release the new product next month, and the market response is expected to [1].
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) be
(b) is
(c) was
(d) being
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Future simple after 'expected to' requires the base form of the verb.
'expected to' के बाद बेस फ़ॉर्म का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Future Simple after 'Expected To'

---

[Q855] Passage: Education is not merely about acquiring knowledge; it is about shaping character and fostering critical thin king In today's fast-paced world, students often (1) ______ immense pressure to excel academically. This constant stress can (2) ______ their mental well being Therefore, schools must (3) ______ a balanced environment where extracurricular activities are given equal (4) ______.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) face
(b) facing
(c) faced
(d) faces
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Plural subject 'students' requires the base form of the verb 'face' in the simple present tense.
उदा. बहुवचन कर्ता 'students' के साथ Present Indefinite Tense में वर्ब की पहली फॉर्म (face) का प्रयोग होगा।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q856] Passage: Education is not merely about acquiring knowledge; it is about shaping character and fostering critical thin king In today's fast-paced world, students often face immense pressure to excel academically. This constant stress can (2) ______ their mental well being Therefore, schools must (3) ______ a balanced environment where extracurricular activities are given equal (4) ______.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) improve
(b) damage
(c) elevate
(d) strengthen
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Modal verb 'can' is followed by a verb. Based on the context of 'constant stress', it negatively affects mental health, so 'damage' is the appropriate word.
उदा. मॉडल वर्ब 'can' के बाद वर्ब आती है। 'तनाव' (stress) का मानसिक स्वास्थ्य पर नकारात्मक प्रभाव पड़ता है, इसलिए 'damage' सही है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q857] Passage: Education is not merely about acquiring knowledge; it is about shaping character and fostering critical thin king In today's fast-paced world, students often face immense pressure to excel academically. This constant stress can damage their mental well being Therefore, schools must (3) ______ a balanced environment where extracurricular activities are given equal (4) ______.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) create
(b) destroy
(c) ignore
(d) reduce
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After modal 'must', a base verb is required. To counteract stress, schools need to 'create' a balanced environment.
उदा. मॉडल 'must' के बाद बेस वर्ब चाहिए। तनाव से निपटने के लिए स्कूलों को संतुलित माहौल 'create' करना चाहिए।
[RULE] Modals + Verb Base Form

---

[Q858] Passage: Education is not merely about acquiring knowledge; it is about shaping character and fostering critical thin king In today's fast-paced world, students often face immense pressure to excel academically. This constant stress can damage their mental well being Therefore, schools must create a balanced environment where extracurricular activities are given equal (4) ______.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) neglect
(b) importance
(c) punishment
(d) friction
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The context implies giving equal value or 'importance' to extracurricular activities alongside academics.
उदा. संदर्भ यह है कि पाठ्येतर गतिविधियों को भी पढ़ाई के बराबर 'importance' (महत्व) दी जानी चाहिए।
[RULE] Noun Collocations

---

[Q859] Passage: Technology has radically transformed the way we communicate. Gone are the days when letters (1) ______ weeks to reach their destination. Today, messages are transmitted globally in (2) ______ seconds. While this connectivity has brought people closer, it has also (3) ______ concerns regarding privacy and digital addiction. Hence, responsible usage of technology is the (4) ______ of the hour.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) take
(b) took
(c) takes
(d) taking
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The sentence refers to past time ('Gone are the days when...'), so the past tense 'took' is correct.
उदा. वाक्य भूतकाल की बात कर रहा है ('Gone are the days when...'), इसलिए Past Indefinite Tense की वर्ब 'took' सही है।
[RULE] Tenses (Past Context)

---

[Q860] Passage: Technology has radically transformed the way we communicate. Gone are the days when letters took weeks to reach their destination. Today, messages are transmitted globally in (2) ______ seconds. While this connectivity has brought people closer, it has also raised concerns regarding privacy and digital addiction. Hence, responsible usage of technology is the (4) ______ of the hour.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) few
(b) a few
(c) the few
(d) fewer
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'A few' means a small number and is used positively to denote quick transmission of messages within a short span.
उदा. 'A few' का अर्थ है 'कुछ', जो सकारात्मक अर्थ में कम समय में संदेश पहुंचने को दर्शाता है।
[RULE] Determiners (Articles / Quantifiers)

---

[Q861] Passage: Technology has radically transformed the way we communicate. Gone are the days when letters took weeks to reach their destination. Today, messages are transmitted globally in a few seconds. While this connectivity has brought people closer, it has also (3) ______ concerns regarding privacy and digital addiction. Hence, responsible usage of technology is the (4) ______ of the hour.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) allayed
(b) raised
(c) extinguished
(d) eliminated
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The phrase 'raised concerns' is a standard collocation used when negative issues or doubts are brought up.
उदा. वाक्यांश 'raised concerns' एक निश्चित संयोजन (collocation) है जिसका प्रयोग चिंताएं पैदा होने के संदर्भ में होता है।
[RULE] Collocations

---

[Q862] Passage: Technology has radically transformed the way we communicate. Gone are the days when letters took weeks to reach their destination. Today, messages are transmitted globally in a few seconds. While this connectivity has brought people closer, it has also raised concerns regarding privacy and digital addiction. Hence, responsible usage of technology is the (4) ______ of the hour.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) need
(b) needlessly
(c) needy
(d) needy's
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The idiom is 'need of the hour', meaning something that is urgently required at the present time.
उदा. सही मुहावरा 'need of the hour' (समय की मांग) है, जिसके लिए यहाँ केवल 'need' noun फिट बैठता है।
[RULE] Idiomatic Expressions

---

[Q863] Passage: Wild life conservation is essential for maintaining the ecological balance of our planet. Many species of animals are currently (1) ______ extinction due to habitat loss and poaching. Governments and NGOs must (2) ______ strict laws to protect these vulnerable creatures. Public awareness campaigns can also (3) ______ a vital role in educating communities about the importance of (4) ______ nature.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) facing
(b) facing with
(c) faced
(d) face
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Present continuous tense 'are currently facing' indicates an ongoing action or threat.
उदा. Present Continuous tense ('are currently facing') का प्रयोग वर्तमान में जारी खतरे को दर्शाने के लिए किया गया है।
[RULE] Verb Tenses

---

[Q864] Passage: Wild life conservation is essential for maintaining the ecological balance of our planet. Many species of animals are currently facing extinction due to habitat loss and poaching. Governments and NGOs must (2) ______ strict laws to protect these vulnerable creatures. Public awareness campaigns can also (3) ______ a vital role in educating communities about the importance of (4) ______ nature.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) play
(b) make
(c) do
(d) take
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The standard English collocation is 'play a vital role' or 'play a role'.
उदा. निश्चित अंग्रेजी संयोजन (collocation) 'play a vital role' होता है, जिसका अर्थ है महत्वपूर्ण भूमिका निभाना।
[RULE] Fixed Collocations

---

[Q865] Passage: Reading books is a wonderful habit. It (1) ______ our horizons and enriches our minds. Options for blank 1
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) broadens
(b) narrows
(c) heightens
(d) deepens
[ANS] a
[SOL]
We use the verb 'broadens' with 'horizons' as a standard collocation meaning to expand one's knowledge or experience.
उदा. क्षितिज (horizons) को विस्तृत करने के लिए 'broadens' सबसे उपयुक्त शब्द है।
[RULE] Collocation

---

[Q866] Passage: Books are our best friends. They guide us (2) ______ difficult times and never leave us alone.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) through
(b) across
(c) over
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'through' is used to indicate moving from one end to another of a period of time or difficult situation.
उदा. कठिन समय से निकलने के लिए 'through' प्रीपोजिशन का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Prepositions

---

[Q867] Passage: Technology has revolutionized communication. However, it also has (3) ______ effects on human relationships.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) adverse
(b) aversive
(c) diverse
(d) reverse
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Adverse' means harmful or unfavorable, which fits the context of negative effects of technology.
उदा. प्रतिकूल प्रभावों के लिए 'adverse' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Confusable Words

---

[Q868] Passage: Success in life depends largely on hard work and perseverance. Those who (4) ______ obstacles with courage always succeed.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) face
(b) escape
(c) avoid
(d) ignore
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The context requires a verb meaning to confront or deal with obstacles. 'Face' is the correct term.
उदा. बाधाओं का सामना करने के लिए 'face' सही शब्द है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q869] Passage: The government has launched a new scheme to (5) ______ poverty in rural areas.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) eradicate
(b) cultivate
(c) originate
(d) generate
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Eradicate' means to destroy completely, which is the standard collocation used with poverty.
उदा. गरीबी को पूरी तरह समाप्त करने (eradicate) के लिए यह उपयुक्त शब्द है।
[RULE] Collocation / Vocabulary

---

[Q870] Passage: Honesty is the best policy. An honest person is trusted and respected (6) ______ every one in society.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) by
(b) with
(c) from
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In passive voice sentences, the doer of the action is preceded by the preposition 'by'.
उदा. पैसिव वॉइस में कर्ता से पहले 'by' प्रीपोजिशन का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Passive Voice Preposition

---

[Q871] Passage: Forests are essential for maintaining ecological balance. They (7) ______ oxygen and absorb carbon dioxide.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) release
(b) absorb
(c) consume
(d) extract
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Trees take in carbon dioxide and 'release' oxygen during photosynthesis.
उदा. पेड़ ऑक्सीजन छोड़ते (release) हैं।
[RULE] Scientific Context Vocabulary

---

[Q872] Passage: Time and tide wait for none. Once lost, time can never be (8) ______.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) recovered
(b) recalled
(c) retrieved
(d) reserved
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Recovered' means to get back something lost, which fits best with lost time.
उदा. खोए हुए समय को वापस पाने के लिए 'recovered' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Contextual Word Choice

---

[Q873] Passage: Travelling is an important part of education. It (9) ______ our knowledge by showing us different cultures.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) enhances
(b) decreases
(c) lessens
(d) reduces
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Travelling increases or improves knowledge, making 'enhances' the correct positive verb.
उदा. ज्ञान बढ़ाने के लिए 'enhances' सबसे उपयुक्त है।
[RULE] Positive/Negative Tone Matching

---

[Q874] Passage: Physical exercise is crucial for good health. It keeps our body active and (10) ______.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) fit
(b) fat
(c) weak
(d) lazy
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The standard pairing with 'active' in fitness contexts is 'fit'.
उदा. 'active and fit' एक सामान्य जोड़ी (collocation) है।
[RULE] Collocation

---

[Q875] The ancient city of Petra, located in Jordan, is renowned for its rock-cut architecture. It was carved out of the sandstone cliffs by the Nabateans around 4th century BCE, and it became a major trading hub. The city’s most famous land mark the Treasury, is a testament to the Nabateans' skill in engineering and art. The ruins of Petra are now a UNESCO World Heritage site, attracting tourists from all over the world. 1. is 2. carved 3. renowned
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) is, carved, renowned
(b) was, carved, famous
(c) is, cut, renowned
(d) was, cut, famous
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The first blank uses the present simple form of the verb 'to be' because the sentence states a general fact. The second blank uses the past participle 'carved' in the passive voice to describe an action that occurred in the past. The third blank uses the adjective 'renowned' to describe the city. 
उदा. The museum **is** famous for its collection.
[RULE] Present Simple / Past Participle in Passive Voice / Adjective Usage

---

[Q876] When planning a trip, it is essential to book accommodations early and to arrange transportation in advance. If you wait until the last minute, you may find that the hotels are **(1)** full and the flight tickets **(2)** expensive. Therefore, it is advisable to start your booking process **(3)** months before your departure date. 1. filled 2. are 3. a few
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) filled, are, a few
(b) full, were, few
(c) filled, was, a few
(d) full, are, a few
[ANS] d
[SOL]
The correct adjectives are 'full' (not 'filled') and the verb form 'are' to agree with the plural noun 'flight tickets'. The adverbial phrase 'a few months' indicates a short period. 
उदा. The room is **full** and the tickets are **expensive**.
[RULE] Adjective Agreement / Verb Agreement / Adverbial Phrase

---

[Q877] The company will launch its new product line next quarter, **(1)** it will face stiff competition from established brands. However, they believe that the product’s unique features will attract customers. **(2)**, the marketing team will intensify its promotional activities to gain a competitive edge. 1. although 2. furthermore
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) although, furthermore
(b) however, moreover
(c) but, also
(d) yet, furthermore
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The first blank requires a correlative conjunction that introduces a contrast: 'although'. The second blank uses a correlative conjunction that adds information: 'furthermore'. 
उदा. I wanted to go, **but** I stayed home.
[RULE] Correlative Conjunctions

---

[Q878] She only wanted to go out **(1)**, but she realized that she had to finish her assignment first. The dead line was close, and she did not want to **(2)** anyone. Finally, she decided to study for two hours and then go for a short walk. 1. to relax 2. disappoint
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to relax, disappoint
(b) to relax, disappoints
(c) to relax, disappointed
(d) to relax, disappointedly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adverbial clause of concession uses the infinitive 'to relax'. The verb 'disappoint' is in base form because it follows the modal 'to'. 
उदा. He came **to** help, but he could not help.
[RULE] Adverbial Clause of Concession / Infinitive Usage

---

[Q879] When I was a child, I could **(1)** a kite in the park. My brother could also **(2)** one, but he lost it on the first day. The memory remains vivid because of the bright colors and the wind that **(3)** the kite high. 1. fly 2. fly 3. lifted
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) fly, fly, lifted
(b) fly, fly, lifts
(c) fly, flew, lifted
(d) flying, fly, lifts
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The modal verb 'could' is used for past ability, so the base form 'fly' is correct. The past tense of the verb in the last blank is 'lifted' to match the past narrative. 
उदा. I **could** swim when I was five.
[RULE] Modal Verb 'Could' for Past Ability

---

[Q880] The new smartphone has a screen that is **(1)** than the previous model. It is also lighter and more durable. Users find it easier to handle the device, especially during long calls. The battery life is **(2)**, and the camera quality has improved significantly. 1. bigger 2. good
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) larger, better
(b) bigger, good
(c) bigger, better
(d) Affluent
[ANS] c
[SOL]
When comparing two things, 'bigger' is the comparative form of 'big'. For battery life, the comparative adjective is 'better' because we compare the quality of battery life. 
उदा. This bag is **heavier** than the other.
[RULE] Comparative Adjectives

---

[Q881] She said, "I will finish the report by tomorrow." The manager replied, **(1)** "you should have completed it by now." The conversation highlights a difference in tense usage in reported speech. 1. that she should have finished
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) that she should have finished
(b) that she has finished
(c) that she had finished
(d) that she will finish
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Reported speech converts the future tense 'will finish' to the past tense 'should have finished', reflecting the shift in reporting verb. 
उदा. He said **that** he was tired.
[RULE] Reported Speech – Future to Past

---

[Q882] If I **(1)** more time, I would travel to Japan. Unfortunately, I have a busy schedule. **(2)** I have a lot of work, I promise to visit Japan next year. 1. had 2. though
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had, though
(b) have, though
(c) had, nevertheless
(d) has, though
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The second conditional uses the past simple 'had' in the if‑clause. The concession is introduced by 'though' to show contrast. 
उदा. **If** I had money, I would buy a car.
[RULE] Second Conditional / Concessive Clause

---

[Q883] She bought **(1)** apple from the market. The fruit was **(2)** than the one I bought yesterday. She also purchased **(3)** oranges, which were ripe and sweet. 1. an 2. fresher 3. some
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) an, fresher, some
(b) a, fresher, some
(c) an, fresher, a
(d) Venerable
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The indefinite article 'an' is used before a vowel sound. The comparative adjective 'fresher' compares the apples. 'Some' is used for an unspecified quantity of oranges. 
उदा. I bought **an** apple.
[RULE] Indefinite Articles / Comparative Adjectives / Quantifiers

---

[Q884] If I **(1)** you, I would not ignore the warning. The situation is serious, and ignoring the alert could lead to serious consequences. **(2)** I had warned you, you would have taken precautions. 1. were 2. Had
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) were, Had
(b) was, Had
(c) Tenacious
(d) Vibrant
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The subjunctive mood uses 'were' in hypothetical statements. In the second clause, the past perfect 'had warned' is used to express a past unreal condition. 
उदा. If I **were** you, I would leave.
[RULE] Subjunctive Mood / Past Perfect Conditional

---

[Q885] The government has decided to ________ the use of plastic bags in the market to protect the environment.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) cut
(b) cutting
(c) cuts
(d) to cut
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Use of infinitive after ‘decided to’. Example: I decided to go to the market. 
उदाहरण: मैंने बाजार जाने का निर्णय लिया।
[RULE] Infinitive after 'decide to'

---

[Q886] She has been working in the office ________ 2015.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) during
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Use ‘since’ to indicate the starting point of an action that continues to the present. Example: We have lived here since 2010.
उदाहरण: हम 2010 से यहाँ रह रहे हैं।
[RULE] Since for continuous actions

---

[Q887] The committee met, but no decision was ________.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) made
(b) made up
(c) make
(d) making
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Past passive form of ‘make’. Example: The problem was solved by the team. 
उदाहरण: समस्या टीम द्वारा हल की गई।
[RULE] Past passive

---

[Q888] I would have attended the meeting had I ________ the invitation.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) received
(b) get
(c) receiveded
(d) receiveding
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Past perfect tense for unreal past condition. Example: If I had known, I would have helped.
उदाहरण: अगर मुझे पता होता, तो मैं मदद करता।
[RULE] Past perfect in conditionals

---

[Q889] They suggested that we ________ the project earlier.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) start
(b) started
(c) starting
(d) to start
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Subjunctive mood after ‘suggest’. Example: I suggest that he leave early. 
उदाहरण: मैं सुझाव देता हूँ कि वह जल्दी चले जाए।
[RULE] Subjunctive after 'suggest'

---

[Q890] The book, which is on the table, belongs to ________.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) me
(b) I
(c) my
(d) mine
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Rule: Use of possessive pronoun ‘mine’ to show ownership. Example: The red pen is mine.
उदाहरण: लाल कलम मेरा है।
[RULE] Possessive pronoun usage

---

[Q891] He will be ________ at the conference next week.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) present
(b) presented
(c) presenting
(d) to present
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Rule: Gerund after ‘be’ to indicate activity. Example: She will be studying tomorrow.
उदाहरण: वह कल पढ़ रही होगी।
[RULE] Gerund after 'be'

---

[Q892] The photographs were taken ________ the mountains.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) in
(c) at
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: ‘taken by’ indicates the photographer. Example: The painting was made by the artist.
उदाहरण: पेंटिंग कलाकार द्वारा बनाई गई।
[RULE] Preposition with ‘taken’

---

[Q893] If it rains tomorrow, we will cancel the picnic ________.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) then
(b) when
(c) therefore
(d) so
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: ‘then’ is used to indicate consequence in conditional sentences. Example: If you study, you will pass, then you can celebrate.
उदाहरण: यदि आप पढ़ते हैं, तो आप पास हो जाएंगे, फिर आप जश्न मना सकते हैं।
[RULE] Conditional ‘then’

---

[Q894] The city has been experiencing a severe drought, which has caused a ________ in the water supply.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) shortage
(b) shorter
(c) short
(d) shorting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Use of noun ‘shortage’ to denote lack. Example: There is a shortage of vaccines.
उदाहरण: टीकों की कमी है।
[RULE] Noun usage for lack

---

[Q895] Passage: Forests are essential for life on Earth. They (1) ______ oxygen, store carbon dioxide, and provide habitats for millions of species.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) consume
(b) produce
(c) destroy
(d) absorb
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Contextual meaning: Forests give out or produce oxygen for living organisms.
उदा. संदर्भ के अनुसार, जंगल ऑक्सीजन पैदा करते हैं।
[RULE] Vocabulary and Contextual Usage

---

[Q896] Passage: Regular exercise is important for maintaining good health. It helps in (2) ______ weight and building muscle strength.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) gaining
(b) losing
(c) increasing
(d) expanding
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Contextual collocation: 'losing weight' is the standard collocation when talking about the benefits of exercise.
उदा. वजन कम करने (losing weight) के लिए नियमित व्यायाम महत्वपूर्ण है।
[RULE] Collocation

---

[Q897] Passage: The invention of the internet has completely (3) ______ the way we communicate and access in form at i on.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) transformed
(b) hindered
(c) delayed
(d) reversed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextual meaning: The internet changed communication completely, meaning it 'transformed' it.
उदा. इंटरनेट ने संचार के तरीके को पूरी तरह से बदल (transform) दिया है।
[RULE] Synonyms / Contextual Fit

---

[Q898] Passage: Reading books not only enhances vocabulary but also (4) ______ our imagination and analytical skills.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) weakens
(b) reduces
(c) sharpens
(d) dulls
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Contextual meaning: Positive effect on imagination implies sharpening or improving it.
उदा. किताबें पढ़ने से हमारी कल्पना शक्ति और विश्लेषणात्मक कौशल तेज (sharpens) होते हैं।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q899] Passage: Renewable energy sources like solar and wind power are crucial because they do not (5) ______ harmful pollutants into the atmosphere.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) absorb
(b) collect
(c) release
(d) trap
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Contextual meaning: Pollutants are emitted or released into the atmosphere.
उदा. अक्षय ऊर्जा स्रोत हानिकारक प्रदूषकों को वायुमंडल में नहीं छोड़ते (release) हैं।
[RULE] Verbs & Collocations

---

[Q900] Passage: Honesty is the best policy. An honest person is always trusted and respected by (6) ______ in society.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) nobody
(b) everyone
(c) nowhere
(d) none
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Contextual meaning: An honest person is respected by all people or everyone.
उदा. एक ईमानदार व्यक्ति का समाज में हर कोई (everyone) सम्मान करता है।
[RULE] Pronouns & Determiners

---

[Q901] Passage: Travelling broadens our mind. It exposes us to different cultures and traditions, helping us become more (7) ______ individuals.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) narrow-minded
(b) tolerant
(c) hostile
(d) ignorant
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Contextual meaning: Exposure to cultures makes a person accepting and tolerant.
उदा. यात्रा हमें अधिक सहनशील (tolerant) व्यक्ति बनने में मदद करती है।
[RULE] Adjective Context

---

[Q902] Passage: Water conservation is the need of the hour. If we do not stop wasting water, we will soon face a severe water (8) ______.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) abundance
(b) surplus
(c) scarcity
(d) luxury
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Contextual meaning: Wasting water leads to a shortage or scarcity of water.
उदा. अगर हमने पानी की बर्बादी नहीं रोकी, तो जल्द ही गंभीर जल संकट (scarcity) का सामना करना पड़ेगा।
[RULE] Vocabulary and Tone

---

[Q903] Passage: Hard work is the key to success. There are no shortcuts to achieving your goals; one must persevere (9) ______ difficult times.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) through
(b) above
(c) beneath
(d) onto
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Preposition usage: 'Through difficult times' means enduring and persisting across difficult phases.
उदा. कठिन समय के दौरान (through) डटे रहना आवश्यक है।
[RULE] Prepositions

---

[Q904] Passage: Time and tide wait for none. We must learn to manage our time wisely so that we can accomplish our tasks (10) ______.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) inefficiently
(b) efficiently
(c) carelessly
(d) slowly
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Contextual meaning: Managing time wisely helps in accomplishing tasks well and efficiently.
उदा. बुद्धिमानी से समय का प्रबंधन करने से हम अपने कार्यों को कुशलतापूर्वक (efficiently) पूरा कर सकते हैं।
[RULE] Adverb Usage

---

[Q905] Passage: Forests are essential for life on Earth. They (1) ______ oxygen, store carbon dioxide, and support countless species of plants and animals.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) produce
(b) consume
(c) destroy
(d) waste
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextual meaning requires a verb that signifies the release of oxygen by forests. 'Produce' is the correct scientific and contextual fit.
उदा. संदर्भ के अनुसार, जंगल ऑक्सीजन छोड़ते हैं (उत्पन्न करते हैं), इसलिए 'produce' सही विकल्प है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q906] Passage: Despite knowing the risks of smoking, many people continue to (2) ______ in this harmful habit.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) indulge
(b) refrain
(c) abstain
(d) cease
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Indulge in' is a fixed collocation meaning to allow oneself to enjoy the pleasure of something, especially something bad.
उदा. 'Indulge in' एक निश्चित वाक्यांश (collocation) है जिसका अर्थ किसी बुरी आदत में लिप्त होना है।
[RULE] Fixed Collocations

---

[Q907] Passage: The government has launched a new scheme to (3) ______ financial assistance to small scale farmers across the country.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) provide
(b) borrow
(c) withhold
(d) reduce
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The context is about a government scheme offering monetary help, making 'provide' the logical choice.
उदा. योजना का उद्देश्य किसानों को वित्तीय सहायता 'प्रदान करना' (provide) है।
[RULE] Contextual Usage

---

[Q908] Passage: Reading books not only expands our knowledge but also (4) ______ our imagination and creative thin king
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) stimulates
(b) stifles
(c) blocks
(d) dulls
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Stimulates' means to encourage or foster development, which aligns positively with expanding knowledge and imagination.
उदा. 'Stimulates' का अर्थ बढ़ावा देना है, जो ज्ञान और कल्पना को बढ़ाने के सकारात्मक संदर्भ में फिट बैठता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary & Tone

---

[Q909] Passage: The committee members could not reach a consensus because they had (5) ______ views on the new education policy.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) divergent
(b) identical
(c) similar
(d) uniform
[ANS] a
[SOL]
If they could not reach a consensus (agreement), it implies their views were different or 'divergent'.
उदा. यदि वे किसी सहमति पर नहीं पहुँच सके, तो इसका कारण उनके विचार 'भिन्न' (divergent) होना था।
[RULE] Logical Consistency

---

[Q910] Passage: Technology has dramatically transformed the way we communicate, making the world feel much (6) ______ than before.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) smaller
(b) larger
(c) colder
(d) darker
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Faster communication through technology figuratively shrinks the world, making it feel 'smaller'.
उदा. तकनीक के कारण संचार तेज हो गया है जिससे दुनिया छोटी (smaller) महसूस होने लगी है।
[RULE] Figurative Context

---

[Q911] Passage: Regular exercise and a balanced diet are (7) ______ for maintaining good physical and mental health.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) crucial
(b) trivial
(c) harmful
(d) optional
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Crucial' means extremely important, which fits the necessity of exercise and a good diet for health.
उदा. स्वास्थ्य के लिए व्यायाम और आहार महत्वपूर्ण हैं, इसलिए 'crucial' सही शब्द है।
[RULE] Adjective Choice

---

[Q912] Passage: Despite facing severe economic hardships, the entrepreneur refused to (8) ______ and eventually built a successful enterprise.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) give up
(b) take off
(c) look down
(d) turn on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Give up' is a phrasal verb meaning to stop trying or surrender, which contrasts with building a successful enterprise later.
उदा. 'Give up' एक phrasal verb है जिसका अर्थ हार मानना या प्रयास छोड़ना है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q913] Passage: The ancient monuments of our country are a proud heritage that we must protect and (9) ______ for future generations.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) preserve
(b) demolish
(c) neglect
(d) abandon
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Heritage sites must be protected and 'preserved' (kept safe from harm or decay).
उदा. ऐतिहासिक स्मारकों की रक्षा और उन्हें भविष्य की पीढ़ियों के लिए 'संरक्षित' (preserve) करना चाहिए।
[RULE] Synonym & Tone Match

---

[Q914] Passage: Sleep deprivation can seriously impair cognitive functions, reducing concentration and (10) ______ decision-making abilities.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) impairing
(b) enhancing
(c) improving
(d) sharpening
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Since sleep deprivation negatively impacts cognitive functions ('impair'), it also negatively affects decision making ('impairing'). Parallel negative tone is required.
उदा. नींद की कमी से संज्ञानात्मक कार्य खराब होते हैं, जिससे निर्णय लेने की क्षमता भी प्रभावित (impairing) होती है।
[RULE] Parallelism & Tone

---

[Q915] The committee had decided to postpone the meeting, but _______ did not want to abandon the schedule.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) they
(b) he
(c) she
(d) it
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Subject-verb agreement: The noun *committee* is singular, so the pronoun must be singular *it*.
उदाहरण: The committee is ready. (समिति तैयार है)
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q916] Despite the rain, the children were excited to _______ the outdoor game.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) play
(b) played
(c) playing
(d) plays
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Infinitive after *to*: After *to*, use the base form of the verb.
उदाहरण: I want to play. (मैं खेलना चाहता हूँ)
[RULE] Infinitive after 'to'

---

[Q917] The scientist had been working on the experiment for years, yet the results remained _______.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) uncertain
(b) certain
(c) uncertainly
(d) uncertainties
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Adjective usage: *uncertain* describes the noun *results*.
उदाहरण: The outcome was uncertain. (परिणाम अनिश्चित थे)
[RULE] Adjective vs Adverb

---

[Q918] If you _______ the instructions carefully, you will avoid mistakes.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) follow
(b) following
(c) followed
(d) follows
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Infinitive after *if*: Use the base form of the verb.
उदाहरण: If you follow the steps, you succeed. (यदि आप चरणों का पालन करते हैं)
[RULE] Infinitive after 'if'

---

[Q919] The new policy will _______ the company to meet its environmental targets.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) help
(b) helps
(c) helped
(d) helping
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Modal verb *will* is followed by the base form *help*.
उदाहरण: It will help us. (यह हमारी मदद करेगा)
[RULE] Modal Verb + Base Form

---

[Q920] By the time the train arrived, the passengers had already _______ their luggage.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) packed
(b) packing
(c) pack
(d) packs
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past participle used after *had already*: *packed* is the correct past participle.
उदाहरण: She had packed her bag. (उसने अपना बैग पैक किया था)
[RULE] Past Participle

---

[Q921] She told me that she would _______ me the documents tomorrow.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) send
(b) sent
(c) sending
(d) sends
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Modal verb *would* uses the base form *send*.
उदाहरण: He would send the letter. (वह पत्र भेजेगा)
[RULE] Modal Verb + Base Form

---

[Q922] The old house was in a state of _______ and needed renovation.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) decay
(b) decayed
(c) decaying
(d) decays
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Noun form used after *state of*: *decay* is a noun.
उदाहरण: The state of decay was evident. (अवस्था क्षय की स्पष्ट थी)
[RULE] Noun vs Adjective

---

[Q923] He had to _______ the decision before the dead line
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) make
(b) made
(c) making
(d) makes
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Infinitive after *to*: use base form *make*.
उदाहरण: I need to make a choice. (मुझे एक विकल्प चुनना है)
[RULE] Infinitive

---

[Q924] The evidence presented in court was _______ to the jury.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) convincing
(b) convince
(c) convinceing
(d) Munificent
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Adjective used as complement: *convincing* describes the evidence.
उदाहरण: The evidence was convincing. (साक्ष्य विश्वसनीय थे)
[RULE] Adjective as complement

---

[Q925] Education is the key to success. It ____ (1) the mind and helps us grow as individuals.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) shapes
(b) breaks
(c) hides
(d) ignores
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextually, education shapes the mind positively.
उदा. संदर्भ के अनुसार, शिक्षा हमारे दिमाग को आकार (shapes) देती है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q926] Pollution has become a major threat. We must take immediate steps to ____ (2) the environment.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) destroy
(b) protect
(c) pollute
(d) neglect
[ANS] b
[SOL]
To counter pollution, we need to protect the environment.
उदा. प्रदूषण से निपटने के लिए हमें पर्यावरण की रक्षा (protect) करनी चाहिए।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q927] Hard work always ____ (3) off in the end, no matter how difficult the path is.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) pays
(b) takes
(c) brings
(d) puts
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrasal idiom/collocation is 'pays off' which means to yield good results.
उदा. 'Pays off' एक निश्चित collocation है जिसका अर्थ सफल होना होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Collocation / Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q928] Books are our best companions. They provide us ____ (4) knowledge and wisdom.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) from
(b) with
(c) by
(d) to
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb 'provide' is correctly followed by the preposition 'with' when providing a thing to a person.
उदा. 'Provide someone with something' निश्चित प्रीपोजिशन संरचना है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q929] Technology has made the world a global village. Communication has become very ____ (5).
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) difficult
(b) slow
(c) easy
(d) complex
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Technology makes communication easy and fast, fitting the positive context.
उदा. तकनीक ने संचार को बहुत आसान (easy) बना दिया है।
[RULE] Contextual Appropriateness

---

[Q930] Physical exercise is essential for health. It keeps our body ____ (6) and active.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) fat
(b) lazy
(c) fit
(d) weak
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Exercise keeps the body fit and active, forming a synonymous pair.
उदा. व्यायाम शरीर को फिट (fit) और सक्रिय रखता है।
[RULE] Contextual Synonyms / Parallelism

---

[Q931] Honesty is the best policy. An honest person is trusted ____ (7) every one.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) with
(c) from
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In passive voice structures, 'trusted' is followed by 'by' to denote the doer.
उदा. पैसिव वॉइस में एजेंट को दर्शाने के लिए 'by' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Passive Voice Preposition

---

[Q932] Time and tide wait for none. We should never ____ (8) precious time.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) utilize
(b) waste
(c) save
(d) value
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Based on the proverb 'Time and tide wait for none', wasting time is discouraged.
उदा. कहावत के अनुसार, हमें कीमती समय कभी बर्बाद (waste) नहीं करना चाहिए।
[RULE] Proverbial Context

---

[Q933] Reading habits should be cultivated from childhood. It ____ (9) vocabulary and comprehension.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) worsens
(b) reduces
(c) improves
(d) destroys
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Reading habit positively improves vocabulary.
उदा. पढ़ने की आदत से शब्दावली में सुधार (improves) होता है।
[RULE] Positive/Negative Tone Agreement

---

[Q934] Success does not come over night. It ____ (10) consistent effort and dedication.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) requires
(b) avoids
(c) dismisses
(d) refuses
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Achieving success requires consistent effort.
उदा. सफलता पाने के लिए निरंतर प्रयास की आवश्यकता (requires) होती है।

[Q935] Read the passage and fill in blank (1): 'Education is not merely about acquiring knowledge, but it also _________ character and instills moral values in a student.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) destroys
(b) shapes
(c) ignores
(d) ruins
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Contextual vocabulary: 'shapes' is the appropriate verb to show building or forming character.
उदा. संदर्भ के अनुसार, चरित्र का निर्माण करने के लिए 'shapes' सही शब्द है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary / Collocation

---

[Q936] Read the passage and fill in blank (2): 'Pollution has become a major threat to our environment, and urgent steps must be taken to _________ it.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) encourage
(b) promote
(c) mitigate
(d) increase
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'Mitigate' means to make less severe, which fits the context of reducing pollution.
उदा. 'Mitigate' का अर्थ कम करना होता है, जो प्रदूषण को कम करने के संदर्भ में बिल्कुल सही है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Synonyms in Context

---

[Q937] Read the passage and fill in blank (3): 'Hard work and perseverance are the keys to success. Those who do not give up easily are _________ to achieve their goals.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) bound
(b) unlikely
(c) failed
(d) reluctant
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Are bound to' is a fixed phrase meaning certain or destined to do something.
उदा. 'Are bound to' एक निश्चित वाक्यांश है जिसका अर्थ 'तय है' होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Phrases / Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q938] Read the passage and fill in blank (4): 'Books are our best companions. They enrich our minds and _________ us with valuable insights into human life.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) deprive
(b) rob
(c) provide
(d) strip
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The verb 'provide' correctly pairs with 'with' (provide someone with something) to show giving insights.
उदा. 'Provide' सही क्रिया है जो 'with' के साथ मिलकर किसी को कुछ प्रदान करने का अर्थ देती है।
[RULE] Prepositional Verb Agreement

---

[Q939] Read the passage and fill in blank (5): 'The ancient ruins of the city bear testimony to a glorious civilization that flourished _________ centuries ago.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) many
(b) much
(c) more
(d) most
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Centuries' is a countable plural noun, so the determiner 'many' must be used.
उदा. 'Centuries' एक गणनीय बहुवचन संज्ञा है, इसलिए इसके साथ 'many' का प्रयोग होगा।
[RULE] Determiners / Quantifiers

---

[Q940] Read the passage and fill in blank (6): 'Despite facing numerous hurdles, the young entrepreneur refused to _________ his dream of launching a start up
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) pursue
(b) abandon
(c) achieve
(d) fulfill
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The conjunction 'Despite' indicates a contrast; hence, refusing to 'abandon' (give up) a dream fits logically.
उदा. 'Despite' विरोधाभास दर्शाता है, इसलिए अपने सपने को छोड़ने (abandon) से मना करना तार्किक है।
[RULE] Contextual Logic / Contrast

---

[Q941] Read the passage and fill in blank (7): 'Regular physical exercise helps in maintaining good health and _________ the risk of chronic diseases.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) increasing
(b) elevating
(c) minimizing
(d) aggravating
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Good health is maintained by reducing or minimizing the risk of diseases.
उदा. अच्छा स्वास्थ्य बीमारियों के जोखिम को कम (minimizing) करके बनाए रखा जाता है।
[RULE] Logical Consistency / Vocabulary

---

[Q942] Read the passage and fill in blank (8): 'Technology has transformed our lives completely. _________, it has also brought along several challenges regarding privacy.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Therefore
(b) Furthermore
(c) However
(d) Consequently
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'However' is used to introduce a contrasting statement against the positive transformation of technology.
उदा. तकनीक के सकारात्मक बदलाव के विपरीत एक चुनौती को पेश करने के लिए 'However' (हालाँकि) का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Transition Words / Conjunctions

---

[Q943] Read the passage and fill in blank (9): Wild life conservation is the need of the hour. We must protect endangered species from _________ extinction.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) imminent
(b) distant
(c) unlikely
(d) doubtful
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Imminent' means about to happen very soon, fitting the threat faced by endangered species.
उदा. 'Imminent' का अर्थ आसन्न या निकट भविष्य में घटने वाला खतरा है।
[RULE] Adjective-Noun Collocation

---

[Q944] Read the passage and fill in blank (10): 'Effective communication is not just about speaking fluently, but also about _________ attentively to others.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) ignoring
(b) listening
(c) interrupting
(d) neglecting
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Communication requires both speaking and listening; 'listening attentively' completes the balanced idea.
उदा. संवाद में बोलने के साथ-साथ ध्यान से सुनना (listening attentively) भी आवश्यक है।
[RULE] Parallelism / Contextual Meaning

---

[Q945] The main [1] of the policy is to improve education.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) aim
(b) aims
(c) aimed
(d) aiming
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The noun *aim* is singular and requires a singular verb *is*. In Hindi: नीति का मुख्य उद्देश्य शिक्षा में सुधार करना है।
[RULE] Singular noun with singular verb

---

[Q946] The scientists were astonished by the [1] of the new species.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) discoveries
(b) discovery
(c) discovering
(d) discovered
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The noun *discovery* is singular and matches the singular verb *was*. In Hindi: वैज्ञानिकों को नई प्रजाति की खोज से आश्चर्य हुआ।
[RULE] Singular noun with singular verb

---

[Q947] The manager [1] a meeting at 10 a.m.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) schedules
(b) schedule
(c) scheduled
(d) scheduling
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Past tense of *schedule* is *scheduled*. In Hindi: प्रबंधक ने 10 बजे एक बैठक का आयोजन किया।
[RULE] Past tense of regular verbs

---

[Q948] She has been living in London [1] 2010.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) during
(d) before
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *since* is used for a starting point in time. In Hindi: वह 2010 से लंदन में रह रही है।
[RULE] Since for time duration

---

[Q949] If I [1] you, I would apologize.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) am
(d) are
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Subjunctive mood uses *were* for hypothetical situations. In Hindi: अगर मैं तुम्हारा होता, तो मैं माफी मांगता।
[RULE] Subjunctive mood

---

[Q950] The book, along with its illustrations, [1] expensive.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) is
(b) are
(c) was
(d) were
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The subject *book* is singular; hence *is* is used. In Hindi: पुस्तक, चित्रों के साथ, महंगी है।
[RULE] Singular noun with singular verb

---

[Q951] The committee will announce the results [1] next week.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) in
(c) at
(d) during
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Preposition *in* is used for a future time period. In Hindi: समिति अगले सप्ताह परिणाम घोषित करेगी।
[RULE] Preposition for time

---

[Q952] They have [1] the project for months.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) worked on
(b) work on
(c) working on
(d) worked
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Present perfect tense *have worked on* indicates an action that started in the past and continues. In Hindi: वे महीनों से परियोजना पर काम कर रहे हैं।
[RULE] Present perfect tense

---

[Q953] I would have gone to the party if I [1] the invitation.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had received
(b) have received
(c) received
(d) getting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Third conditional uses *had received* in the if-clause. In Hindi: अगर मैंने निमंत्रण प्राप्त किया होता, तो मैं पार्टी में जाता।
[RULE] Third conditional

---

[Q954] The children were delighted because the teacher [1] them a trip.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) gave
(b) gave to
(c) give
(d) have given
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past simple *gave* is used for a completed action in the past. In Hindi: बच्चों को खुश कर दिया क्योंकि शिक्षक ने उन्हें एक यात्रा दी।
[RULE] Past simple tense

---

[Q955] Communication is a vital part of human existence. We share our thoughts and feelings _____(1) others through words, gestures, and expressions.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) to
(b) with
(c) from
(d) against
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The correct preposition to use with the verb 'share' when dealing with people is 'with'. 
उदा. 'Share with others' का अर्थ है दूसरों के साथ साझा करना। यहाँ 'with' सही प्रीपोजिशन है।
[RULE] Preposition usage

---

[Q956] Books are our best friends. They guide us when we are lost and _____ (2) us when we are sad.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) comfort
(b) annoy
(c) frighten
(d) disturb
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextually, books provide solace or comfort when someone is sad. 
उदा. जब हम दुखी होते हैं, तो किताबें हमें सांत्वना (comfort) देती हैं, न कि परेशान करती हैं।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q957] Regular exercise is essential for maintaining good health. It keeps the body active and _____ (3) various diseases.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) invites
(b) causes
(c) prevents
(d) encourages
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Exercise helps in stopping or keeping diseases away, which means 'prevents'.
उदा. नियमित व्यायाम विभिन्न बीमारियों को रोकता (prevents) है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q958] Pollution has become a major global threat. Governments world wide are taking steps to _____ (4) carbon emissions.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) increase
(b) reduce
(c) promote
(d) generate
[ANS] b
[SOL]
To combat global threats like pollution, governments take steps to lower or 'reduce' emissions.
उदा. प्रदूषण से निपटने के लिए सरकारें कार्बन उत्सर्जन को कम (reduce) करने के कदम उठा रही हैं।
[RULE] Collocation and Vocabulary

---

[Q959] The museum had a vast collection of ancient artifacts. Visitors were instructed not to _____ (5) any of the delicate exhibits.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) touch
(b) admire
(c) observe
(d) sketch
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Museum guidelines generally prohibit visitors from physically contacting delicate items, hence 'touch'.
उदा. संग्रहालयों में अक्सर नाजुक प्रदर्शनों को छूने (touch) की मनाही होती है।
[RULE] Contextual Appropriateness

---

[Q960] Hard work is the key to success. Those who are persistent and never _____ (6) up eventually achieve their goals.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) give
(b) take
(c) look
(d) bring
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrasal verb 'give up' means to stop trying or surrender.
उदा. 'Give up' का अर्थ हार मानना होता है। कभी हार न मानने वाले लोग अंततः सफल होते हैं।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q961] Technology has revolutionized education. Online learning has made education accessible to students residing in _____ (7) areas.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) urban
(b) remote
(c) metropolitan
(d) central
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Online learning bridges the gap by reaching out to far-flung or 'remote' areas.
उदा. ऑनलाइन शिक्षा ने दूरदराज (remote) क्षेत्रों में रहने वाले छात्रों के लिए शिक्षा को सुलभ बना दिया है।
[RULE] Adjective-Noun Collocation

---

[Q962] Honesty is a great virtue. An honest person is trusted by every one and always speaks the _____ (8).
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) lie
(b) truth
(c) fact
(d) rumour
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The standard collocation with 'speaks' in the context of honesty is 'the truth'.
उदा. ईमानदार व्यक्ति हमेशा सच बोलता है, जिसके लिए 'speaks the truth' सही वाक्यांश है।
[RULE] Fixed Collocation

---

[Q963] Forests play a crucial role in maintaining ecological balance. Unfortunately, large scale deforestation is leading to the _____ (9) of many wild life species.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) protection
(b) survival
(c) extinction
(d) growth
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Deforestation destroys habitats, resulting in the permanent loss or 'extinction' of species.
उदा. बड़े पैमाने पर वनों की कटाई कई वन्यजीव प्रजातियों के विलुप्त होने (extinction) का कारण बन रही है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q964] Time and tide wait for none. We must utilize every moment wisely and never _____ (10) time on trivial matters.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) spend
(b) waste
(c) invest
(d) save
[ANS] b
[SOL]
In the context of 'trivial matters' (worthless things), time is said to be 'wasted'.
उदा. तुच्छ बातों पर समय बर्बाद (waste) नहीं करना चाहिए।
[RULE] Contextual Appropriateness

---

[Q965] Air pollution is a major environmental issue that ___(1)___ millions of people world wide.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) affects
(b) effects
(c) affecting
(d) effect
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Here, a verb is needed in the simple present tense to show a general fact about pollution impacting people. 'Affects' is the verb form.
उदा. यहाँ एक क्रिया (verb) की आवश्यकता है जो वर्तमान काल में हो। 'Affects' एक verb है जिसका अर्थ है 'प्रभाव डालना'।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement / Verbs

---

[Q966] Governments must take strict ___(2)___ to curb the rising levels of toxic emissions.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Timid
(b) steps
(c) actions
(d) measures
[ANS] d
[SOL]
'Take measures' is a fixed collocation in English meaning to implement actions or precautions.
उदा. 'Take measures' एक निश्चित शब्द-युग्म (collocation) है जिसका अर्थ है आवश्यक कदम उठाना।
[RULE] Collocations

---

[Q967] Deforestation has ___(3)___ reduced the natural habitat of many wild animals.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) drastic
(b) drastically
(c) drasticness
(d) drasticed
[ANS] b
[SOL]
An adverb is needed to modify the past participle verb 'reduced'. Therefore, 'drastically' is correct.
उदा. क्रिया के रूप 'reduced' की विशेषता बताने के लिए adverb (drastically) का प्रयोग होगा।
[RULE] Adverb vs Adjective

---

[Q968] Water scarcity is becoming a ___(4)___ problem in many developing nations.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) severe
(b) severely
(c) severity
(d) severing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
An adjective is required before the noun 'problem'. 'Severe' functions as an adjective meaning grave or serious.
उदा. संज्ञा 'problem' से पहले एक विशेषण (adjective) की आवश्यकता है, अतः 'severe' सही है।
[RULE] Adjective Usage

---

[Q969] Education plays a ___(5)___ role in shaping the future of young minds.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) pivotal
(b) pivot
(c) pivoting
(d) pivots
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase 'plays a pivotal role' is standard English usage denoting extreme importance.
उदा. 'plays a pivotal role' एक मानक वाक्यांश है जिसका अर्थ है अत्यंत महत्वपूर्ण भूमिका निभाना।
[RULE] Fixed Phrases / Vocabulary

---

[Q970] Technology has ___(6)___ transformed the way we communicate with each other.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) profoundly
(b) profound
(c) profundity
(d) profoundness
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'transformed' requires modification by an adverb indicating depth or scale, hence 'profoundly'.
उदा. क्रिया 'transformed' को संशोधित करने के लिए adverb 'profoundly' का उपयोग किया जाएगा।
[RULE] Adverb Modification

---

[Q971] Reading books not only enhances vocabulary ___(7)___ improves concentration.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) but also
(b) and
(c) or
(d) yet
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction pair 'not only' is always followed by 'but also'.
उदा. सहसंबद्ध समुच्चयबोधक (correlative conjunction) 'not only' के साथ हमेशा 'but_also' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conjunctions (Correlative)

---

[Q972] Regular physical exercise is ___(8)___ for maintaining good health and fitness.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) essential
(b) essentially
(c) essence
(d) essentials
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After the linking verb 'is', an adjective is required to describe the subject. 'Essential' means necessary.
उदा. लिंकिंग वर्ब 'is' के बाद subject की विशेषता बताने वाले adjective 'essential' की आवश्यकता है।
[RULE] Linking Verbs & Adjectives

---

[Q973] The manager asked the team to ___(9)___ all the errors in the financial report.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) rectify
(b) rectification
(c) rectifying
(d) rectified
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After the infinitive marker 'to', the base form of the verb (V1) must be used. 'Rectify' means to correct.
उदा. इनफिनिटिव 'to' के बाद क्रिया का पहला रूप (V1) आता है, इसलिए 'rectify' सही है।
[RULE] Infinitive (to + V1)

---

[Q974] Good governance relies heavily ___(10)___ transparency and public accountability.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) upon
(c) at
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrasal verb/verb 'rely' is standardly followed by the preposition 'on' or 'upon'. 'Relies on' fits best.
उदा. 'Rely' क्रिया के बाद आमतौर पर preposition 'on' का प्रयोग किया जाता है जिसका अर्थ है निर्भर होना।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q975] Agriculture is the backbone of the Indian economy. Despite rapid industrialization, it (1) ______ the principal source of livelihood for a majority of the population.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) remains
(b) remain
(c) remained
(d) retaining
[ANS] a
[SOL]
English: The passage is in the simple present tense describing a general truth/fact about agriculture. The subject 'it' is third-person singular, so 'remains' is correct.
उदा. यह वाक्य वर्तमान काल (Simple Present Tense) में है और एक सामान्य सत्य को दर्शाता है। एकवचन कर्ता 'it' के साथ 'remains' का प्रयोग होगा।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement in Simple Present Tense

---

[Q976] Books are our best friends. They guide us when we are in doubt and (2) ______ us when we are depressed.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) discourage
(b) comfort
(c) aggravate
(d) annoy
[ANS] b
[SOL]
English: The context shows a positive relationship between books and humans. When depressed, books 'comfort' us.
उदा. संदर्भ सकारात्मक है; जब हम उदास होते हैं तो पुस्तकें हमें 'सांत्वना' (comfort) देती हैं।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q977] Pollution has become a major global threat. Governments must take stringent measures to (3) ______ carbon emissions urgently.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) increase
(b) curb
(c) promote
(d) accelerate
[ANS] b
[SOL]
English: To combat pollution, governments need to control or reduce carbon emissions. 'Curb' means to restrain or control.
उदा. प्रदूषण से निपटने के लिए सरकारों को कार्बन उत्सर्जन को नियंत्रित या कम करना चाहिए। 'Curb' का अर्थ रोकना या नियंत्रित करना है।
[RULE] Vocabulary and Collocation

---

[Q978] Disaster management requires a proactive approach. Authorities should be well-prepared to (4) ______ any unforeseen natural calamities.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) tackle
(b) ignore
(c) create
(d) worsen
[ANS] a
[SOL]
English: 'Tackle' means to make determined efforts to deal with a problem or difficult task, fitting the context of natural calamities.
उदा. 'Tackle' का अर्थ किसी समस्या या कठिन परिस्थिति से निपटना होता है, जो प्राकृतिक आपदाओं के संदर्भ में बिल्कुल सही है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs and Context

---

[Q979] Proper time management is essential for success. Those who waste time often (5) ______ regret later in life.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) escape
(b) face
(c) avoid
(d) deny
[ANS] b
[SOL]
English: People who waste time ultimately end up experiencing or facing regret. 'Face' fits logically here.
उदा. जो लोग समय बर्बाद करते हैं, वे जीवन में बाद में पछतावे का सामना ('face') करते हैं।
[RULE] Logical Agreement in Context

---

[Q980] The internet has revolutionized communication. It has bridged geographical gaps, making the world (6) ______ smaller place.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) an
(b) the
(c) a
(d) no article
[ANS] c
[SOL]
English: 'Place' is a singular countable noun used here with a modifier phrase ('smaller place'), requiring the indefinite article 'a'.
उदा. 'Place' एक एकवचन संज्ञा है जिसके साथ विशेषण 'smaller' आया है, अतः यहाँ अनिश्चित आर्टिकल 'a' का प्रयोग होगा।
[RULE] Articles Usage

---

[Q981] Physical exercise is vital for maintaining good health. It not only keeps our body fit (7) ______ also refreshes our mind.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) and
(b) but
(c) or
(d) yet
[ANS] b
[SOL]
English: The correlative conjunction pair is 'not only ... but also'. Hence, 'but' is the correct choice.
उदा. सहसंबद्ध समुच्चयबोधक (Correlative Conjunction) 'not only' के साथ हमेशा 'but also' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conjunctions (Correlative Conjunctions)

[Q982] Honesty is the best policy. An honest person is trusted by every one and commands (8) ______ in society.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) disrespect
(b) ridicule
(c) respect
(d) criticism
[ANS] c
[SOL]
English: An honest person earns admiration and respect in society, fitting the positive tone of the proverb.
उदा. एक ईमानदार व्यक्ति समाज में प्रशंसा और सम्मान ('respect') अर्जित करता है।
[RULE] Contextual Tone Matching

[Q983] Wild life conservation is the need of the hour. Poaching and illegal logging must be punished (9) ______ to save endangered species.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) severely
(b) gently
(c) mildly
(d) casually
[ANS] a
[SOL]
English: To stop illegal activities like poaching, offenders must be punished strictly or 'severely'.
उदा. अवैध शिकार जैसे अपराधों को रोकने के लिए अपराधियों को सख्ती से यानी 'severely' दंडित किया जाना चाहिए।
[RULE] Adverb Usage

[Q984] Team work is crucial for achieving organizational goals. When members collaborate effectively, the overall productivity (10) ______ significantly.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) decreases
(b) drops
(c) rises
(d) falls
[ANS] c
[SOL]
English: Effective collaboration leads to an increase in overall productivity. Therefore, 'rises' is the correct contextual choice.
उदा. प्रभावी सहयोग से कुल उत्पादकता में वृद्धि होती है, इसलिए 'rises' (बढ़ती है) सही विकल्प है।
[RULE] Contextual Meaning and Vocabulary

[Q985] Communication is a vital part of human existence. We use language to (1) our thoughts, feelings, and ideas to others.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) convey
(b) confess
(c) conceal
(d) consume
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextually, we 'convey' (communicate/express) our thoughts and feelings to others. 
उदा. यहाँ विचारों और भावनाओं को दूसरों तक पहुँचाने (convey) की बात हो रही है, इसलिए सही विकल्प 'convey' है।
[RULE] Vocabulary in Context

[Q986] Books are our best friends. They guide us when we are lost and (2) us when we are low.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) provoke
(b) comfort
(c) disappoint
(d) disturb
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Books provide solace and 'comfort' when a person is feeling low or sad.

[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

[Q987] The government has taken several initiatives to (3) the agricultural sector in the country.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) weaken
(b) boost
(c) hinder
(d) diminish
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Governments take initiatives to 'boost' (improve/strengthen) a particular sector. 
उदा. सरकारें किसी क्षेत्र को मजबूत या प्रोत्साहित करने (boost) के लिए पहल करती हैं।
[RULE] Collocation and Phrasal Usage

[Q988] Pollution is a grave threat to humanity. It has (4) the balance of nature in unprecedented ways.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) restored
(b) maintained
(c) disturbed
(d) protected
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Pollution negatively impacts nature, hence it has 'disturbed' the natural balance. 
उदा. प्रदूषण प्रकृति पर बुरा प्रभाव डालता है, इसलिए इसने प्राकृतिक संतुलन को बिगाड़ (disturbed) दिया है।
[RULE] Logical Consistency

[Q989] Hard work is the key to success. Those who are ready to (5) extra hours always achieve their goals.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) put in
(b) put off
(c) put out
(d) put down
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Put in' means to invest time or effort. Thus, putting in extra hours is the correct collocation. 
उदा. 'Put in' का अर्थ समय या प्रयास लगाना होता है। अतिरिक्त घंटे लगाने के लिए 'put in' सही वाक्यांश है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

[Q990] Regular exercise helps in maintaining physical fitness and also plays a crucial role in (6) mental health.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) ruining
(b) enhancing
(c) neglecting
(d) worsening
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Exercise positively impacts health, so it 'enhances' (improves) mental health. 
उदा. व्यायाम मानसिक स्वास्थ्य को बेहतर बनाने (enhancing) में महत्वपूर्ण भूमिका निभाता है।
[RULE] Contextual Antonyms/Synonyms

[Q991] A wise leader listens to the grievances of the citizens and tries to find a (7) solution.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) viable
(b) fragile
(c) hostile
(d) futile
[ANS] a
[SOL]
A leader looks for a 'viable' (practicable and workable) solution to problems. 
उदा. एक समझदार नेता समस्याओं का व्यावहारिक और प्रभावी (viable) समाधान खोजने का प्रयास करता है।
[RULE] Advanced Vocabulary

[Q992] Technology has revolutionized education. Online learning has made education accessible to students (8) geographical boundaries.
