[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) across
(b) within
(c) beside
(d) along
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'across' is used to indicate from one side to another or transcending boundaries (e.g., across geographical boundaries). 
उदा. भौगोलिक सीमाओं को पार करते हुए शिक्षा उपलब्ध कराने के लिए preposition 'across' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Prepositions

[Q993] Honesty is a great virtue. An honest person never resorts to (9) means to achieve success.

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) noble
(b) fair
(c) unfair
(d) just
[ANS] c
[SOL]
An honest person will never use 'unfair' means. Context dictates a negative word here.

[RULE] Contextual Tone

[Q994] Wild life conservation is the need of the hour. Many species of animals are on the (10) of extinction.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) verge
(b) peak
(c) tip
(d) height
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The idiom/phrase 'on the verge of' means very near to a specific state or happening (like extinction). 
उदा. 'On the verge of' एक निश्चित स्थिति (जैसे विलुप्ति) के बिलकुल करीब होने को दर्शाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Phrases / Idioms

[Q995] The old bridge, which had been in use for more than a century, was finally (1) ________ to prevent any further damage. The local authorities decided that a modern replacement would (2) ________ the area’s traffic flow. (1) A) repaired B) repairing C) repair D) repaired (2) A) improve B) improves C) improving D) improved
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) repair
(b) repairing
(c) repaired
(d) Quaint
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Past simple tense is used for completed actions in the past.
उदा. वह पुल (repair) किया गया था।
[RULE] Past Simple Tense

---

[Q996] If we (1) ________ the data earlier, we (2) ________ the mistakes in the report. (1) A) had analyzed B) have analyzed C) analyzed D) had to analyze (2) A) would have corrected B) would correct C) correct D) have corrected
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had analyzed
(b) have analyzed
(c) analyzed
(d) had to analyze
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Past perfect is used for an action that happened before another past action.
उदा. यदि हमने डेटा (had analyzed) किया होता, तो हम त्रुटियों को सुधारते।
[RULE] Past Perfect Tense

---

[Q997] She (1) ________ to the conference when the keynote speaker (2) ________. (1) A) was arriving B) arrived C) has arrived D) arrives (2) A) started B) has started C) start D) started
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was arriving
(b) arrived
(c) has arrived
(d) arrives
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule: Past simple is used for a completed action at a specific time.
उदा. वह (arrived) जब मुख्य वक्ता (started) था।
[RULE] Past Simple Tense

---

[Q998] The committee, which (1) ________ the issue for months, finally (2) ________ a solution. (1) A) has discussed B) had discussed C) discussed D) discussing (2) A) present B) presents C) presented D) presenting
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) has discussed
(b) had discussed
(c) discussed
(d) discussing
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Rule: Simple past tense is used for actions completed in the past.
उदा. समिति (discussed) इस मुद्दे पर।
[RULE] Simple Past Tense

---

[Q999] The new policy will (1) ________ many changes in the industry, but it (2) ________ to be implemented gradually. (1) A) bring B) brings C) bringing D) brought (2) A) will be B) is C) has D) will
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) bring
(b) brings
(c) bringing
(d) brought
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Future simple (will + base verb) is used for predictions.
उदा. नई नीति (bring) बदलाव लाएगी।
[RULE] Future Simple Tense

---

[Q1000] He (1) ________ the project, but the dead line (2) ________. (1) A) missed B) misses C) missing D) missed (2) A) was arriving B) arrived C) has arrived D) arriving
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) missed
(b) misses
(c) missing
(d) Timid
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Simple past tense for completed action; past simple for another past action.
उदा. उसने प्रोजेक्ट (missed) लेकिन समयसीमा (arrived) थी।
[RULE] Past Simple Tense

---

[Q1001] The scientist (1) ________ the experiment, yet he (2) ________ the expected results. (1) A) conducted B) conducts C) conducting D) conducted (2) A) did not yield B) does not yield C) did not yielding D) does not yielded
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) conducted
(b) conducts
(c) conducting
(d) Diligent
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Simple past for completed action; simple past for another completed action.
उदा. वैज्ञानिक ने प्रयोग (conducted) किया, पर परिणाम (did not yield) नहीं मिले।
[RULE] Past Simple Tense

---

[Q1002] If the company (1) ________ the new guidelines, it (2) ________ more efficient operations. (1) A) had adopted B) has adopted C) adopted D) adopting (2) A) would produce B) will produce C) produced D) producing
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had adopted
(b) has adopted
(c) adopted
(d) adopting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Past perfect for an earlier action; conditional perfect for result.
उदा. यदि कंपनी (had adopted) दिशा-निर्देश, तो वह (would produce) कुशल संचालन करती।
[RULE] Past Perfect & Conditional Perfect

---

[Q1003] During the present at i on, the speaker (1) ________ a series of data points, which (2) ________ the audience. (1) A) showed B) shows C) showing D) shown (2) A) impressed B) impresses C) impressing D) impressed
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) showed
(b) shows
(c) showing
(d) shown
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Past simple for actions performed during an event; past simple for resulting effect.
उदा. वक्ता ने (showed) डेटा, जिससे श्रोता (impressed) हुए।
[RULE] Past Simple Tense

---

[Q1004] The committee (1) ________ a new framework last year, which (2) ________ in the upcoming regulation. (1) A) introduced B) introduces C) introduced D) introducing (2) A) will appear B) has appeared C) appeared D) appearing
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) introduced
(b) introduces
(c) introducing
(d) Pensive
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Past simple for completed action; future simple for future inclusion.
उदा. समिति (introduced) नया ढाँचा, जो (will appear) आगामी विनियम में।
[RULE] Past Simple & Future Simple Tense

[Q1005] Passage: Reading books is a wonderful habit. It (1) ______ our knowledge and broadens our perspective. When we read, we enter a new world.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) enhances
(b) reduces
(c) destroys
(d) ignores
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextual vocabulary: Reading books increases or improves knowledge, so 'enhances' is the correct fit.
उदा. किताबें पढ़ने से हमारा ज्ञान बढ़ता है, इसलिए यहाँ 'enhances' (बढ़ाना) सही विकल्प है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

[Q1006] Passage: Honesty is the best policy. An honest person is trusted by every one. He never resorts (1) ______ unfair means to achieve success.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) to
(c) on
(d) with
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Fixed preposition: The verb 'resort' is always followed by the preposition 'to'.
उदा. 'Resort' के बाद हमेशा प्रपोजिशन 'to' का प्रयोग होता है (Resort to).
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

[Q1007] Passage: Pollution has become a major threat to our environment. Forests are being cut down rapidly, (1) ______ leads to ecological imbalance.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) who
(b) whom
(c) which
(d) whose
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Relative pronoun: 'Which' refers to the whole preceding clause or a non-living thing as a subject.
उदा. संपूर्ण खंड या निर्जीव वस्तु के लिए रिलेटिव प्रोनाउन 'which' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Relative Pronouns

[Q1008] Passage: Time and tide wait for none. Every second is precious. We must learn to (1) ______ time wisely instead of wasting it.

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) spend
(b) utilize
(c) pass
(d) kill
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Collocation: We 'utilize' time wisely in formal contexts. 'Utilize' means to make practical and effective use of.
उदा. समय का सही उपयोग करने के लिए 'utilize' सबसे उपयुक्त शब्द है।
[RULE] Collocations / Vocabulary

[Q1009] Passage: Hard work is the key to success. Those who work hard (1) ______ achieve their goals in life.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) never
(b) seldom
(c) surely
(d) hardly
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Adverb of affirmation/degree: In context of hard work leading to success, people who work hard 'surely' achieve their goals.
उदा. कड़ी मेहनत करने वाले लोग निश्चित रूप से अपने लक्ष्यों को प्राप्त करते हैं, अतः 'surely' सही है।

[Q1010] Passage: Discipline is crucial for students. A disciplined student follows all the rules (1) ______ regulations of the school.

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) or
(b) and
(c) but
(d) so
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Conjunction pair: 'Rules and regulations' is a standard collocation/pair used together.
उदा. 'Rules and regulations' एक निश्चित युग्म (collocation) है, इसलिए यहाँ 'and' आएगा।

[Q1011] Passage: Physical exercise keeps us fit and healthy. It improves blood circulation and (1) ______ our immune system.

(a) weakens
(b) strengthens
(c) damages
(d) lowers
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Contextual meaning: Exercise makes the body stronger and improves immunity, hence 'strengthens'.
उदा. व्यायाम शरीर को मजबूत करता है और रोग प्रतिरोधक क्षमता बढ़ाता है, इसलिए 'strengthens' सही है।

[Q1012] Passage: The internet has revolutionized communication. People can now connect with anyone around the globe (1) ______ a click of a button.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) in
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Fixed preposition: The standard idiom is 'at the click of a button'.
उदा. निश्चित मुहावरेदार प्रयोग 'at the click of a button' होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition / Idiomatic Usage

[Q1013] Passage: Mahatma Gandhi believed in non-violence. He led India to independence (1) ______ peaceful means.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) by
(b) through
(c) with
(d) via
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Preposition of medium/instrumentality: 'Through peaceful means' means by the method of peace.
उदा. शांतिपूर्ण साधनों के माध्यम से के अर्थ में 'through' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Prepositions

---

[Q1014] Passage: Trees are our best friends. They provide us with oxygen, shade, and fruits. We should plant (1) ______ trees to save our planet.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) few
(b) little
(c) more
(d) less
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Determiners: To save the planet, we need a greater quantity of trees, hence the comparative adjective 'more' is required.
उदा. ग्रह को बचाने के लिए हमें और अधिक पेड़ लगाने की आवश्यकता है, इसलिए 'more' सही विकल्प है।
[RULE] Determiners / Adjectives

[Q1015] Passage: Education is not merely acquiring knowledge, but it is the development of the character and mind. True education helps an individual to (1) ______ critical thin king and problem-solving skills.

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) develop
(b) destroy
(c) hinder
(d) ignore
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextually, education helps a person foster or 'develop' positive skills like critical thinking.
उदा. संदर्भ के अनुसार, शिक्षा व्यक्ति में आलोचनात्मक सोच और समस्या-समाधान कौशल को 'विकसित' (develop) करने में मदद करती है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

[Q1016] Passage: Forests play a crucial role in maintaining the ecological balance of the earth. They provide habitats for countless species and (2) ______ a significant amount of carbon dioxide.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) release
(b) absorb
(c) emit
(d) consume
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Scientifically, forests 'absorb' carbon dioxide during photosynthesis, helping to reduce greenhouse gases.
उदा. वैज्ञानिक रूप से, वन प्रकाश संश्लेषण के दौरान कार्बन डाइऑक्साइड को 'अवशोषित' (absorb) करते हैं।

[Q1017] Passage: The invention of the internet has revolutionized communication. People can now connect with anyone across the globe (3) ______ a fraction of a second.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The standard prepositional idiom is 'in a fraction of a second' meaning very quickly.
उदा. सही पूर्वसर्ग मुहावरा 'in a fraction of a second' है जिसका अर्थ बहुत कम समय में होता है।

[Q1018] Passage: Time management is an essential skill for success. Those who waste time often (4) ______ behind in their professional and personal lives.

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) stay
(b) fall
(c) walk
(d) run
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The phrasal verb 'fall behind' means to fail to keep up with a set pace or standard.
उदा. वाक्यांश क्रिया 'fall behind' का अर्थ है पीछे छूट जाना।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q1019] Passage: Reading books broadens our horizons and improves our vocabulary. It takes us to imaginary worlds and introduces us (5) ______ diverse cultures and perspectives.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) with
(b) to
(c) in
(d) for
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb 'introduce' is followed by the preposition 'to' when introducing someone or something to another.
उदा. 'Introduce' क्रिया के बाद हमेशा 'to' पूर्वसर्ग का प्रयोग होता है (introduce someone to something)।
[RULE] Prepositional Usage

---

[Q1020] Passage: Regular exercise is vital for maintaining physical health. It strengthens our muscles, boosts immunity, and keeps various chronic diseases (6) ______.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) away
(b) near
(c) close
(d) together
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The idiom 'keep away' means to prevent something from coming near or affecting someone.
उदा. मुहावरा 'keep away' का अर्थ बीमारियों को दूर रखना होता है।
[RULE] Idiomatic Expressions

---

[Q1021] Passage: Renewable sources of energy are becoming increasingly popular. Governments are promoting solar and wind power to (7) ______ dependence on fossil fuels.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) increase
(b) reduce
(c) promote
(d) maintain
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Contextually, shifting to renewable energy aims to 'reduce' dependence on fossil fuels.
उदा. संदर्भ के अनुसार, नवीकरणीय ऊर्जा का उद्देश्य जीवाश्म ईंधन पर निर्भरता को 'कम करना' (reduce) है।
[RULE] Contextual Logic

---

[Q1022] Passage: Honesty is the best policy. An honest person is trusted by every one and earns immense respect (8) ______ society.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) on
(b) at
(c) in
(d) upon
[ANS] c
[SOL]
We use the preposition 'in' when referring to a community, group, or society as a whole.
उदा. समाज या समुदाय के भीतर संदर्भ देने के लिए पूर्वसर्ग 'in' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Prepositions of Place/Group

---

[Q1023] Passage: Water scarcity is a growing global concern. It is imperative that we adopt water-saving techniques and stop the (9) ______ of this precious resource.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) conservation
(b) wastage
(c) storage
(d) usage
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Given the problem of water scarcity, we need to stop the 'wastage' of water.
उदा. पानी की कमी को देखते हुए, हमें पानी की 'बरबादी' (wastage) को रोकना चाहिए।
[RULE] Antonym/Context Match

---

[Q1024] Passage: Perseverance is the key to overcoming obstacles. Those who never give up (10) ______ eventually achieve their goals.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) will
(b) can
(c) shall
(d) may
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Can' denotes capability or possibility as a general truth in this motivational context.
उदा. इस प्रेरक संदर्भ में 'can' का प्रयोग क्षमता या संभावना को दर्शाने के लिए किया गया है।
[RULE] Modals Usage

---

[Q1025] The scientist decided to conduct the experiment after the committee had approved the proposal. Consequently, the [1] of the project was scheduled for next week.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) implementation
(b) implementation of
(c) implemented
(d) implementing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The sentence requires a noun to describe the action. "Implementation" is the noun form of the verb *implement*.  
उदा. The implementation of the new policy was delayed.
[RULE] Noun usage / Gerund

---

[Q1026] If you want to improve your English, you should read books and [1] daily.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) study
(b) studying
(c) studied
(d) studies
[ANS] b
[SOL]
After an infinitive phrase, we use the gerund form to show an action. Here, "studying" is the correct gerund.  
उदा. He enjoys studying history.
[RULE] Gerund after infinitive phrase

---

[Q1027] The manager was aware that the report was due, but the team had not yet [1] it.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) submittal
(b) submitted
(c) submission
(d) submitting
[ANS] d
[SOL]
The verb should be in the present participle form to match the continuous tense. "Submitting" is correct.  
उदा. She is submitting the documents now.
[RULE] Present participle after 'had not yet'

---

[Q1028] She only [1] the letter after the mailman delivered it.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) opened
(b) opening
(c) open
(d) opens
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The simple past tense is required after 'only' in this context. "Opened" is the correct past form.  
उदा. He opened the door when I arrived.
[RULE] Simple past tense after 'only'

---

[Q1029] Although the weather was cold, the hikers continued their trek, showing great [1].
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) determination
(b) determining
(c) determined
(d) determines
[ANS] a
[SOL]
A noun is needed to describe the quality of the hikers. "Determination" is the noun form.  
उदा. Her determination helped her finish the race.
[RULE] Noun usage for quality

---

[Q1030] The committee will review the proposal and then [1] a decision by the end of the month.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) make
(b) making
(c) made
(d) to make
[ANS] b
[SOL]
After a noun phrase, the verb should be in the gerund form for a future action. "Making" is correct.  
उदा. They are making a plan.
[RULE] Gerund after noun phrase

---

[Q1031] The students were asked to write an essay on the topic, and they were [1] to submit it within 48 hours.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) required
(b) requiring
(c) require
(d) requires
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The past participle "required" fits the passive construction.  
उदा. They were required to finish the assignment.
[RULE] Past participle in passive voice

---

[Q1032] If the project is completed on time, the company will [1] the new product line next quarter.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) launch
(b) launching
(c) launched
(d) to launch
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The base form of the verb follows the modal 'will'. "Launch" is correct.  
उदा. He will launch the campaign tomorrow.
[RULE] Base verb after modal 'will'

---

[Q1033] The direct or, known for his strict policies, has always [1] the staff to meet deadlines.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) demand
(b) demands
(c) demanding
(d) demanded
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The present participle "demanding" is used after a verb to describe ongoing actions.  
उदा. She is demanding more resources.
[RULE] Present participle after verb

---

[Q1034] The artist's latest exhibition, which [1] the city, received rave reviews.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) draws
(b) drawn
(c) drawing
(d) to draw
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The relative clause requires a present tense verb to describe an ongoing action. "Draws" is correct.  
उदा. She draws inspiration from nature.
[RULE] Present tense in relative clause

[Q1035] Passage: Archaeology is the study of human history through the material remains of the past. It involves excavating sites to (1) artifacts, architecture, and biofacts.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) recover
(b) destroy
(c) neglect
(d) hide
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextual vocabulary: 'Recover' means to find or retrieve something lost or buried. In archaeology, excavating sites is done to recover artifacts.
उदा. संदर्भ के अनुसार 'recover' (प्राप्त करना या निकालना) सबसे उपयुक्त है क्योंकि पुरातत्वविद् प्राचीन अवशेषों को निकालते हैं।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q1036] Passage: These objects are then analyzed to (2) how ancient people lived, worked, and interacted with their environment.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) ignore
(b) understand
(c) forget
(d) complicate
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Contextual usage: Objects are analyzed in order to 'understand' past human behavior and lifestyle.
उदा. वस्तुओं का विश्लेषण इसलिए किया जाता है ताकि प्राचीन लोगों के जीवन को 'समझ' (understand) सकें।
[RULE] Logical Inference

---

[Q1037] Passage: However, archaeology is not just about treasure hunting; it requires meticulous (3) and recording of every single item found.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) carelessness
(b) destruction
(c) documentation
(d) abandonment
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Collocation: Archaeological excavation requires careful documentation and recording.
उदा. पुरातात्विक उत्खनन के लिए प्रत्येक वस्तु की सावधानीपूर्वक 'दस्तावेजीकरण' (documentation) की आवश्यकता होती है।
[RULE] Noun Collocation

[Q1038] Passage: Without proper context, an artifact loses much of its historical (4).
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) value
(b) weight
(c) color
(d) price
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Collocation: Historical 'value' refers to the importance of an artifact in history.
उदा. ऐतिहासिक 'value' (महत्व/मूल्य) का उपयोग यहाँ सही बैठेगा क्योंकि संदर्भ महत्व से जुड़ा है।
[RULE] Fixed Phrase / Collocation

[Q1039] Passage: Therefore, modern archaeologists use advanced technology to (5) the past accurately.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) obscure
(b) reconstruct
(c) fabricate
(d) damage
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Contextual meaning: Archaeologists reconstruct the past using artifacts and data.
उदा. वैज्ञानिक साक्ष्यों के आधार पर अतीत का 'पुनर्निर्माण' (reconstruct) किया जाता है।
[RULE] Verb Usage in Context

[Q1040] Passage: The internet has revolutionized communication. It has (1) the world into a global village.

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) divided
(b) transformed
(c) reduced
(d) broken
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Collocation: 'Transformed' means changed completely for the better. The internet transformed the world.
उदा. 'Transformed' (बदल दिया) सही है क्योंकि इंटरनेट ने दुनिया को एक वैश्विक गांव में बदल दिया है।

[Q1041] Passage: In form at i on is now available at the (2) of a button.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) push
(b) touch
(c) turn
(d) strike
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Fixed idiom/phrase: 'At the touch of a button' means very quickly and easily.

[Q1042] Passage: Despite its numerous benefits, excessive use of the internet can lead to social (3).

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) connection

(d) gathering
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Contextual antonym/contrast: While the internet connects people, excessive use can cause social 'isolation' (separating from society).

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) blessing
(b) concern
(c) solution
(d) comfort
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Context: Cyber threats cause worry or 'concern' for everyone.

[Q1044] Passage: Therefore, digital literacy is essential to navigate the online world (5).
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) safely
(b) dangerously
(c) carelessly
(d) recklessly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Adverb usage: Digital literacy helps people navigate online securely and 'safely'.

[Q1045] Read the passage below and fill in the blanks. Pollution has become a major concern in modern cities. The rapid growth of industries and vehicles has (1) ______ the air we breathe. Trees, which act as natural purifiers, are being cut down indiscriminately.' Options for blank (1)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) purified
(b) polluted
(c) enhanced
(d) protected
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Contextual vocabulary: The sentence talks about the negative impact of industries and vehicles on air, making 'polluted' the logical choice.
उदा. संदर्भ के अनुसार, उद्योग और वाहन हवा को दूषित कर रहे हैं, इसलिए सही उत्तर 'polluted' है।

[Q1046] Continual noise from traffic and construction work has a severe (2) ______ on human health. People living in urban areas often suffer from stress, sleep disorders, and hearing loss.' Options for blank (2)

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) affect
(b) effect
(c) affection
(d) effort
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Effect' is a noun used after the adjective 'severe'. 'Affect' is a verb.
उदा. विशेषण 'severe' के बाद संज्ञा (noun) 'effect' का प्रयोग होगा।

[Q1047] Water scarcity is another alarming issue. Many rivers that were once perennial have now dried (3) ______ due to excessive extraction and erratic rain fall patterns.' Options for blank (3)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) out
(b) up
(c) down
(d) off
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Phrasal verb 'dry up' means to completely lose its water supply or dry completely.
उदा. वाक्यांश 'dry up' का अर्थ पूरी तरह सूख जाना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

[Q1048] Governments and individuals must join hands to mitigate these environmental challenges. Planting more trees and adopting renewable energy sources can (4) ______ a huge difference.' Options for blank (4)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) do
(b) make
(c) take
(d) give
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Collocation: The correct fixed collocation with 'a huge difference' is the verb 'make'.
उदा. 'make a difference' एक निश्चित सह-स्थापन (collocation) है।
[RULE] Collocations

[Q1049] Awareness programs should be conducted in schools and colleges to (5) ______ the youth about the importance of ecological balance.' Options for blank (5)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) educate
(b) ignore
(c) discourage
(d) confuse
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextual meaning: Awareness programs aim to teach or 'educate' youth about ecological balance.
उदा. जागरूकता कार्यक्रमों का उद्देश्य युवाओं को पारिस्थितिक संतुलन के बारे में शिक्षित करना है।

[Q1050] Books are our best companions. They open doors to new worlds and (6) ______ our imagination without leaving the comfort of our rooms.' Options for blank (6)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) restrict
(b) stimulate
(c) dull
(d) weaken
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Books expand knowledge and 'stimulate' or inspire imagination positively.
उदा. किताबें हमारी कल्पना को 'stimulate' (प्रेरित/उत्तेजित) करती हैं।

---

[Q1051] Unlike electronic gadgets that strain our eyes, reading a physical book provides a (7) ______ reading experience that calms the mind.' Options for blank (7)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) disturbing
(b) peaceful
(c) hectic
(d) stressful
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Contrast indicator 'Unlike electronic gadgets that strain...' suggests a positive, calm experience.

[RULE] Contextual Contrast

---

[Q1052] Reading habits must be inculcated in children from an early age. It helps in vocabulary development and (8) ______ comprehension skills.' Options for blank (8)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) improves
(b) worsens
(c) destroys
(d) hinders
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Parallel structure: 'helps in vocabulary development and improves comprehension skills.'
उदा. समानांतर संरचना के अनुसार 'improves' सही सकारात्मक क्रिया है।

[Q1053] Space exploration has always fascinated man kind Scientists have been trying to uncover the mysteries of the universe (9) ______ decades.' Options for blank (9)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) from
(d) during
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Decades' is a period of time (duration), hence preposition 'for' is used with present perfect continuous tense.
उदा. समयावधि (duration) 'decades' के साथ 'for' का प्रयोग होता है।

[Q1054] Recent missions to Mars have provided valuable data, bringing us closer to the possibility of human habitation on (10) ______ planets.' Options for blank (10)

[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) other
(b) another
(c) others
(d) every
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Planets' is a plural noun, so the determiner must be plural-modifying 'other'. 'Another' is used before singular countable nouns.
उदा. बहुवचन संज्ञा 'planets' के साथ 'other' का प्रयोग होगा।
[RULE] Determiners (Other vs Another)

[Q1055] The new museum, which was opened last month, has already attracted a ______ number of visitors.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) large
(b) larger
(c) largest
(d) larger than
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use the adjective ‘large’ to describe a big number.
उदा. Hindi: नई संग्रहालय ने पहले ही एक बड़ी संख्या में आगंतुकों को आकर्षित किया है।
[RULE] Adjective usage

---

[Q1056] By the time the train arrived, the tourists had ______ their tickets.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) purchased
(b) purchase
(c) buy
(d) buying
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Past perfect tense is formed with ‘had’ + past participle.
उदा. Hindi: जब ट्रेन पहुँची, तो पर्यटकों ने अपनी टिकटें खरीदी हुई थीं।
[RULE] Past Perfect Tense

---

[Q1057] She has been living in Paris ______ 2010.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) during
(d) from
[ANS] a

Rule in English: Use ‘since’ to indicate the starting point of an action that continues to the present.

[RULE] Since vs for

[Q1058] If it ______ tomorrow, we will cancel the picnic.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) rains
(b) rain

(d) rained
[ANS] a

---

[Q1059] I will give you a call ______ I arrive.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) when
(b) after

(d) in
[ANS] a

[Q1060] He ______ the book on the table.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) put
(b) puts
(c) putting
(d) placed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: ‘put’ is the past tense of the irregular verb ‘put’.
उदा. Hindi: उसने किताब मेज पर रखी।
[RULE] Irregular Verb – Put

---

[Q1061] She is the only person ______ can solve this problem.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) that
(b) who
(c) whom
(d) whichever
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use ‘who’ as the relative pronoun referring to a person.

[Q1062] We need to finish the project ______ the dead line
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) before
(b) after
(c) during
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use ‘before’ to indicate something must be done prior to the deadline.
उदा. Hindi: हमें समय सीमा से पहले परियोजना पूरी करनी है।
[RULE] Preposition – Before

---

[Q1063] The committee will decide ______ the new policy next week.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use ‘on’ when referring to a decision or discussion.
उदा. Hindi: समिति अगले हफ़्ते नई नीति पर निर्णय लेगी।
[RULE] Preposition – On

---

[Q1064] There is a chance that the flight ______ delayed.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) will get
(b) would get
(c) might get
(d) has got
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use ‘might’ for a possibility in the future.
उदा. Hindi: उड़ान के विलंब होने की संभावना है।
[RULE] Modal Verb – Might

---

[Q1065] The ancient city of ___ was known for its magnificent temples and bustling markets.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Ayodhya
(b) Varanasi
(c) Hampi
(d) Ujjain
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The phrase *known for* is followed by a noun phrase that indicates the reason for being famous.
उदा. *She is known for her kindness.*
[RULE] Preposition 'for' after 'known'

---

[Q1066] During the monsoon season, the river ___ to its fullest capacity, causing occasional floods in the nearby villages.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) floods
(b) overflows
(c) constricts
(d) dilates
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When a river exceeds its banks, we say it *overflows*.
उदा. *The highway overflows during rush hour.*
[RULE] Verb choice for natural events

---

[Q1067] The committee will convene tomorrow to discuss the new policy; however, the final decision ___ to the Minister of Finance.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) belong
(b) rests
(c) depends
(d) effective
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb *to rest* (in this context) is used to indicate that something is located or assigned to someone.
उदा. *The responsibility rests on the team leader.*
[RULE] Verb 'rest' meaning 'to be located/assigned'

---

[Q1068] To increase productivity, the company introduced a new software that ___ real time data analysis.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) enable
(b) enables
(c) enabling
(d) enabled
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The subject *software* is singular, so the verb must be in singular form: *enables*.
उदा. *The tool enables quick calculations.*
[RULE] Subject-verb agreement (singular noun + singular verb)

---

[Q1069] She has a talent for ___ music, and her performances are always well-received.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) compose
(b) composing
(c) composed
(d) composition
[ANS] b
[SOL]
After *talent for*, we use a gerund: *composing*.
उदा. *He has a talent for drawing.*
[RULE] Gerund after preposition 'for'

---

[Q1070] The scientist proposed a new theory; never the less, the evidence ___ not sufficient to prove it.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) is
(b) was
(c) were
(d) be
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The subject *evidence* is singular, so the verb must be *is*.
उदा. *The data is convincing.*
[RULE] Subject-verb agreement (singular noun + singular verb)

---

[Q1071] He was ___ to the committee's decision and expressed his disappointment openly.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) conformable
(b) conform
(c) conforming
(d) conformity
[ANS] c
[SOL]
After *was*, we need a participle or adjective; *conforming* is the correct participle.
उदा. *She was conforming to the rules.*
[RULE] Past tense + participle

---

[Q1072] The museum’s new exhibit showcases artifacts that ___ the region’s cultural heritage.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) represent
(b) representing
(c) represented
(d) Affluent
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The clause *that represent* is a relative clause describing the artifacts, so the verb is present tense.
उदा. *The book that explains history.*
[RULE] Relative clause with present tense verb

---

[Q1073] The engineer suggested that the bridge be inspected ___ the next month.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] d
[SOL]
With *by*, we indicate the deadline.
उदा. *Finish the report by Friday.*
[RULE] Preposition indicating deadline

---

[Q1074] Despite the heavy rain, the marathon ___ and attracted participants from all over the country.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) continued
(b) continuing
(c) continue
(d) contend
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The simple past form *continued* correctly describes the event that happened.
उदा. *The festival continued despite the cold.*
[RULE] Simple past tense for completed action

---

[Q1075] The new regulations are expected to ___ the growth of renewable energy projects.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) promote
(b) promoting
(c) promoted
(d) promotion
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After *expected to*, we use the base form of the verb: *promote*.
उदा. *We expect to see changes.*
[RULE] Modal verb + base form

---

[Q1076] She has been working here for five years and is now [1] a senior accountant.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) appointed
(b) appoint
(c) appointing
(d) appointed to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The past participle "appointed" is used as an adjective to describe the subject.
उदाहरण: उसने पाँच साल से यहाँ काम किया है और अब वह एक वरिष्ठ लेखाकार के रूप में नियुक्त है।
[RULE] Past participle used as adjective

---

[Q1077] The book, [1] in the middle of the table, was accidentally knocked over.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) which
(b) that
(c) who
(d) whom
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The relative pronoun "which" is used for non‑restrictive clauses referring to objects.
उदाहरण: वह पुस्तक, जो टेबल के बीच में थी, अचानक गिर गई।
[RULE] Relative pronoun for non‑restrictive clause

---

[Q1078] By the time the train arrived, the passengers had [1] their luggage.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) packed
(b) packing
(c) packed up
(d) packet
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The past perfect tense "had packed" shows an action completed before another past event.
उदाहरण: ट्रेन आने से पहले यात्रियों ने अपना सामान पैक कर लिया था।
[RULE] Past perfect tense

---

[Q1079] The committee will decide whether the proposal [1] approved.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) is
(b) was
(c) has
(d) will
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Passive voice in the future simple: "will be approved"; the missing word is the auxiliary "is".
उदाहरण: समिति यह तय करेगी कि प्रस्ताव स्वीकृत है।
[RULE] Passive voice in future simple

---

[Q1080] It is necessary that he [1] arrive on time.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) shall
(b) should
(c) must
(d) would
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Subjunctive mood in formal contexts: "It is necessary that he shall arrive".
उदाहरण: यह आवश्यक है कि वह समय पर पहुँचे।
[RULE] Subjunctive mood

---

[Q1081] I would rather stay at home than [1] outside.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) going
(b) to go
(c) go
(d) gone
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Infinitive "to go" follows "would rather" in negative preference.
उदाहरण: मैं घर पर रहना पसंद करूँगा बजाय बाहर जाने के।
[RULE] Infinitive after "would rather"

---

[Q1082] The painting, which was [1] in 1920, is now in the museum.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) created
(b) create
(c) creates
(d) creating
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Created" is the past participle used as adjective to indicate the time of creation.
उदाहरण: यह पेंटिंग 1920 में बनाई गई थी और अब संग्रहालय में है।
[RULE] Past participle as adjective

---

[Q1083] The students were asked to submit their reports by [1].
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Friday
(b) the Friday
(c) Fridays
(d) a Friday
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Days of the week are used without articles: "by Friday".

[RULE] Singular noun for day of week

---

[Q1084] The doctor advised him to take rest, [1] he should not exercise.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) otherwise
(b) otherwise if
(c) otherwise that
(d) otherwise to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Otherwise" introduces a consequence: otherwise he should not exercise.
उदाहरण: डॉक्टर ने उसकी सलाह दी कि वह आराम करे, अन्यथा उसे व्यायाम नहीं करना चाहिए।
[RULE] Conjunction "otherwise"

---

[Q1085] If it rains [1], we will cancel the picnic.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) then
(b) there
(c) so
(d) therefore
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Then" is used after a conditional clause to indicate result.
उदाहरण: यदि बारिश होती है तो हम पिकनिक रद्द कर देंगे।
[RULE] Conditional clause with "then"

[Q1086] Read the passage and fill in blank (1): Education is not merely acquiring knowledge, but it is the (1) of character and personality.

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) formation
(b) destruction
(c) neglect
(d) waste
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextually, education helps in building or forming character, hence 'formation' is the correct fit.

[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

[Q1087] Read the passage and fill in blank (2): A true student must cultivate the habit of discipline and should (2) his teachers with utmost respect.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) disobey
(b) treat
(c) ignore
(d) scorn
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb 'treat' fits well with 'respect' in this context (treat with utmost respect).

[Q1088] Read the passage and fill in blank (3): Books are our best companions. They enrich our minds and (3) us with wisdom.

[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) provide
(b) endow
(c) deprive
(d) rob
[ANS] b

'Endow with' is the correct collocation meaning to provide with a quality, asset, or ability.

[Q1089] Read the passage and fill in blank (4): Regular reading broadens our outlook and (4) our horizons.

(a) narrows
(b) contracts
(c) expands
(d) restricts
[ANS] c

The phrase 'expand our horizons' is a common idiom meaning to broaden one's experience or range of ideas.

[Q1090] Read the passage and fill in blank (5): Sports play a crucial role in maintaining physical fitness. They teach us the value of team work and fair (5).

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) play
(b) fight
(c) loss
(d) game
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Fair play' is a standard collocation referring to respectful and honest treatment or competition.

[RULE] Fixed Phrase / Collocation

[Q1091] Read the passage and fill in blank (6): Environmental pollution has become a grave threat to humanity. We must take immediate steps to (6) nature.

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) destroy
(b) conserve
(c) pollute
(d) spoil
[ANS] b
[SOL]
To counteract pollution, steps must be taken to protect or 'conserve' nature.

[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

[Q1092] Read the passage and fill in blank (7): Trees are vital for our survival as they absorb carbon dioxide and (7) oxygen into the atmosphere.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) absorb
(b) consume
(c) release
(d) block
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Trees take in carbon dioxide and give out or 'release' oxygen.

[RULE] Scientific Context / Verbs

[Q1093] Read the passage and fill in blank (8): Honesty is the best policy. An honest person is trusted and respected by all, whereas a liar is looked (8) upon by society.

[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) down
(b) up
(c) forward
(d) after
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrasal verb 'look down upon' means to consider someone inferior or treat with contempt.
उदा. ईमानदारी सबसे अच्छी नीति है। एक ईमानदार व्यक्ति पर सभी द्वारा भरोसा और सम्मान किया जाता है, जबकि एक झूठे को समाज द्वारा नीच दृष्टि से देखा (look down upon) जाता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

[Q1094] Read the passage and fill in blank (9): Technology has made our lives easier. However, excessive use of gadgets can have a negative (9) on health.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) affect
(b) impact
(c) benefit
(d) advantage
[ANS] b

'Impact' is a noun that follows the adjective 'negative' in this structure (have a negative impact).

[Q1095] Read the passage and fill in blank (10): Time and tide wait for none. We must utilize every moment wisely and never (10) time on trivial matters.

(a) save

(d) utilize
[ANS] c

The passage warns against wasting time on trivial matters, making 'waste' the accurate antonym for wise utilization.

[Q1096] Passage: Education is not merely acquiring knowledge, but it is a process that (1) ______ the overall personality of an individual. Options

(a) destroys

[SOL]
Contextual meaning: Hard work leads to success.
उदा. कड़ी मेहनत और दृढ़ता किसी भी क्षेत्र में सफलता (success) की कुंजी हैं।
[RULE] Contextual Association

[Q1106] Passage: Education is essential for the (1) ______ development of an individual. It helps in shaping personality and (2) ______ career opportunities. People who are well-educated can (3) ______ better decisions in life. Literacy rates have (4) ______ over the years due to government initiatives. However, a lot still needs to be (5) ______ to achieve 100% literacy. Options for Blank 1
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) overall
(b) surface
(c) partial
(d) minimal
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Overall' means complete or total development, which fits the context of education. 
उदा. 'Overall' का अर्थ संपूर्ण विकास है, जो शिक्षा के संदर्भ में बिल्कुल सही बैठता है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

[Q1107] Passage: Education is essential for the (1) ______ development of an individual. It helps in shaping personality and (2) ______ career opportunities. People who are well-educated can (3) ______ better decisions in life. Literacy rates have (4) ______ over the years due to government initiatives. However, a lot still needs to be (5) ______ to achieve 100% literacy. Options for Blank 2
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) narrow
(b) limited
(c) better
(d) scarcely
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Education creates 'better' career opportunities for individuals. 
उदा. शिक्षा व्यक्तियों के लिए 'बेहतर' (better) करियर के अवसर पैदा करती है।
[RULE] Adjective Context

[Q1108] Passage: Education is essential for the (1) ______ development of an individual. It helps in shaping personality and (2) ______ career opportunities. People who are well-educated can (3) ______ better decisions in life. Literacy rates have (4) ______ over the years due to government initiatives. However, a lot still needs to be (5) ______ to achieve 100% literacy. Options for Blank 3
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) take
(b) make
(c) do
(d) give
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Collocation: We always 'make decisions', not take decisions.
उदा. सही collocation 'make decisions' होता है, 'take decisions' नहीं।
[RULE] Fixed Collocation

[Q1109] Passage: Education is essential for the (1) ______ development of an individual. It helps in shaping personality and (2) ______ career opportunities. People who are well-educated can (3) ______ better decisions in life. Literacy rates have (4) ______ over the years due to government initiatives. However, a lot still needs to be (5) ______ to achieve 100% literacy. Options for Blank 4
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) declined
(b) decreased
(c) risen
(d) fallen
[ANS] c

Due to government initiatives, literacy rates must have 'risen' (increased).
उदा. सरकारी पहलों के कारण साक्षरता दर 'बढ़ी' (risen) होगी।
[RULE] Logical Contextual Flow

[Q1110] Passage: Education is essential for the (1) ______ development of an individual. It helps in shaping personality and (2) ______ career opportunities. People who are well-educated can (3) ______ better decisions in life. Literacy rates have (4) ______ over the years due to government initiatives. However, a lot still needs to be (5) ______ to achieve 100% literacy. Options for Blank 5
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) done
(b) made
(c) ignored
(d) lost
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Passive voice construction 'needs to be done' fits the meaning of work remaining.
उदा. 'needs to be done' (किया जाना बाकी है) इस संदर्भ में सटीक है।
[RULE] Passive Voice Structure

[Q1111] Passage: Pollution has become a major (1) ______ for the entire world. Industrial waste and vehicle smoke are (2) ______ to the environment. Governments are trying to (3) ______ strict laws to curb this menace. Public awareness campaigns also play a (4) ______ role in protecting nature. We must all contribute to saving our (5) ______ planet. Options for Blank 1
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) blessing
(b) solution
(c) concern
(d) benefit
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Pollution is a major 'concern' (worry) for the world.
उदा. प्रदूषण पूरी दुनिया के लिए एक बड़ी 'चिंता' (concern) बन गया है।
[RULE] Vocabulary & Context

[Q1112] Passage: Pollution has become a major (1) ______ for the entire world. Industrial waste and vehicle smoke are (2) ______ to the environment. Governments are trying to (3) ______ strict laws to curb this menace. Public awareness campaigns also play a (4) ______ role in protecting nature. We must all contribute to saving our (5) ______ planet. Options for Blank 2
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) beneficial
(b) harmful
(c) helpful
(d) friendly
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Industrial waste and smoke are 'harmful' to the environment.
उदा. औद्योगिक कचरा और वाहन का धुआं पर्यावरण के लिए 'हानिकारक' (harmful) होता है।
[RULE] Antonyms & Context

[Q1113] Passage: Pollution has become a major (1) ______ for the entire world. Industrial waste and vehicle smoke are (2) ______ to the environment. Governments are trying to (3) ______ strict laws to curb this menace. Public awareness campaigns also play a (4) ______ role in protecting nature. We must all contribute to saving our (5) ______ planet. Options for Blank 3
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) break
(b) violate
(c) enact
(d) ignore
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Governments 'enact' (pass/implement) strict laws.
उदा. सरकारें कड़े कानून 'बनाती या लागू करती' (enact) हैं।
[RULE] Verb Collocation

---

[Q1114] Passage: Pollution has become a major (1) ______ for the entire world. Industrial waste and vehicle smoke are (2) ______ to the environment. Governments are trying to (3) ______ strict laws to curb this menace. Public awareness campaigns also play a (4) ______ role in protecting nature. We must all contribute to saving our (5) ______ planet. Options for Blank 4
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) vital
(b) trivial
(c) minor
(d) insignificant
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Campaigns play a 'vital' (important) role.
उदा. जागरूकता अभियान प्रकृति की रक्षा में एक 'महत्वपूर्ण' (vital) भूमिका निभाते हैं।
[RULE] Fixed Phrase (Vital role)

---

[Q1115] Passage: Pollution has become a major (1) ______ for the entire world. Industrial waste and vehicle smoke are (2) ______ to the environment. Governments are trying to (3) ______ strict laws to curb this menace. Public awareness campaigns also play a (4) ______ role in protecting nature. We must all contribute to saving our (5) ______ planet. Options for Blank 5
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) dying
(b) fragile
(c) hostile
(d) ruined
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Earth is referred to as our 'fragile' planet needing protection.
उदा. पृथ्वी को हमारे 'नाजुक' (fragile) ग्रह के रूप में संदर्भित किया जाता है जिसे सुरक्षा की आवश्यकता है।
[RULE] Adjective Usage

---

[Q1116] Read the passage and fill in blank (1): Education is not merely about acquiring knowledge, but it is about building character and shaping the (1) ______ of an individual.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) personality
(b) building
(c) structure
(d) skeleton
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextually, education shapes the 'personality' of an individual. 
उदा. संदर्भ के अनुसार, शिक्षा किसी व्यक्ति के 'personality' (व्यक्तित्व) को आकार देती है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q1117] Read the passage and fill in blank (2): Regular exercise is vital for maintaining physical health. It helps to keep our body active and (2) ______.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) lethargic
(b) fit
(c) dull
(d) heavy
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Fit' is the positive antonym to lethargic and dull, matching the benefits of regular exercise. 
उदा. नियमित व्यायाम के लाभ के रूप में शरीर को 'fit' (स्वस्थ/गठीला) रखना सही विकल्प है।
[RULE] Adjective Selection in Context

---

[Q1118] Read the passage and fill in blank (3): Technology has transformed our lives completely. Today, we (3) ______ on computers and smartphones for almost every thing.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) depend
(b) distrust
(c) neglect
(d) ignore
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Depend on' is the correct phrasal collocation indicating reliance on technology. 
उदा. तकनीक पर निर्भरता दर्शाने के लिए 'depend' (निर्भर होना) सबसे उपयुक्त शब्द है।
[RULE] Collocation / Phrasal Verb

---

[Q1119] Read the passage and fill in blank (4): Books are our best companions. They enrich our mind and (4) ______ us with immense knowledge and wisdom.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) provide
(b) rob
(c) deprive
(d) strip
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'provide' is correctly followed by 'with' (provide us with immense knowledge). 
उदा. 'Provide' के साथ 'with' preposition का प्रयोग होता है, जो ज्ञान देने के संदर्भ में सही है।
[RULE] Prepositional Verb Agreement

---

[Q1120] Read the passage and fill in blank (5): Pollution is a grave threat to humanity. We must plant more trees to (5) ______ the effects of global warming.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) aggravate
(b) counter
(c) increase
(d) promote
[ANS] b
[SOL]
We plant trees to 'counter' (combat/mitigate) the negative effects of global warming. 
उदा. ग्लोबल वार्मिंग के प्रभावों का मुकाबला करने या उन्हें कम करने के लिए 'counter' सही शब्द है।
[RULE] Contextual Inference

---

[Q1121] Read the passage and fill in blank (6): Time and tide wait for none. Once lost, time can never be (6) ______ back.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) taken
(b) brought
(c) got
(d) passed
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The passive voice construction requires 'brought back' to mean retrieved. 
उदा. समय वापस लाने के संदर्भ में 'brought back' (वापस लाया जाना) का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Voice and Phrasal Usage

---

[Q1122] Read the passage and fill in blank (7): Honesty is the best policy. An honest person is respected by every one because he never resorts to (7) ______ means to achieve success.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) honest
(b) fair
(c) unfair
(d) noble
[ANS] c
[SOL]
An honest person avoids 'unfair' means, creating a logical contrast. 
उदा. एक ईमानदार व्यक्ति सफलता के लिए 'unfair' (अनुचित) साधनों का सहारा नहीं लेता है।
[RULE] Logical Contextual Contrast

---

[Q1123] Read the passage and fill in blank (8): Traveling broadens our horizons. It exposes us to different cultures and traditions, making us more (8) ______ minded.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) narrow
(b) broad
(c) strict
(d) rigid
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Broad-minded' is a standard compound adjective meaning tolerant and open to different views. 
उदा. यात्रा करने से व्यक्ति 'broad-minded' (विस्तृत सोच वाला) बनता है।
[RULE] Compound Adjective

---

[Q1124] Read the passage and fill in blank (9): Water is essential for life on Earth. However, due to rapid urbanization, clean drinking water is becoming (9) ______ scarce.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) increasingly
(b) decreasingly
(c) hardly
(d) scarcely
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Increasingly scarce' correctly modifies the adjective scarce to show a growing trend. 
उदा. पानी के कम होने की बढ़ती प्रवृत्ति को दर्शाने के लिए 'increasingly' (लगातार) का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Adverb-Adjective Modification

---

[Q1125] Read the passage and fill in blank (10): Success does not come over night. It requires consistent hard work, dedication, and the ability to (10) ______ failures.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) overcome
(b) welcome
(c) create
(d) invite
[ANS] a
[SOL]
One must 'overcome' failures to achieve ultimate success. 
उदा. सफलता पाने के लिए असफलताओं से पार पाना या उन्हें दूर करना ('overcome') जरूरी है।
[RULE] Collocation with Obstacles

---

[Q1126] By now, she has finished her research and is ready to present it to the committee. In fact, she has been working on it for months, and she will have [1] it by the end of the week.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) presented
(b) present
(c) presenting
(d) to present
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The sentence uses the Future Perfect tense to indicate an action that will be completed before a specific future time.
Hindi: "उसने अपनी शोध पूरी कर ली है और वह इसे प्रस्तुत कर चुका है।"
[RULE] Future Perfect Tense

---

[Q1127] The old library, which was once a bustling hub of learning, has now become a quiet place where students can [1] and reflect.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) read
(b) reading
(c) readed
(d) to read
[ANS] a
[SOL]
A simple present verb is needed after 'can' to express ability. The verb does not change in this construction.
Hindi: "छात्र पढ़ सकते हैं और चिंतन कर सकते हैं।"
[RULE] Modal Verb + Base Form

---

[Q1128] By the time the guests arrived, the chef had already [1] the entire menu for the evening.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) prepped
(b) prepared
(c) preparing
(d) preparation
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Past Simple tense is used for an action completed before another past action.
Hindi: "जब मेहमान आए, तब शेफ ने मेनू तैयार कर लिया था।"
[RULE] Past Simple Tense

---

[Q1129] If the company fails to meet the dead line the project will be [1] and additional funding will be sought.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) canceled
(b) cancelled
(c) cancelling
(d) Gregarious
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The past participle of 'cancel' is 'cancelled' (British spelling). It is used after 'will be' to form the passive future.
Hindi: "अगर कंपनी समय सीमा पूरी नहीं करती है तो परियोजना रद्द हो जाएगी।"
[RULE] Passive Voice with Future Tense

---

[Q1130] The new policy aims to reduce waste, but it is still unclear whether it will [1] the desired effect.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) have
(b) has
(c) having
(d) had
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The future perfect construction 'will have + past participle' indicates completion before another future event.
Hindi: "नीति वांछित प्रभाव डालेगी या नहीं, यह अभी स्पष्ट नहीं है।"
[RULE] Future Perfect Tense

---

[Q1131] The committee has been debating the proposal for weeks, yet no final decision has been [1] yet.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) made
(b) made up
(c) making
(d) make
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The passive present perfect 'has been made' indicates an action completed in the recent past.
Hindi: "कोई अंतिम निर्णय अभी तक नहीं लिया गया है।"
[RULE] Passive Voice with Present Perfect

---

[Q1132] He insisted that the report be [1] as soon as possible, but the team needed more time to gather data.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) submitted
(b) submit
(c) submitting
(d) submittal
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After 'be', the base form of the verb is used in the passive infinitive.
Hindi: "उसने कहा कि रिपोर्ट जल्द से जल्द सबमिट की जानी चाहिए।"
[RULE] Passive Infinitive

---

[Q1133] Students who study regularly tend to [1] better grades than those who cram before exams.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) achieve
(b) achieved
(c) achieving
(d) to achieve
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The infinitive 'to achieve' is the correct form after the verb 'tend to'.
Hindi: "जो छात्र नियमित रूप से पढ़ते हैं वे बेहतर अंक प्राप्त करते हैं।"
[RULE] Infinitive after Verb 'Tend to'

---

[Q1134] The mayor's speech was so inspiring that it motivated every one to [1] the community project.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) join
(b) joining
(c) joined
(d) to join
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After a verb of motivation like 'motivate', the base form of the following verb is used.
Hindi: "उसकी प्रेरणादायक भाषण ने सभी को समुदाय परियोजना में शामिल होने के लिए प्रेरित किया।"
[RULE] Verb + Base Form after 'Motivate'

---

[Q1135] The software update will [1] the security vulnerabilities that were discovered last month.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) fix
(b) fixed
(c) fixing
(d) to fix
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The passive future construction uses 'will' + base form of verb.
Hindi: "सॉफ्टवेयर अपडेट सुरक्षा कमजोरियों को ठीक करेगा।"
[RULE] Future Simple Passive

---

[Q1136] She has been working here _______ 2015.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) from
(d) during
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When you talk about a point in time from which something has happened, use **since**.
उदाहरण: वह 2015 से यहाँ काम कर रही है।
[RULE] Time expressions – since

---

[Q1137] If I _______ you, I would not take that job.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) am
(b) were
(c) was
(d) be
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The subjunctive mood uses **were** for hypothetical situations.
उदाहरण: यदि मैं तुम्हारी जगह होता, तो मैं वह नौकरी नहीं लेता।
[RULE] Subjunctive mood – were

---

[Q1138] The committee has decided to postpone the meeting _______ next week.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) until
(b) for
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use **until** to indicate the point up to which something continues.
उदाहरण: समिति ने बैठक अगले हफ्ते तक स्थगित करने का निर्णय लिया।
[RULE] Prepositions – until

---

[Q1139] He was the first person who _______ the mystery solved.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) solved
(b) solving
(c) solves
(d) solvedly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After a relative pronoun (who), use past tense to describe a completed action.
उदाहरण: वह पहला व्यक्ति था जिसने रहस्य को हल किया।
[RULE] Relative clauses – past tense

---

[Q1140] The new policy will be implemented _______ the fiscal year.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) in
(c) on
(d) at
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use **in** for a period of time such as a fiscal year.
उदाहरण: नई नीति वित्तीय वर्ष में लागू की जाएगी।
[RULE] Prepositions – in

---

[Q1141] If you _______ the instructions carefully, you will not make any mistakes.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) follow
(b) followed
(c) following
(d) to follow
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In a conditional clause, the verb remains in base form.
उदाहरण: यदि आप निर्देशों का ध्यानपूर्वक पालन करेंगे, तो कोई गलती नहीं होगी।
[RULE] Conditional sentences – base verb

---

[Q1142] She has been studying English _______ her entire life.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) through
(b) for
(c) from
(d) since
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Use **since** for a starting point in time.
उदाहरण: वह अपनी पूरी ज़िंदगी से अंग्रेज़ी का अध्ययन कर रही है।
[RULE] Time expressions – since

---

[Q1143] The dog, _______ was barking loudly, finally stopped.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) which
(b) who
(c) that
(d) whom
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use **which** for non‑essential relative clauses with animals and objects.
उदाहरण: कुत्ता, जो जोर से भौंक रहा था, अंततः रुक गया।
[RULE] Relative clauses – which

---

[Q1144] There are only a few people left who _______ the tradition.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) preserve
(b) preserved
(c) preserving
(d) preserves
[ANS] c
[SOL]
After a relative pronoun (who), use present participle for ongoing action.
उदाहरण: केवल कुछ लोग बचे हैं जो परंपरा को बनाए रखते हैं।
[RULE] Relative clauses – present participle

---

[Q1145] The report was published _______ the committee’s findings were finalized.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) after
(b) before
(c) when
(d) until
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use **after** to indicate that one event follows another.
उदाहरण: रिपोर्ट प्रकाशित हुई जब समिति के निष्कर्ष समाप्त हो गए थे।
[RULE] Prepositions – after

---

[Q1146] He always _______ to the market on Sundays.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) goes
(b) go
(c) going
(d) gone
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Subject-verb agreement in simple present tense. The subject "He" is singular, so the verb must be in singular form.
उदाहरण: "वह हर रविवार को बाजार जाता है।"
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1147] She decided _______ early to avoid the traffic.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to leave
(b) to leaving
(c) to left
(d) to lefts
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Infinitive form after the verb "decide". The correct form is "to leave".
उदाहरण: "उसने भीड़ से बचने के लिए जल्दी जाने का फैसला किया।"
[RULE] Infinitive after Modal Verb

---

[Q1148] The picture is _______ the wall.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Preposition of location for a picture on a wall is "on".
उदाहरण: "चित्र दीवार पर है।"
[RULE] Preposition of Location

---

[Q1149] It was _______ that he won the competition.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a fact
(b) the fact
(c) fact
(d) the facts
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Indefinite article usage before a singular countable noun. "Fact" is singular, so use "a".
उदाहरण: "यह एक तथ्य था कि उसने प्रतियोगिता जीती।"
[RULE] Indefinite Article Usage

---

[Q1150] This book is _______ than that one.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) more interesting
(b) most interesting
(c) interestinger
(d) interesting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Comparative form uses "more" + adjective for two items.
उदाहरण: "यह किताब दूसरी से अधिक रोचक है।"
[RULE] Comparatives

---

[Q1151] Yesterday, she _______ the letter to the post office.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) write
(b) writes
(c) wrote
(d) written
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Simple past tense of "write" is "wrote".
उदाहरण: "कल उसने पत्र डाकघर भेजा।"
[RULE] Simple Past Tense

---

[Q1152] You _______ finish the work by tomorrow.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) must
(b) musted
(c) musting
(d) mustes
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Modal verb "must" is used for obligation.
उदाहरण: "आपको कल तक काम पूरा करना अनिवार्य है।"
[RULE] Modal Verb for Obligation

---

[Q1153] The cake _______ by the chef yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was made
(b) is made
(c) made
(d) make
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Passive voice uses "was" + past participle for past tense.
उदाहरण: "केक कल शेफ द्वारा बनाया गया था।"
[RULE] Passive Voice (Past Tense)

---

[Q1154] ______ elephant is huge.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) An
(b) The
(c) A
(d) Some
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use "An" before a word starting with a vowel sound.
उदाहरण: "एक हाथी बहुत बड़ा होता है।"
[RULE] Article Usage (An)

---

[Q1155] I will go, _______ I will study.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) but
(b) so
(c) and
(d) or
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Conjunction "but" shows contrast between two clauses.
उदाहरण: "मैं जाऊँगा, लेकिन मैं पढ़ूँगा।"
[RULE] Conjunction (Contrast)

---

[Q1156] Air pollution is a major environmental issue that (1) ______ millions of people world wide.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) affects
(b) effects
(c) affecting
(d) affected
[ANS] a
[SOL]
We need a verb in the present tense to state a general fact. 'Affects' is a verb meaning to influence, whereas 'effect' is usually a noun.
उदा. यहाँ वर्तमान काल (Present Tense) की क्रिया की आवश्यकता है। 'Affects' एक क्रिया है जिसका अर्थ प्रभावित करना है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement / Vocabulary

---

[Q1157] Regular physical activity is vital (2) ______ maintaining good health and fitness.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) in
(b) for
(c) at
(d) with
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The adjective 'vital' is followed by the preposition 'for' when expressing the purpose or benefit.
उदा. विशेषण 'vital' (आवश्यक) के बाद उद्देश्य बताने के लिए 'for' पूर्वसर्ग (preposition) का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

---

[Q1158] Books are our best companions. They enrich our minds and (3) ______ us with knowledge.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) provide
(b) provide with
(c) furnish
(d) supply
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'provide' takes the structure 'provide someone with something' or simply 'provide something'. Here, 'provide us with' means to give us.
उदा. क्रिया 'provide' के बाद 'provide us with knowledge' संरचना सही बैठती है।
[RULE] Collocation / Verb Usage

---

[Q1159] Technology has (4) ______ our lives easier and more comfortable in many ways.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) make
(b) making
(c) made
(d) makes
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The sentence uses the present perfect tense structure (has + V3). Therefore, 'made' is the correct past participle form.
उदा. वाक्य में Present Perfect Tense (has + V3) का प्रयोग हुआ है, इसलिए 'made' सही विकल्प है।
[RULE] Verb Forms (Tenses)

---

[Q1160] The government has launched several schemes to (5) ______ poverty in rural areas.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) eradicate
(b) promote
(c) increase
(d) escalate
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextually, schemes are launched to remove or eradicate poverty, not to promote or increase it.
उदा. संदर्भ के अनुसार, योजनाएं गरीबी को दूर करने (eradicate) के लिए शुरू की जाती हैं, बढ़ाने के लिए नहीं।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q1161] A balanced diet is (6) ______ for the proper growth and development of children.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) essential
(b) trivial
(c) harmful
(d) optional
[ANS] a
[SOL]
For proper growth, a balanced diet is 'essential' (extremely important). Other options contradict the positive context.
उदा. बच्चों के उचित विकास के लिए संतुलित आहार 'essential' (आवश्यक) होता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Context

---

[Q1162] Trees play a crucial role in maintaining ecological balance by (7) ______ carbon dioxide.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) absorbing
(b) releasing
(c) emitting
(d) producing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Scientifically, trees absorb carbon dioxide from the atmosphere during photosynthesis.
उदा. वैज्ञानिक रूप से, पेड़ प्रकाश संश्लेषण के दौरान वायुमंडल से कार्बन डाइऑक्साइड को 'absorb' (अवशोषित) करते हैं।
[RULE] Scientific Fact / Vocabulary

---

[Q1163] Hard work is the only key to success; (8) ______ shortcuts never lead to lasting achievement.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) therefore
(b) however
(c) moreover
(d) consequently
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'However' is used to introduce a statement that contrasts with or seems to contradict something that has been said previously.
उदा. 'However' (हालाँकि/फिर भी) का प्रयोग विपरीत विचार या विरोधाभास प्रकट करने के लिए किया जाता है।
[RULE] Conjunctions / Transition Words

---

[Q1164] Water scarcity is a growing problem that requires immediate (9) ______ from all citizens.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) attention
(b) neglect
(c) indifference
(d) carelessness
[ANS] a
[SOL]
A growing problem of scarcity requires 'attention' or action, whereas other options are negative attributes.
उदा. पानी की कमी जैसी बढ़ती समस्या पर सभी नागरिकों से तत्काल 'attention' (ध्यान) की आवश्यकता है।
[RULE] Contextual Usage / Noun Forms

---

[Q1165] Traveling broadens our horizons and (10) ______ us to diverse cultures and traditions.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) exposes
(b) hides
(c) restricts
(d) limits
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Exposes us to' means introduces us to new experiences and cultures. Restricts or hides do not fit the positive context.
उदा. 'Exposes us to' का अर्थ हमें विभिन्न संस्कृतियों से परिचित कराना है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs / Collocation

[Q1166] Education is the key to success, and it plays a vital role in shaping an individual's future. It not only provides knowledge but also (1) skills necessary for personal and professional growth.

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) hones
(b) destroys
(c) ignores
(d) weakens
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextually, education enhances or hones skills. Therefore, 'hones' is the correct choice.
उदा. संदर्भ के अनुसार, शिक्षा कौशल को तराशती (hones) है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

[Q1167] Books are our best companions. They never demand anything from us, yet they (2) us with immense knowledge and pleasure.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) deprive
(b) provide
(c) rob
(d) strip
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The preposition 'with' following the blank strongly points towards the verb 'provide' (provide someone with something).
उदा. रिक्त स्थान के बाद आने वाला 'with' preposition 'provide' के साथ प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition / Verb-Preposition Collocation

[Q1168] Pollution has become a major threat to humanity. Unless we take urgent steps to control it, our planet will soon (3) uninhabitable.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) remain
(b) become
(c) stay
(d) persist
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The earth transitioning into a state of being uninhabitable requires the linking verb 'become'.
उदा. पृथ्वी का रहने योग्य न रहना (uninhabitable होना) दर्शाने के लिए 'become' का प्रयोग होगा।
[RULE] Linking Verbs / Context

---

[Q1169] Regular physical exercise is essential for maintaining good health. It keeps our body active and (4) the risk of chronic diseases.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) increases
(b) magnifies
(c) reduces
(d) escalates
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Exercise helps lower or reduce the risk of diseases. Thus, 'reduces' fits the positive context.
उदा. व्यायाम बीमारियों के जोखिम को कम (reduces) करता है।
[RULE] Contextual Logic

[Q1170] Time and tide wait for none. Once time is lost, it can never be (5).
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) retrieved
(b) forwarded
(c) delayed
(d) extended
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Retrieved' means to get back something that was lost. Lost time cannot be retrieved.
उदा. 'Retrieved' का अर्थ है खोई हुई चीज़ को वापस पाना। समय एक बार जाने पर वापस नहीं पाया जा सकता।
[RULE] Vocabulary in Context

[Q1171] Hard work is the bedrock of success. Those who are willing to put (6) extra effort always achieve their goals.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) off
(c) down
(d) out
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrasal verb 'put in' means to invest time or effort into something.
उदा. Phrasal verb 'put in' का अर्थ प्रयास या समय लगाना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

[Q1172] Traveling broadens our horizons. It exposes us to different cultures and traditions, making us more (7) towards others.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) intolerant
(b) tolerant
(c) suspicious
(d) hostile
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Exposure to diverse cultures naturally makes a person more accepting and 'tolerant'.
उदा. विभिन्न संस्कृतियों के संपर्क में आने से व्यक्ति अधिक सहनशील (tolerant) बनता है।
[RULE] Positive/Negative Tone Context

[Q1173] Honesty is the best policy. An honest person is respected every where because they never resort to (8) means to achieve success.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) fair
(b) noble
(c) unfair
(d) righteous
[ANS] c
[SOL]
An honest person avoids unfair means. Therefore, 'unfair' is the correct antonym-context fit.
उदा. एक ईमानदार व्यक्ति सफलता के लिए अनुचित (unfair) साधनों का सहारा नहीं लेता है।
[RULE] Contextual Antonyms / Modifiers

[Q1174] Water scarcity is a growing global concern. We must adopt rain water harvesting methods to (9) precious water for future generations.

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) waste
(b) conserve
(c) deplete
(d) scatter
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rainwater harvesting is done to save or 'conserve' water.
उदा. वर्षा जल संचयन का मुख्य उद्देश्य पानी को संरक्षित (conserve) करना है।

(a) poorly
(b) inefficiently
(c) properly
(d) wrongly
[ANS] c
[SOL]
A balanced diet helps the body function properly (correctly and efficiently).
उदा. संतुलित आहार शरीर को सुचारू रूप से (properly) कार्य करने में मदद करता है।
[RULE] Adverb Usage in Context

[Q1176] The committee (1) _______ the proposal after careful deliberation.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) approved
(b) approve
(c) approving
(d) approvedly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Subject-verb agreement: The verb "approved" must agree with the singular subject "committee" in simple past tense.
उदाहरण: "committee approved"।

[Q1177] She has (1) _______ to the market yesterday.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) gone
(b) go
(c) going
(d) gone to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Present perfect with "has" requires the past participle of the verb. Here the past participle of "go" is "gone".
उदाहरण: "She has gone to the market."

[Q1178] The students were (1) _______ by the teacher.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) praise
(b) praising
(c) praised
(d) praises
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Passive voice in the simple past: the teacher "praised" the students.
उदाहरण: "The students were praised by the teacher."

[Q1179] I would have gone to the party if I (1) _______ invited.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) have
(b) had
(c) has
(d) haves
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Third conditional: the if‑clause uses the past perfect "had".
उदाहरण: "if I had been invited"।
[RULE] Third Conditional

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) important
(b) importantly
(c) importance
(d) importantness
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Adjective "important" is used before a noun or verb, not as an adverb.
उदाहरण: "It is important to study."

[Q1181] He (1) _______ the book as soon as he saw it.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) read
(b) reads
(c) reading
(d) readed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Simple past tense: the action of reading is completed.
उदाहरण: "He read the book."
[RULE] Simple Past Tense

---

[Q1182] The price of oil has (1) _______ significantly since last year.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) increased
(b) increasing
(c) increase
(d) increas
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past participle of "increase" is "increased" used after "has".
उदाहरण: "The price has increased."

[Q1183] She is the most (1) _______ dancer in the group.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) good
(b) better
(c) best
(d) goodest
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Superlative form of "good" is "best".

[Q1184] They (1) _______ to finish the project by the dead line

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) will
(b) would
(c) should
(d) should have
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Modal verb "should" expresses an obligation or recommendation.

[Q1185] The doctor advised him to (1) _______ the medication.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) take
(b) took
(c) taking
(d) taken
[ANS] a

Infinitive form after verbs like "advise": "to take".

[Q1186] The scientific method is a systematic approach to acquiring knowledge about the world. It involves making observations, formulating a hypothesis, conducting experiments, and finally drawing conclusions. The hypothesis must be __________ (1) and testable, which means it can be verified through empirical evidence. Options: 1. [1] subject 2. [1] objective 3. [1] descriptive 4. [1] theoretical
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) subject
(b) objective
(c) descriptive
(d) theoretical
[ANS] b
[SOL]
In the context of a hypothesis, the word that best completes the sentence is "objective" because a hypothesis must be objective and testable. 

**Hindi Explanation:** एक परिकल्पना को उद्देश्यपूर्ण और परीक्षण योग्य होना चाहिए, इसलिए "objective" उपयुक्त शब्द है।
[RULE] Word Choice / Contextual Meaning

[1] literal

(a) evocative
(b) static
(c) abstract
(d) literal
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The passage refers to art that stimulates imagination, so "evocative" is the correct choice.

[Q1188] In the field of genetics, DNA replication occurs during the S phase of the cell cycle. This process is essential for __________ (1) of the organism. Options: 1. [1] growth 2. [1] decay 3. [1] analysis 4. [1] comparison

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) growth
(b) decay
(c) analysis
(d) comparison
[ANS] a
[SOL]
DNA replication is vital for the growth and development of an organism, not decay or comparison.
