[RULE] Noun Selection

---

[Q1189] The ancient city of Machu Picchu was built by the Incas in the 15th century. It is located at an altitude of about 2,400 metres, which made it __________ (1) for modern tourists. Options: 1. [1] challenging 2. [1] trivial 3. [1] mandatory 4. [1] impossible
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) challenging
(b) trivial
(c) mandatory
(d) impossible
[ANS] a
[SOL]
High altitude can be challenging for tourists, not trivial or impossible.

**Hindi Explanation:** ऊँचाई के कारण यह आधुनिक यात्रियों के लिए चुनौतीपूर्ण है, इसलिए "challenging" सही है।
[RULE] Adjective Context

---

[Q1190] In economics, inflation refers to the __________ (1) of the general price level of goods and services over time. Options: 1. [1] increase 2. [1] decrease 3. [1] stability 4. [1] fluctuation
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) increase
(b) decrease
(c) stability
(d) fluctuation
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Inflation is the increase in price levels.

**Hindi Explanation:** मुद्रास्फीति का मतलब वस्तुओं और सेवाओं की कीमतों में वृद्धि है, इसलिए "increase" सही उत्तर है।
[RULE] Noun Definition

---

[Q1191] The novel "1984" by George Orwell portrays a dystopian society where the government exercises totalitarian control. In this society, individual freedom is __________ (1) to the maximum. Options: 1. [1] reduced 2. [1] enhanced 3. [1] preserved 4. [1] expanded
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reduced
(b) enhanced
(c) preserved
(d) expanded
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Totalitarian regimes reduce individual freedom.

**Hindi Explanation:** सर्वसत्तावादी शासन में व्यक्तिगत स्वतंत्रता कम हो जाती है, इसलिए "reduced" सही है।
[RULE] Adverbial Context

---

[Q1192] When conducting a scientific experiment, it is crucial to control variables that could otherwise influence the out come These variables are known as __________ (1). Options: 1. [1] confounding factors 2. [1] primary variables 3. [1] independent variables 4. [1] dependent variables
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) confounding factors
(b) primary variables
(c) independent variables
(d) dependent variables
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Variables that may affect the outcome are confounding factors.

**Hindi Explanation:** प्रयोग में उन परिवर्तनों को नियंत्रित करना चाहिए जो परिणाम को प्रभावित कर सकते हैं, इन्हें "confounding factors" कहते हैं।
[RULE] Technical Term Usage

---

[Q1193] The process of photosynthesis in plants converts light energy into chemical energy, which is stored in the form of __________ (1). Options: 1. [1] glucose 2. [1] cellulose 3. [1] chlorophyll 4. [1] nitrogen
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) glucose
(b) cellulose
(c) chlorophyll
(d) nitrogen
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Photosynthesis stores energy as glucose.

**Hindi Explanation:** पौधे प्रकाश ऊर्जा को रासायनिक ऊर्जा में बदलते हैं, जिसे ग्लूकोज़ के रूप में संग्रहीत किया जाता है।
[RULE] Scientific Vocabulary

---

[Q1194] In the context of the Indian Constitution, the right to freedom of speech is protected under Article __________ (1). Options: 1. [1] 19 2. [1] 21 3. [1] 14 4. [1] 16
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) 19
(b) 21
(c) 14
(d) 16
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Article 19 guarantees freedom of speech and expression.

**Hindi Explanation:** भारतीय संविधान के अनुच्छेद 19 में अभिव्यक्ति की स्वतंत्रता का अधिकार संरक्षित है।
[RULE] Article Reference

---

[Q1195] The Treaty of Versailles, signed in 1919, aimed to punish Germany after World War I. It imposed severe __________ (1) on Germany, leading to economic hard ship Options: 1. [1] reparations 2. [1] incentives 3. [1] subsidies 4. [1] concessions
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reparations
(b) incentives
(c) subsidies
(d) concessions
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The treaty required Germany to pay reparations.

**Hindi Explanation:** वर्साय संधि ने जर्मनी पर भारी क्षतिपूर्ति (reparations) लगाई, इसलिए "reparations" सही है।
[RULE] Historical Term Usage

---

[Q1196] Agriculture is the backbone of the Indian economy. Despite rapid industrialization, a vast majority of the population still _____ on agriculture for their livelihood.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) depends
(b) depended
(c) depending
(d) depend
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Subject 'majority of the population' refers to plural sense here taking plural verb 'depend' for present simple habitual action.
उदा. बहुवचन कर्ता के साथ वर्तमान काल में क्रिया का मूल रूप (plural verb) प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1197] However, farming in India faces several challenges. One of the major problems is the fragmentation of land holdings, which makes modern farming techniques difficult to _____.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) adopt
(b) adapt
(c) adept
(d) abide
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Adopt' means to take up or start using a method or idea. 'Adapt' means to adjust.
उदा. यहाँ नई तकनीक को 'अपनाने' (adopt) के अर्थ में सही शब्द चुनना है।
[RULE] Confusing Words / Vocabulary

---

[Q1198] Furthermore, farmers are heavily _____ on monsoon rains, leading to severe crop failures during drought years.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) dependent
(b) independant
(c) reliable
(d) trustworthy
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Dependent on' is the correct fixed prepositional phrase.
उदा. dependent के साथ हमेशा preposition 'on' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q1199] To over come these issues, the government has introduced various welfare schemes to provide financial _____ to distressed farmers.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) support
(b) supporter
(c) supporting
(d) supported
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Noun is required after the adjective 'financial'. 'Support' acts as a noun here.
उदा. 'financial' विशेषण के बाद संज्ञा (Noun) का प्रयोग होगा, अतः 'support' सही है।
[RULE] Parts of Speech

---

[Q1200] Irrigation facilities are also being expanded to ensure that farmers do not have to _____ solely on erratic rain fall
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) rely
(b) relying
(c) relied
(d) relies
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Modal verb 'have to' is followed by the base form of the verb (V1).
उदा. 'have to' के बाद हमेशा वर्ब की पहली फॉर्म (V1) आती है।
[RULE] Modals + Verb Form

---

[Q1201] Digital India initiatives have started playing a crucial role in bringing transparency _____ the distribution of subsidies.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) into
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The noun 'transparency' takes the preposition 'in' when referring to areas or processes.
उदा. प्रक्रिया या क्षेत्र में पारदर्शिता के लिए 'transparency in' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Prepositions

---

[Q1202] Soil health cards are being issued so that farmers can understand the nutrient status of their fields and apply fertilizers _____.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) wisely
(b) wisest
(c) wise
(d) wisdom
[ANS] a
[SOL]
An adverb is needed to modify the verb 'apply'. 'Wisely' is the correct adverb.
उदा. क्रिया 'apply' की विशेषता बताने के लिए एडवर्ब (adverb) 'wisely' का प्रयोग होगा।
[RULE] Adverb Usage

---

[Q1203] Cold storage chains are being built to reduce the huge post-harvest losses _____ farmers suffer every year.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) that
(b) who
(c) whom
(d) whose
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Relative pronoun 'that' is preferred for non-living antecedents or general restrictive clauses involving things/losses.
उदा. निर्जीव वस्तुओं या सामान्य संदर्भ के लिए relative pronoun 'that' उपयुक्त है।
[RULE] Relative Pronouns

---

[Q1204] If these measures are implemented effectively, the agrarian crisis can be _____ managed in the coming years.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) better
(b) best
(c) good
(d) well
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Comparative degree 'better' is used to denote an improvement over the current state of crisis management.
उदा. स्थिति में सुधार दर्शाने के लिए comparative degree 'better' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Degrees of Comparison

---

[Q1205] Ultimately, sustainable agricultural growth is _____ for ensuring food security for the entire nation.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) essential
(b) trivial
(c) harmful
(d) optional
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextual meaning: Agricultural growth is extremely important ('essential') for food security.
उदा. संदर्भ के अनुसार खाद्य सुरक्षा के लिए कृषि विकास 'आवश्यक' (essential) है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q1206] Passage: Forests are essential for maintaining ecological balance. They provide habitats for numerous species and (1) _______ clean air. Deforestation, however, has become a major threat. Options for blank (1)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) generate
(b) generates
(c) generated
(d) generating
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The subject 'They' (referring to forests) is plural, and the sentence is in the simple present tense indicating a general truth. Therefore, the plural verb 'generate' is required.
उदा. Subject 'They' plural है और वाक्य Simple Present Tense में है, इसलिए plural verb 'generate' का प्रयोग होगा।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1207] Passage: Forests are essential for maintaining ecological balance. They provide habitats for numerous species and generate clean air. Deforestation, however, has become a major threat. It not only destroys wild life (2) _______ also contributes to global warming. Options for blank (2)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) and
(b) but
(c) also
(d) or
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction pair is 'not only... but also'. Hence, 'but' is the correct word to follow 'not only'.
उदा. Correlative conjunction 'not only... but also' का प्रयोग होता है, इसलिए खाली स्थान में 'but' आएगा।
[RULE] Conjunctions (Correlative Pair)

---

[Q1208] Passage: Reading books is a wonderful habit. It broadens our horizons and (3) _______ our minds. People who read regularly have better vocabulary and concentration. Options for blank (3)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) enriches
(b) enrich
(c) enriched
(d) enriching
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The singular subject 'It' takes the singular verb in the present tense 'enriches' to maintain parallelism with 'broadens'.
उदा. Singular subject 'It' के साथ Present Tense में singular verb 'enriches' का प्रयोग होगा ('broadens' के साथ parallel रखने के लिए)।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement / Parallelism

---

[Q1209] Passage: Success in life depends largely (4) _______ hard work and perseverance. Luck may play a small role, but consistent effort is irreplaceable. Options for blank (4)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) for
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb 'depend' is always followed by the fixed preposition 'on' (or 'upon').
उदा. Verb 'depend' के साथ निश्चित preposition 'on' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q1210] Passage: Technology has revolutionized communication. Emails and instant messaging have made the world a global village, (5) _______ people to connect instantly across continents. Options for blank (5)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) allow
(b) allowed
(c) allowing
(d) allows
[ANS] c
[SOL]
A present participle ('allowing') is used here to show the result or simultaneous action of the preceding clause.
उदा. पिछले clause के परिणाम या निरंतर क्रिया को दर्शाने के लिए Present Participle ('allowing') का उपयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Participles

---

[Q1211] Passage: Water scarcity is a growing global concern. Many regions face severe droughts due to erratic rain fall and (6) _______ exploitation of ground water. Options for blank (6)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) excessive
(b) excess
(c) exceed
(d) exceedingly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
An adjective is required before the noun 'exploitation'. 'Excessive' means too much, which fits the context of groundwater misuse.
Noun 'exploitation' से पहले Adjective की आवश्यकता है। 'Excessive' (अत्यधिक) यहाँ संदर्भ के अनुसार सबसे उपयुक्त है।
[RULE] Adjective Usage

---

[Q1212] Passage: Regular exercise is vital for maintaining physical fitness. It strengthens the heart, improves stamina, and helps in (7) _______ weight. Options for blank (7)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) lose
(b) losing
(c) lost
(d) loss
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Prepositions like 'in' are followed by gerunds (verb + -ing). Hence, 'losing' is the correct choice.
Preposition 'in' के बाद Gerund (verb + -ing) का प्रयोग होता है, अतः 'losing' सही विकल्प है।
[RULE] Gerunds after Prepositions

---

[Q1213] Passage: Honesty is the best policy. An honest person is trusted by every one and (8) _______ respect in society. Options for blank (8)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) earns
(b) earn
(c) earned
(d) earning
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The subject 'An honest person' is singular, requiring the singular present tense verb 'earns' to match 'is trusted'.
Subject 'An honest person' singular है, इसलिए Present Tense में singular verb 'earns' आएगा जो 'is trusted' के साथ मेल खाता है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1214] Passage: Traveling broadens the mind. It exposes us to diverse cultures and traditions, (9) _______ us more tolerant and open-minded. Options for blank (9)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) make
(b) making
(c) made
(d) makes
[ANS] b
[SOL]
A participle clause showing consequence uses the '-ing' form (making). It shows the effect of the previous statement.
परिणाम दर्शाने वाले Participle clause में '-ing' फॉर्म (making) का प्रयोग होता है जो पिछले कथन के प्रभाव को बताता है।
[RULE] Participles and Result Clauses

---

[Q1215] Passage: Time and tide wait for none. Once time is lost, it can never be (10) _______. Options for blank (10)
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) recover
(b) recovered
(c) recovering
(d) recovers
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The sentence is in the passive voice ('can never be...'). The modal passive structure takes the past participle (V3) form of the verb.
वाक्य Passive Voice में है ('can never be...'), जिसके साथ verb की Past Participle (V3) फॉर्म यानी 'recovered' का प्रयोग होगा।
[RULE] Passive Voice Modals

---

[Q1216] Read the passage and fill in blank (1): Education is not merely acquiring knowledge for a livelihood, it is (1) ______ for life. Passage context: Education and human development.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) preparation
(b) prevention
(c) prediction
(d) prescription
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextually, education prepares a person for life's challenges. 'Preparation' fits best.
उदा. संदर्भ के अनुसार, शिक्षा जीवन के लिए तैयार करती है, इसलिए 'preparation' सही विकल्प है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q1217] Read the passage and fill in blank (2): Books are our best companions. They never demand anything from us (2) ______ give us immense joy and wisdom. Passage context: The value of reading books.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) so
(b) but
(c) or
(d) because
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The conjunction 'but' is used to show contrast between not demanding anything and giving immense joy.
उदा. यहाँ विरोध (contrast) दर्शाने के लिए 'but' (लेकिन) कंजंक्शन का प्रयोग किया जाएगा।
[RULE] Conjunctions

---

[Q1218] Read the passage and fill in blank (3): Pollution has become a major threat to our environment. It is (3) ______ our natural resources and harming wild life Passage context: Environmental degradation.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) protecting
(b) conserving
(c) depleting
(d) building
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Pollution negatively affects natural resources; 'depleting' means exhausting or reducing resources, which fits the context.
उदा. प्रदूषण प्राकृतिक संसाधनों को कम या नष्ट कर रहा है, इसलिए 'depleting' (ह्रास करना) उपयुक्त है।
[RULE] Vocabulary and Context

---

[Q1219] Read the passage and fill in blank (4): Success in any field requires consistent hard work. There is no (4) ______ cut to success. Passage context: Importance of hard work.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) short
(b) easy
(c) quick
(d) fast
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The standard idiom is 'short cut', meaning a quicker way to do something.
उदा. सही मुहावरा 'short cut' है, जिसका अर्थ है किसी काम को करने का छोटा रास्ता।
[RULE] Idiomatic Expressions

---

[Q1220] Read the passage and fill in blank (5): Physical exercise is essential to keep our body fit and active. It improves blood circulation and (5) ______ our immune system. Passage context: Benefits of physical exercise.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) weakens
(b) destroys
(c) strengthens
(d) ignores
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Exercise has positive effects on health, so it 'strengthens' the immune system.
उदा. व्यायाम स्वास्थ्य के लिए अच्छा होता है, यह हमारी प्रतिरक्षा प्रणाली को मजबूत (strengthens) करता है।
[RULE] Contextual Logic

---

[Q1221] Read the passage and fill in blank (6): Time and tide wait for none. Once time is lost, it can never be (6) ______. Passage context: The value of time.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) recovered
(b) recalled
(c) retrieved
(d) returned
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Lost time cannot be regained or 'recovered'. Recover is standard for getting back lost time or health.
उदा. खोए हुए समय को वापस नहीं पाया जा सकता, इसके लिए 'recovered' शब्द सबसे उपयुक्त है।
[RULE] Collocations

---

[Q1222] Read the passage and fill in blank (7): Honesty is the best policy. An honest person is trusted by every one and leads a (7) ______ life. Passage context: Virtues of honesty.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) peaceful
(b) turbulent
(c) stressful
(d) fearful
[ANS] a
[SOL]
An honest person has a clear conscience and leads a peaceful life.
उदा. एक ईमानदार व्यक्ति का मन शांत होता है और वह शांतिपूर्ण (peaceful) जीवन जीता है।
[RULE] Adjective Selection

---

[Q1223] Read the passage and fill in blank (8): Traveling broadens our horizons. It exposes us to different cultures and traditions, making us more (8) ______. Passage context: Benefits of traveling.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) narrow-minded
(b) tolerant
(c) ignorant
(d) rigid
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Experiencing diverse cultures makes a person accepting and 'tolerant' of differences.
उदा. विभिन्न संस्कृतियों को जानने से व्यक्ति अधिक सहनशील (tolerant) बनता है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q1224] Read the passage and fill in blank (9): Technology has transformed our lives completely. Tasks that took days can now be done (9) ______ a fraction of the time. Passage context: Impact of technology.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correct preposition phrase is 'in a fraction of the time'.
उदा. सही पूर्वसर्ग (preposition) 'in' है जिसका प्रयोग 'in a fraction of the time' वाक्यांश में होता है।
[RULE] Prepositions

---

[Q1225] Read the passage and fill in blank (10): Forests are vital for maintaining ecological balance. Deforestation leads to global warming and climate (10) ______. Passage context: Environment and climate change.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) change
(b) shift
(c) variation
(d) modification
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The fixed environmental term is 'climate change'.
उदा. पर्यावरण से जुड़ा हुआ निश्चित शब्द समूह 'climate change' (जलवायु परिवर्तन) है।
[RULE] Fixed Phrase / Collocation

---

[Q1226] Despite all challenges, the team managed to (1) ______ the project on time.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) complete
(b) completed
(c) completing
(d) completes
[ANS] a
[SOL]
To + base form of the verb (Infinitive) is required after 'managed'. Therefore, 'complete' is correct.
उदा. 'Manage' के बाद infinitive (to + verb की पहली फॉर्म) का प्रयोग होता है, इसलिए 'complete' सही है।
[RULE] Infinitive (to + V1)

---

[Q1227] Reading books not only enhances knowledge (2) ______ also broadens our perspective.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) and
(b) but
(c) or
(d) nor
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction pair is 'not only ... but also'. Hence, 'but' is the correct choice.
उदा. 'Not only' के साथ हमेशा 'but also' का जोड़ा प्रयोग होता है। इसलिए यहाँ 'but' आएगा।
[RULE] Correlative Conjunctions

---

[Q1228] Pollution has become a major threat, (3) ______ affects both flora and fauna.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) who
(b) whom
(c) which
(d) whose
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Relative pronoun 'which' is used for non-living things and clauses referring to things like pollution.
उदा. निर्जीव वस्तुओं या पूरे उपवाक्य के लिए relative pronoun 'which' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Relative Pronouns

---

[Q1229] He was accused (4) ______ theft by his employer.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) of
(c) with
(d) in
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The fixed preposition used with the adjective/participle 'accused' is 'of'.
उदा. 'Accused' शब्द के साथ हमेशा निश्चित preposition 'of' का प्रयोग होता है (accused of theft)।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q1230] Hardly had I stepped out of the house (5) ______ it started raining heavily.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) than
(b) when
(c) then
(d) that
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Hardly/Scarcely' is always followed by the conjunction 'when' (or 'before').
उदा. 'Hardly' या 'Scarcely' के साथ हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग होता है, जबकि 'No sooner' के साथ 'than' आता है।
[RULE] Conjunction pairs

---

[Q1231] She speaks English (6) ______ than her brother.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) fluent
(b) fluently
(c) more fluently
(d) most fluently
[ANS] c
[SOL]
A comparative adverb ('more fluently') is required because of the comparison indicated by 'than'.
उदा. 'Than' से तुलना दर्शाई गई है, इसलिए adverbs की comparative degree ('more fluently') का प्रयोग होगा।
[RULE] Degrees of Comparison

---

[Q1232] Neither the teacher nor the students (7) ______ present in the class.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) has
[ANS] b
[SOL]
According to the rule of 'Neither...nor', the verb agrees with the closer subject ('students', which is plural).
उदा. 'Neither...nor' में क्रिया पास वाले कर्ता (subject) के अनुसार आती है। 'students' बहुवचन है, अतः 'were' आएगा।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1233] The government has taken several steps to (8) ______ the economy.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) boost
(b) boosted
(c) booster
(d) boosting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Infinitive marker 'to' is followed by the base form of the verb (V1), which is 'boost'.
उदा. 'To' के बाद वर्ब की पहली फॉर्म (V1) आती है, इसलिए 'boost' सही विकल्प है।
[RULE] Verb Forms

---

[Q1234] A great deal of effort (9) ______ required to achieve success.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) are
(b) is
(c) were
(d) have
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'A great deal of' is followed by an uncountable noun ('effort'), which takes a singular verb ('is').
उदा. 'A great deal of' के बाद uncountable noun आता है जिसके साथ हमेशा एकवचन (singular) क्रिया 'is' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Quantifiers & Verb Agreement

---

[Q1235] The manager asked the employee why he (10) ______ late for work.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) is
(b) was
(c) has been
(d) were
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The reporting verb 'asked' is in the past tense, so the reported clause must also be in the past tense ('was').
उदा. मुख्य वाक्य past tense ('asked') में है, इसलिए आगे का उपवाक्य भी past tense ('was') में होगा।
[RULE] Sequence of Tenses

(a) working
(b) worked
(c) work
(d) works
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Present perfect continuous tense.
उदाहरण: वह पूरे दिन काम कर रही है।
[RULE] Tense - Present Perfect Continuous

(a) on

Prepositions used with days.

(a) correct

Infinitive after verb.

[Q1240] If you ___ the instructions, you will get the right answer.

(a) follow

Conditional sentence with base verb.

Noun for picture.

(a) smart

Comparative adjective.

Past tense verb after 'had to'.

Adjective form.

Base form after 'to'.

[Q1246] The ___ (1) of the old building was ___ (2) as a museum.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) conversion

(c) reconfiguration
(d) renovation

[Q1247] He could not help but feel that the new policy would ___ (1) the economy.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) stimulate
(b) stimulating
(c) stimulated
(d) stimulus
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Stimulate" is the infinitive form of the verb required after "would" to express future effect. The other options are either wrong form or a noun.
उदाहरण के लिए, "stimulate" यहाँ सही है।

[Q1248] The students, ___ (1) to the quiz, were eager to win.

(a) aspiring
(b) aspirants
(c) aspirated
(d) aspire
[ANS] a

"Aspiring" is an adjective describing the students' desire for the quiz. "Aspirants" would be a noun and not fit the grammatical structure.

[Q1249] ___ (1) the results were announced, the team celebrated.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Upon
(b) In
(c) After
(d) During
[ANS] c

"After" correctly indicates the time sequence. "Upon" and "During" would not fit the context.
उदाहरण के लिए, "After" उपयुक्त है।
[RULE] Preposition usage (time)

[Q1250] The ___ (1) of the treaty was debated for months.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) ratification
(b) ratified
(c) ratify
(d) ratify's
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Ratification" is a noun meaning the act of approving the treaty. The other options are verb forms or incorrect.
उदाहरण के लिए, "ratification" सही है।

[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) sudden
(b) suddenly
(c) suddenness
(d) Impeccable
[ANS] b
[SOL]
"Suddenly" is the adverb needed to describe how the cat behaved. "Sudden" and "suddenly" are incorrect forms.
उदाहरण के लिए, "suddenly" सही है।

[Q1252] She had to ___ (1) the letter before the dead line
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) submit
(b) submitted
(c) submission
(d) submitting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Submit" is the base form of the verb used after "to" following "had to". The other options are wrong forms.
उदाहरण के लिए, "submit" सही है।

[Q1253] The ___ (1) of the experiment proved the hypothesis.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) result
(b) results
(c) resul
(d) results'
[ANS] b
[SOL]
"Results" (plural) fits the context of multiple findings from the experiment. "Result" singular would also be acceptable but the plural is more natural.

[Q1254] ___ (1) the river flowed into the sea, the fish swam.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) When
(b) While
(c) Since
(d) Because
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"When" sets the time condition for the action. "While" could work but "when" is the more precise choice here. "Since" and "Because" do not fit the temporal sense.
उदाहरण के लिए, "When" उपयुक्त है।
[RULE] Temporal conjunctions

[Q1255] The manager's decision was ___ (1) by the board.

(a) approved
(b) approving
(c) approval
(d) approvement
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Approved" is the past participle used in the passive construction "was approved by". "Approving" is a gerund and does not fit.
उदाहरण के लिए, "approved" सही है।

(a) so
(b) very
(c) really
(d) extremely
[ANS] b

(a) read

[ANS] a

Past simple tense is used for an action completed in the past. उदाहरण: "He read the book yesterday."

(a) called
(b) call
(c) calling
(d) to call
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The third conditional uses 'had + past participle' and 'would have + past participle'. उदाहरण: "I would have called you earlier."
[RULE] Conditional perfect

(a) won

[ANS] a

Past simple is used after a prepositional phrase that does not change the tense. उदाहरण: "The team won the championship."

(a) bright

[ANS] b

Use the superlative form of an adjective to indicate the highest degree. उदाहरण: "She is the brightest student."

(a) launch

(a) cover

(a) missed

(a) findings

[Q1269] He has been studying French ______ he moved to Paris. [1]

(a) since
(b) for

(d) after
[ANS] a

Use 'since' to indicate the starting point of an action that continues to the present.

(d) whom
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use 'which' for non‑restrictive relative clauses referring to objects.

[Q1271] Please make sure to bring your ID card, ______ you might not enter the premises. [1]

(a) or

(c) but
(d) so

[SOL]

[Q1272] She could not attend the conference because she was ill, ______ she had to cancel her present at i on. [1]

(b) however
(c) instead
(d) besides
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use 'therefore' to express a logical consequence.

[Q1273] The new policy will be effective from next month, ______ employees will receive more benefits. [1]

(a) so

(c) anyway
(d) instead
[ANS] a

Use 'so' to indicate a result that follows from a previous statement.

[Q1274] The committee will decide on the proposal after reviewing the ______. [1]
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) report
(b) reports
(c) reporting
(d) reported
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use singular noun when referring to a single document.
उदाहरण: समिति रिपोर्ट की समीक्षा के बाद निर्णय लेगी।
[RULE] Singular vs. Plural Noun

---

[Q1275] He was given a chance to prove himself, ______ he did a remarkable job. [1]
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) and
(b) but
(c) so
(d) yet
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use 'and' to link two positive statements.
उदाहरण: उसे स्वयं को साबित करने का मौका मिला, और उसने उत्कृष्ट काम किया।
[RULE] Coordinating Conjunction (And)

[Q1276] Read the passage carefully and select the most appropriate option to fill in blank (1). Passage: Education is the key to unlocking human potential. [Q1277] Read the passage carefully and select the most appropriate option to fill in blank (2). Passage: Education is the key to unlocking human potential. [Q1278] Read the passage carefully and select the most appropriate option to fill in blank (1). Passage: Artificial Intelligence is transforming industries rapidly. [Q1279] Read the passage carefully and select the most appropriate option to fill in blank (2). Passage: Artificial Intelligence is transforming industries rapidly. [EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]

[Q1296] The committee had been working on the proposal for months, but when the final report was submitted, it turned out that [1] was missing.

[Q1297] She has been practicing piano for years, and her performance tonight will demonstrate her [1].

(a) skill

(c) skillful

[ANS] a

After the possessive determiner *her*, a singular noun is required because the performance demonstrates one quality.

(a) main

(a) revealed

(a) deadline
(b) deadlines
(c) dead line
(d) dead lining
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The noun *deadline* is singular and refers to a single time limit.

[ANS] a

(a) its's

(d) Vibrant
[ANS] c

(a) more

(d) most of
[ANS] a

(a) teamwork

(a) incomplete
(b) incompletely

(d) incomplete's
[ANS] a

[Q1306] The chef, who had been working at the restaurant for five years, was finally promoted to [1] chef.

(a) executive
(b) executing
(c) execution
(d) executive's
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The blank requires a noun that denotes a title or position. "Executive" is a noun functioning as a title, whereas "executing" is a verb/adjective, "execution" refers to the act, and "executive's" is possessive.

[Q1307] He was praised for his [1] leader ship during the crisis.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) effective
(b) effectiveness
(c) effectively
(d) effected
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective "effective" correctly describes the quality of leadership. "Effectiveness" is a noun, "effectively" is an adverb, and "effected" is a verb.
उदाहरण: "उनकी प्रभावी नेतृत्व ने संकट को सुलझाया।"

[Q1308] The new software will [1] improve the efficiency of data processing.

(a) significantly
(b) significantlyly
(c) significantly's
(d) Hostile
[ANS] a

[Q1309] She has been an advocate for [1] rights for over a decade.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) human
(b) humane
(c) humanity
(d) humanity's
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Human" is an adjective used to describe the type of rights. "Humane" means compassionate, "humanity" is a noun, and "humanity's" is possessive.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) relevant
(b) relevance
(c) relevantly
(d) Venerable
[ANS] a
[SOL]

[Q1311] The project will [1] next month, provided all approvals are secured.

(a) launch
(b) launches
(c) launching
(d) launched
[ANS] a

"Launch" is the base form used with the future tense "will launch". "Launches" is third person singular present, "launching" is present participle, and "launched" is past tense.

"Ancient" is an adjective describing the era. "Anciently" is an adverb, "ancient's" is possessive, and "ancientness" is a noun.

(a) final

(d) final's

[Q1314] She handled the situation with [1] calmness that impressed every one.

(a) unwavering

[ANS] a

[Q1315] The council will convene next week to discuss [1] matters.

(a) administrative
(b) administratively

(d) administrative's
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Administrative" is the adjective describing the matters. "Administratively" is an adverb, "administration" is a noun, "administrative's" is possessive.

[Q1316] Education is the key to success. It ____ the mind and broadens our perspective.

(a) sharpens
(b) blunts
(c) weakens
(d) destroys
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextual vocabulary: 'Sharpens' means to improve or make acute, which fits the positive context of education. 
उदा. 'Sharpens' का अर्थ तेज करना है, जो शिक्षा के सकारात्मक प्रभाव को दर्शाता है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q1317] Many species of animals are on the ____ of extinction due to human activities.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) verge
(b) middle
(c) center
(d) inside
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Collocation: The phrase 'on the verge of' means very close to experiencing something. 
उदा. 'on the verge of' एक निश्चित वाक्यांश (collocation) है जिसका अर्थ 'के कगार पर' होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Collocations

---

[Q1318] Regular exercise is ____ for maintaining good physical and mental health.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) essential
(b) harmful
(c) trivial
(d) needless
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextual meaning: 'Essential' means extremely important or necessary for health. 
उदा. अच्छे स्वास्थ्य के लिए नियमित व्यायाम 'आवश्यक' (essential) है।
[RULE] Adjective Usage

---

[Q1319] The government has launched a new scheme to ____ poverty in rural areas.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) eradicate
(b) create
(c) promote
(d) increase
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextual verb: 'Eradicate' means to destroy completely or put an end to, perfectly matching the objective of poverty reduction schemes. 
उदा. 'Eradicate' का अर्थ जड़ से उखाड़ना या समाप्त करना है।
[RULE] Verb Forms and Context

---

[Q1320] Books are our best friends. They guide us ____ we feel lost or lonely.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) whenever
(b) wherever
(c) whatever
(d) whoever
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Conjunction of time: 'Whenever' is used to indicate at whatever time or every time. 
उदा. 'Whenever' का प्रयोग समय की अनिश्चितता को दर्शाने के लिए किया जाता है ('जब कभी भी')।
[RULE] Conjunctions

---

[Q1321] Pollution has become a major ____ to the survival of human beings on Earth.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) threat
(b) boon
(c) blessing
(d) help
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextual noun: 'Threat' refers to a cause of danger or harm, fitting the context of pollution. 
उदा. प्रदूषण मानव अस्तित्व के लिए एक बड़ा 'खतरा' (threat) बन गया है।
[RULE] Noun Context

---

[Q1322] She managed to complete the difficult project ____ all the odds against her.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) despite
(b) although
(c) because
(d) unless
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Preposition of contrast: 'Despite' means without being affected by; it does not take 'of'. 
उदा. 'Despite' का अर्थ 'के बावजूद' होता है और इसके साथ 'of' का प्रयोग नहीं होता।
[RULE] Prepositions / Contrast

---

[Q1323] Honesty is the best policy. An honest person is always respected and ____ by every one.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) trusted
(b) hated
(c) doubted
(d) ignored
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Parallelism and tone: 'Trusted' aligns positively with 'respected' in describing an honest person. 
उदा. 'Trusted' शब्द 'respected' के साथ सकारात्मक भाव को बनाए रखता है।
[RULE] Tone and Parallelism

---

[Q1324] Technology has ____ our lives easier and more connected than ever before.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) made
(b) make
(c) making
(d) makes
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Grammar Structure: Present Perfect tense requires 'has/have + V3'. Here 'made' is the past participle of 'make'. 
उदा. Present Perfect टेंस में 'has/have + V3' का प्रयोग होता है, इसलिए 'made' सही है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb / Tense Agreement

---

[Q1325] Time and tide wait for ____. We must complete our tasks on schedule.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) none
(b) someone
(c) anyone
(d) everyone
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Idiomatic Proverb: The standard proverb is 'Time and tide wait for none.' 
उदा. प्रसिद्ध लोकोक्ति (Proverb) 'Time and tide wait for none' का उपयोग किया गया है।
[RULE] Idioms and Proverbs

---

[Q1326] Education is not merely about acquiring knowledge; it is also about character building. A true education helps an individual to (1) ______ critical thin king skills and emotional intelligence.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) develop
(b) destroy
(c) hinder
(d) reduce
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextually, education helps a person foster or develop critical thinking skills. Negative words like destroy or hinder do not fit.
उदा. संदर्भ के अनुसार, शिक्षा व्यक्ति में आलोचनात्मक सोच और भावनात्मक बुद्धिमत्ता को 'विकसित' (develop) करने में मदद करती है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary & Collocation

---

[Q1327] The government has launched several initiatives to promote digital literacy in rural areas. These programs aim to (2) ______ the digital divide between urban and rural populations.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) widen
(b) bridge
(c) increase
(d) separate
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The idiom or standard collocation is to 'bridge the divide/gap' meaning to reduce the differences. 
उदा. डिजिटल विभाजन को 'कम करना या पाटना' (bridge the divide) एक मानक अभिव्यक्ति है।
[RULE] Fixed Collocation (Bridge the gap/divide)

---

[Q1328] Deforestation is a grave environmental threat. Trees absorb carbon dioxide and release oxygen, thus acting as natural (3) ______ of the atmosphere.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) polluters
(b) destroyers
(c) filters
(d) generators
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Trees clean the air by absorbing harmful gases, functioning metaphorically as filters.
उदा. पेड़ हानिकारक गैसों को अवशोषित करके हवा को साफ करते हैं, इसलिए वे 'फिल्टर' (filters) के रूप में कार्य करते हैं।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q1329] Regular physical exercise is indispensable for maintaining good health. It keeps the body active and (4) ______ various lifestyle diseases.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) invites
(b) prevents
(c) causes
(d) promotes
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Exercise helps in keeping the body healthy by stopping or preventing lifestyle diseases.
उदा. व्यायाम जीवन शैली से जुड़ी बीमारियों को 'रोकने' (prevents) में मदद करता है।
[RULE] Logical Consistency in Context

---

[Q1330] Ancient monuments are a part of our rich cultural heritage. We must take proactive measures to (5) ______ these historical sites from vandalism and decay.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) protect
(b) abandon
(c) neglect
(d) ignore
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Heritage sites need to be safeguarded or protected from damage and ruin.
उदा. ऐतिहासिक स्थलों को बर्बादी से 'बचाना' (protect) हमारी जिम्मेदारी है।
[RULE] Contextual Synonyms

---

[Q1331] Time management is a crucial skill for success. Those who procrastinate often (6) ______ behind their peers in academic and professional pursuits.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) stay
(b) fall
(c) run
(d) leap
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The phrasal verb 'fall behind' means to fail to keep up with others in progress or development.
उदा. 'Fall behind' का अर्थ पिछड़ जाना होता है। प्रोक्रैस्टिनेट (ालटाल) करने वाले लोग अपने साथियों से पिछड़ जाते हैं।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs (Fall behind)

---

[Q1332] The rapid growth of artificial intelligence has sparked wide spread debate. While some view it as a boon, others fear it might (7) ______ massive unemployment.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) result
(b) lead
(c) cause
(d) bring
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The verb 'cause' directly takes an object without a preposition, whereas 'lead' requires 'to' (lead to massive unemployment). Therefore, 'cause' fits structurally.
उदा. 'Cause' के बाद सीधे ऑब्जेक्ट आता है (cause unemployment), जबकि 'lead' के बाद 'to' की आवश्यकता होती है।
[RULE] Verb Transitivity and Syntax

---

[Q1333] Water scarcity is becoming an alarming issue globally. Communities must adopt rain water harvesting techniques to (8) ______ precious water resources.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) waste
(b) conserve
(c) deplete
(d) exhaust
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rainwater harvesting is done to save or conserve water, making 'conserve' the correct antonym to waste/deplete in this context.
उदा. वर्षा जल संचयन का उद्देश्य जल संसाधनों को 'संरक्षित करना' (conserve) है।
[RULE] Contextual Antonyms/Synonyms

---

[Q1334] Reading books not only enhances vocabulary but also broadens our perspective. It transports us to imaginary worlds and allows us to (9) ______ different cultures.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) ignore
(b) experience
(c) avoid
(d) dismiss
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Reading allows people to learn about and 'experience' different cultures through literature.
उदा. पुस्तकें पढ़ने से हमें विभिन्न संस्कृतियों को 'अनुभव करने' (experience) का अवसर मिलता है।
[RULE] Semantic Fit

---

[Q1335] Disaster management requires meticulous planning and swift execution. When natural calamities strike, the authorities must act promptly to (10) ______ human suffering.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) alleviate
(b) aggravate
(c) intensify
(d) augment
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Authorities act during disasters to reduce or alleviate suffering. Aggravate, intensify, and augment mean to increase, which is opposite to the goal.
उदा. आपदा के समय अधिकारियों का उद्देश्य मानवीय पीड़ा को 'कम करना' (alleviate) होता है।
[RULE] Advanced Vocabulary & Tone

---

[Q1336] The sun was shining brightly, and the children were playing in the park. They [1] had a great time.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had
(b) have
(c) having
(d) has
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use the simple past tense for completed actions.
उदाहरण: बच्चे ने खेलना समाप्त किया।
[RULE] Simple Past Tense

---

[Q1337] She [1] the keys on the table but they were missing.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) left
(b) leaves
(c) leaving
(d) to leave
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use the simple past of the verb 'leave' for past actions.
उदाहरण: उसने चाबी टेबल पर रखी।
[RULE] Simple Past Tense

---

[Q1338] The company will [1] the new policy next week.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) implement
(b) implemented
(c) implementing
(d) implements
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use the base form of the verb after modal 'will' for future actions.
उदाहरण: कंपनी नीति लागू करेगी।
[RULE] Future Simple with 'will'

---

[Q1339] If I [1] earlier, I would have caught the train.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had arrived
(b) arrived
(c) arriving
(d) have arrived
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use the past perfect tense in the 'if' clause for unreal past situations.
उदाहरण: अगर मैं पहले पहुँचता।
[RULE] Past Perfect Tense

---

[Q1340] The students were given a choice: either study hard or [1] easily.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) pass
(b) passes
(c) passing
(d) passed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use the base form of the verb after 'or' in a choice statement.
उदाहरण: आसान पास करें।
[RULE] Base Form after 'or'

---

[Q1341] He had been working for the company [1] the accident.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) at
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use 'since' with a specific point in time.
उदाहरण: दुर्घटना के बाद से।
[RULE] Use of 'since' with a specific time

---

[Q1342] Although it was raining, the match [1] continued.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) is
(c) will
(d) has been
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use the simple past tense after 'although' for past situations.
उदाहरण: बारिश के बावजूद मैच चला।
[RULE] Past Tense after 'although'

---

[Q1343] They have [1] the project completed before the dead line
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) already
(b) yet
(c) still
(d) just
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use 'already' to indicate an action finished earlier than expected.
उदाहरण: वे पहले ही प्रोजेक्ट पूरा कर चुके हैं।
[RULE] Use of 'already' with Perfect Tense

---

[Q1344] The committee will meet next Monday unless [1] necessary arrangements are made.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) any
(b) no
(c) some
(d) none
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use 'no' after 'unless' to negate the condition.
उदाहरण: यदि कोई आवश्यक व्यवस्था नहीं की गई।
[RULE] Negative with 'unless'

---

[Q1345] She is eager to [1] the new software for the first time.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) try
(b) tried
(c) trying
(d) tries
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use base form after 'to' in infinitive.
उदाहरण: नया सॉफ्टवेयर आज़माना।
[RULE] Infinitive with 'to'

---

[Q1346] The report was prepared by the team [1] they had finished the data collection.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) after
(b) before
(c) until
(d) since
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use 'after' to indicate a later action following a previous one.
उदाहरण: डेटा संग्रह के बाद रिपोर्ट तैयार की गई।
[RULE] Use of 'after' for sequence

---

[Q1347] The committee, along with its members, was __________ to discuss the new policy.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) informed
(b) inform
(c) informing
(d) information
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Subject-verb agreement: The subject is the collective noun 'committee', which is singular. Hence, the verb must be in the singular form.
उदाहरण: यदि विषय एकवचन है तो क्रिया भी एकवचन होनी चाहिए।
[RULE] Subject-verb agreement

---

[Q1348] Neither the manager nor the employees were __________ about the sudden changes.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) aware
(b) aware of
(c) awared
(d) awarding
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When a verb follows a compound subject joined by 'neither...nor', the verb agrees with the part nearest to it. Here, 'employees' (plural) requires a plural verb.
उदाहरण: 'Neither the dog nor the cats are noisy.'
[RULE] Verb agreement with compound subjects

---

[Q1349] The students, along with their teacher, were excited to __________ the exhibition.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) visit
(b) visiting
(c) visited
(d) to visit
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The gerund 'visiting' would be used after a verb like 'to be', but here the infinitive form 'visit' follows 'to'.
उदाहरण: 'I want to go.'
[RULE] Infinitive vs. Gerund

---

[Q1350] If he __________ earlier, he would have caught the train.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had left
(b) left
(c) has left
(d) leaves
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Third conditional requires 'had' + past participle for the if-clause. The main clause uses 'would have' + past participle.
उदाहरण: यदि वह पहले गया होता, तो वह ट्रेन पकड़ लेता।
[RULE] Third conditional

---

[Q1351] There is a large number of people at the rally, __________ many of them are students.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) who
(b) which
(c) that
(d) whom
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Relative pronoun 'which' is used for non‑human antecedents. Here, 'people' refers to a group of individuals.
उदाहरण: 'A book, which is on the table, is mine.'
[RULE] Relative pronouns for non‑human antecedents

---

[Q1352] She has been working at the firm for __________ years.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) five
(b) fived
(c) fives
(d) fives’
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correct form is the adjective 'five' used as a numeral. The other options are incorrect forms.
उदाहरण: 'He has lived here for ten years.'
[RULE] Numeral usage

---

[Q1353] The report, which was submitted yesterday, __________ the director’s approval.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) received
(b) receives
(c) receiving
(d) receiving the
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After a past tense verb, the following verb in a relative clause should be in the past tense as well. 'Received' is the correct past tense form.
उदाहरण: 'The letter, which arrived yesterday, was signed.'
[RULE] Past tense in relative clauses

---

[Q1354] By the time the manager arrives, the employees will have __________ the present at i on.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) completed
(b) completing
(c) completed the
(d) completed the presentation
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Future perfect tense requires 'will have' + past participle. The object 'the presentation' must be included.
उदाहरण: 'They will have finished the report by noon.'
[RULE] Future perfect tense

---

[Q1355] The manager suggested that the employees __________ the new policy immediately.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) implement
(b) implementing
(c) implemented
(d) implementing the
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After 'suggested that', the verb is in the base form (bare infinitive) without 'to'.
उदाहरण: 'She advised him to leave early.'
[RULE] Bare infinitive after 'suggest / advise / recommend'

---

[Q1356] The book was so interesting that I couldn't stop __________ it.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reading
(b) to read
(c) read
(d) readed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The gerund 'reading' follows the infinitive 'to' in the phrase 'couldn't stop'.
उदाहरण: 'I stopped to eat.'
[RULE] Gerund after verb of stopping

---

[Q1357] If the company __________ more profit, it would expand its operations.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) makes
(b) made
(c) Diligent
(d) Resilient
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Second conditional uses 'would' + base verb in main clause and 'if + past simple' in the if‑clause. Here, 'made' is correct.
उदाहरण: यदि वह अधिक समय देता, तो वह जल्दी काम पूरा कर लेता।
[RULE] Second conditional

---

[Q1358] The committee has decided to [1] the project.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) delay
(b) defer
(c) deferral
(d) delayed
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb 'decided' is followed by a base form verb after 'to'. The correct option is 'defer'.
उदाहरण: "वह निर्णय ले चुका है कि वह परियोजना को स्थगित करेगा।"
[RULE] Base verb after 'to'

---

[Q1359] She has been working as a teacher for [1] years.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) five
(b) fifth
(c) fived
(d) fives
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The noun 'years' is plural, so the adjective should be 'five', not 'fifth'.
उदाहरण: "वह पाँच साल से शिक्षिका के रूप में काम कर रही है।"
[RULE] Adjective number agreement

---

[Q1360] If I had known the truth, I would have [1] the decision.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) changed
(b) change
(c) changing
(d) changes
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Conditional past perfect requires a past participle: 'changed'.
उदाहरण: "अगर मुझे सच पता होता, तो मैंने निर्णय बदल दिया होता।"
[RULE] Past perfect conditional

---

[Q1361] The new policy aims to [1] the use of single use plastics.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reduce
(b) reduced
(c) reducing
(d) reduction
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The infinitive verb after 'to' is 'reduce'.
उदाहरण: "नई नीति एक बार इस्तेमाल होने वाले प्लास्टिक के उपयोग को कम करने का लक्ष्य रखती है।"
[RULE] Infinitive after 'to'

[Q1362] By the time he arrived, the concert had already [1].

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) started
(b) start
(c) starting
(d) starteded
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past perfect requires the past participle; here 'started' is correct.
उदाहरण: "जब वह पहुँचा, कॉन्सर्ट पहले ही शुरू हो चुका था।"
[RULE] Past perfect tense

[Q1363] The scientist noted that the results were inconclusive, so he [1] the experiment.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) repeated
(b) repeat
(c) repeating
(d) repeateded
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Simple past tense: 'repeated'.

[RULE] Simple past tense

---

[Q1364] They cannot afford to [1] on such high interest rates.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) borrow
(b) borrowing
(c) borrowed
(d) borrower
[ANS] b
[SOL]
After 'to', the gerund form is used: 'borrowing'.

[RULE] Gerund after 'to'

[Q1365] Either the manager or the employees [1] responsible for the delay.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) is
(b) are
(c) was
(d) were
[ANS] a

Subject-verb agreement with 'either...or' takes singular verb: 'is'.

[RULE] Subject-verb agreement with either/or

(a) discovered
(b) discover
(c) discovering
(d) discovery
[ANS] a

Past participle after 'was' in passive voice: 'discovered'.

[RULE] Past participle in passive voice

[Q1406] By the time the meeting started, all the necessary documents ___ already prepared.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) were
(b) was
(c) have been
(d) had been
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Past perfect tense indicates completion before another past event.
"Had been" भूतकाल की पूरी हुई क्रिया को दर्शाता है जो दूसरी भूतकालीन घटना से पहले हुई।
[RULE] Past Perfect Tense

(a) will have
(b) will has
(c) have
(d) has
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Future perfect construction: "will have access".
भविष्यकाली पूर्ण वाक्य में "will have" का प्रयोग होता है।

[Q1408] The manager asked the employees to ___ their reports by Friday, so that the project could stay on schedule.

(a) submiting
(b) submit
(c) submitted
(d) submitting
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Infinitive form after "to" following a verb of request.
"to" के बाद infinitive "submitting" का उपयोग होता है।

[Q1409] The old library, which has been standing since 1923, is still (1) a symbol of knowledge for the town.

We use the adjective without an article because it is an attributive adjective describing the noun.

(a) opened

(d) to open

(a) must

The phrase ‘by next week’ indicates the deadline.

(a) took

Past simple is used for a completed action in the past.

(a) ignored

(d) impossibility

(a) rejected

[Q1420] The young sailor, who had spent his entire life on the sea, finally decided to retire after a long and eventful career. He had spent years navigating the unpredictable currents and we at her and now he was ready to settle down in a small coastal town. He believed that the calm of the _______ shore would be a perfect antidote to his rest less spirit.

(a) peaceful
(b) bustling
(c) stormy
(d) deserted
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The word *peaceful* is chosen because it best conveys the calm and serene environment that contrasts with the sailor's previous life of turbulence.
उदाहरण: शांतिपूर्ण तट पर रहकर वह अपने बहादुर मन को शांति दे सकेगा।

[Q1421] The ancient manuscript, which had been preserved in a climate‑controlled vault, was finally opened by the scholars. They discovered that the text was not only a historical record but also a valuable source of _______ for modern linguists.

(a) insight

[SOL]
The most suitable word is *knowledge* as it reflects the scholarly value of the manuscript for linguists.

(a) has

[Q1451] When planning a trip to a foreign country, it is essential to keep a copy of your passport (1) a safe place. Many travelers forget to do this and later encounter problems at the border. The embassy can issue a replacement passport, but the process (2) time-consuming and expensive.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Rule in English: The preposition "at" is used with time expressions. 
उदा. The process is time-consuming.
[RULE] Preposition Usage

[Q1452] During the summer months, the temperature in the desert can reach as high as (1) 50°C. Despite the scorching heat, many animals have adapted to survive by (2) water efficiently and seeking shade during the hottest part of the day.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) 50
(b) 55
(c) 45
(d) 60
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: The comparative form 'higher' is used for temperature. 
उदा. The temperature is higher.
[RULE] Comparatives

[Q1453] The new policy aims to reduce the use of single use plastics. Companies that comply with the guidelines will receive a tax incentive (1) 10% on their annual profit. This incentive is expected to encourage more businesses to adopt eco-friendly practices.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) to
(c) for
(d) of
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Rule in English: The preposition "of" is used after a percentage. 
उदा. A tax incentive of 10%.
[RULE] Preposition with Percentages

[Q1454] The team presented their findings (1) the conference last week. They had conducted extensive research over the past year and were eager to share their results with the scientific community.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) in
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]

[Q1455] If you (1) the instructions carefully, you can avoid many common mistakes when assembling the furniture. The manual provides step by step illustrations that help the user understand each part of the process.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) follow
(b) follows
(c) following
(d) followed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: The base form of the verb follows the modal verb "if".

[Q1456] The river (1) the village for centuries, providing water for irrigation and a source of fish for the local population. In recent years, pollution has threatened the river's ecosystem, prompting community leaders to take action.

(a) nourished
(b) nourishing
(c) nourishes
(d) to nourish

(a) at
(b) in
(c) on
(d) to
[ANS] a

(a) by

[ANS] a

The blank requires a numeral indicating the number of years. The correct form is "five".

[Q1464] The scientist conducted a series of experiments and observed that the reaction rate increased ________ (1) temperature.

The blank needs a preposition indicating cause. "With" is the correct preposition.

The blank requires an ordinal number to indicate the century. "Sixth" is correct.

(a) worked

(c) work

[ANS] a

(a) prepared

(d) prepare
[ANS] a

Past simple verb *prepared* is needed after *to* when describing a completed action.

[Q1474] If you (1) this opportunity, you may regret it later.

Conditional clause uses simple present *miss* to express a general or future possibility.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) written

(c) writes
(d) wrote
[ANS] a

Passive voice uses past participle *written* after *was*.

[Q1476] He has already finished his assignment, and now he (1) to relax.

(a) decided

(d) decidedly

Past simple *decided* follows *has finished* to indicate a completed decision.

(a) full

Adjective *filled* is used with *with* to describe the state of being.

[Q1479] The scientist (1) a new method to detect the virus.

(a) developed

(c) developing
(d) develops
[ANS] a

Past simple *developed* is used after *the scientist* to indicate a completed invention.

(a) submit

(d) submits

(a) prompted
(b) prompting

(d) prompt

(c) review
(d) reviewedly
[ANS] a

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) captured

(c) capturing
(d) capturedly
[ANS] a

In the third conditional, the verb in the main clause is in the past participle form. Hence ‘captured’ is correct.

[ANS] a

The correct prepositional phrase is ‘with great speed’ where ‘speed’ is an uncountable noun. The other options misuse plural or adjective forms.

[Q1485] The new policy will be implemented next month, which is expected to _______ the overall efficiency. 1. ___

(a) improve
(b) improved
(c) improving
(d) improvingly
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Here the verb should be in the gerund form ‘improving’ because it follows ‘to’ in the infinitival phrase ‘to improve’. The correct structure is ‘to improve’ but the blank must contain the gerund form ‘improving’.

(a) found
(b) founding

[ANS] a

The past participle ‘found’ correctly describes the data as having been identified as critical. ‘Founding’ is a noun meaning origin, not suitable here.

(a) bolster

[ANS] a

The infinitive ‘bolster’ follows ‘will’. ‘Bolstering’ would need a different construction.

[Q1495] The students were excited (1) the announcement.

[SOL]
The idiomatic phrase is "known for".
उदाहरण: शेफ अपनी तीखी सॉस के लिए मशहूर है।
[RULE] Idiomatic preposition phrase

---

[Q1497] I will keep you (1) any changes.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) out
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The idiom "keep you in the loop" uses "in".
उदाहरण: मैं आपको किसी भी बदलाव के बारे में बताऊँगा।
[RULE] Idiomatic preposition phrase

---

[Q1498] The museum opens (1) 9 AM.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) at
(c) on
(d) in
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use "at" for a specific time.
उदाहरण: संग्रहालय 9 बजे खुलता है।
[RULE] Preposition with time

---

[Q1499] The children were playing (1) the park.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use "in" to refer to an enclosed area.
उदाहरण: बच्चे पार्क में खेल रहे थे।
[RULE] Preposition with location

---

[Q1500] We need to finish this task (1) tomorrow.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) at
(c) on
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use "by" to denote a deadline before a particular time.
उदाहरण: हमें कल तक यह कार्य पूरा करना है।
[RULE] Preposition with deadline

---

[Q1501] The old bridge, which had stood over the river for more than a century, was finally demolished in 2024. The demolition was carried out by a team of engineers who were __________ the structural integrity of the new bridge that would replace it.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) monitoring
(b) monitored
(c) to monitor
(d) monitor
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The past participle form is required after the past tense verb "were" to form the passive voice. 
उदाहरण: "They were monitoring the progress."
[RULE] Past Participle in Passive Voice

---

[Q1502] When she entered the room, she noticed that the lights were not __________; they had all been switched off for a power outage last night.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) working
(b) worked
(c) work
(d) to work
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective form "working" is used to describe the state of the lights. 
उदाहरण: "The lights are working properly."
[RULE] Adjective Form

---

[Q1503] The committee has decided that the new policy will be implemented by the end of the year, provided that all the necessary approvals are __________.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) received
(b) receiving
(c) to receive
(d) receive
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The past participle "received" follows the phrase "are" in a passive construction. 
उदाहरण: "All documents are received before submission."
[RULE] Past Participle in Passive Construction

---

[Q1504] He could not attend the meeting because he had already __________ a commitment with his cousin on that day.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) made
(b) make
(c) to make
(d) making
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The past tense "made" is used after "had already" to indicate a completed action. 
उदाहरण: "I had already finished the report."
[RULE] Past Simple Tense after Past Perfect

---

[Q1505] The new software update, which is expected to improve performance, will be released next week; it should __________ to all users automatically.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) be installed
(b) install
(c) installs
(d) installed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Be installed" is the correct passive form after the modal "will". 
उदाहरण: "The app will be updated automatically."
[RULE] Passive Voice with Modal Verbs

---

[Q1506] The children were excited to see the fireworks, but they were disappointed when the show was __________ by the unexpected rain.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) canceled
(b) cancelled
(c) to cancel
(d) cancelling
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Canceled" is the correct past participle following "was" in passive voice. 
उदाहरण: "The event was canceled last minute."
[RULE] Past Participle in Passive Voice

---

[Q1507] She has been working here for five years, and she feels that her experience __________ her to handle more responsibilities.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) has equipped
(b) equiped
(c) equipped
(d) equipping
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Has equipped" is the correct present perfect form after "has been working". 
उदाहरण: "Her training has equipped her well."
[RULE] Present Perfect with Past Participle

---

[Q1508] The novel, which was written in 1982, has become __________ of the literary classics of the 20th century.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) one of the most important
(b) most important
(c) most important of
(d) important most
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"One of the most important" is the correct idiomatic expression. 
उदाहरण: "She is one of the most talented artists."
[RULE] Idiomatic Expression

---

[Q1509] The team was nervous about the upcoming present at i on; however, their confidence grew when they realized the audience was __________ to understand the technical details.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) willing
(b) will
(c) will to
(d) to willing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Willing" is the adjective describing the audience. 
उदाहरण: "They were willing to learn."
[RULE] Adjective Usage

---

[Q1510] The city council has decided to renovate the old library; the plan will __________ the community's access to knowledge.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) expand
(b) expands
(c) expanded
(d) expanding
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Expand" is the infinitive form required after "will". 
उदाहरण: "The project will expand its reach."
[RULE] Infinitive after Modal Verb

---

[Q1511] The new policy will [1] the product i on of renewable energy in the country.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) implement
(b) implemented
(c) implementation
(d) implementing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After a modal verb like *will*, the base form of the verb is used. Example: *She will arrive tomorrow.*
उदाहरण: *वह कल आएगी।*
[RULE] Base verb after modal *will*

---

[Q1512] She had never seen such a magnificent [1] before.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) mountain
(b) mountains
(c) mountainous
(d) mountainousness
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When *such a* is used, the following noun is singular. Example: *such a day*. 
उदाहरण: *ऐसा दिन*।
[RULE] Singular noun after *such a*

---

[Q1513] The committee had decided to postpone the meeting, but the [1] was still non‑stop.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) opinion
(b) opinions
(c) opinionally
(d) opinioned
[ANS] b
[SOL]
A plural noun is required after *the* when there are many opinions. Example: *the opinions of the members*. 
उदाहरण: *सदस्यों की राय*।
[RULE] Plural noun after *the*

---

[Q1514] He will be [1] by the time we arrive.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) arrived
(b) arrives
(c) arriving
(d) arrival
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The present participle (ending in *-ing*) is used after a modal verb to indicate an action that will be in progress. Example: *She will be arriving at 5 pm.* 
उदाहरण: *वह 5 बजे आ रही होगी।*
[RULE] Present participle after modal *will* for future continuous

---

[Q1515] It is not only the teacher who [1] the students.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) instructs
(b) instructed
(c) instructing
(d) instruction
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After *not only*, the verb remains in the simple present tense. Example: *It is not only the manager but also the secretary who works hard.* 
उदाहरण: *यह न सिर्फ प्रबंधक बल्कि सचिव भी मेहनत करता है।*
[RULE] Simple present after *not only*

---

[Q1516] The [1] of the city was breathtaking.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) scenery
(b) scenaries
(c) scenery's
(d) scenary
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The word *scenery* is a singular noun used to describe the view. Example: *The scenery was beautiful.* 
उदाहरण: *दृश्य सुंदर था।*
[RULE] Singular noun for landscape *scenery*

---

[Q1517] They [1] to the market every Sunday.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) go
(b) went
(c) going
(d) gone
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Simple present tense is used for habitual actions. Example: *I go to school daily.* 
उदाहरण: *मैं रोज स्कूल जाता हूँ।*
[RULE] Simple present for habitual actions

---

[Q1518] The [1] of the letter was sealed with wax.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) envelope
(b) envelopes
(c) envelop
(d) enveloped
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When referring to a single envelope, the singular noun *envelope* is used. Example: *The envelope was mailed.* 
उदाहरण: *पत्र को लिफ़ाफ़े में भेजा गया।*
[RULE] Singular noun *envelope*

---

[Q1519] The book, which was written by a [1] author, has won several awards.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) renowned
(b) renowns
(c) renown
(d) renownedly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Adjectives like *renowned* are used to describe a person. Example: *She is a renowned scientist.* 
उदाहरण: *वह एक प्रसिद्ध वैज्ञानिक है।*
[RULE] Adjective *renowned* to describe a person

---

[Q1520] She has always believed that [1] is the key to success.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) hardwork
(b) hard work
(c) hardworking
(d) hardworks
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The noun phrase *hard work* (two words) is correct. Example: *Hard work pays off.* 
उदाहरण: *कड़ी मेहनत सफल होती है।*
[RULE] Noun phrase *hard work*

---

[Q1521] The committee has decided to ___ (1) the proposal after careful consideration.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) approve
(b) approved
(c) approving
(d) approves
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The infinitive form "approve" follows the marker "to". Rule: Use the base form of the verb after "to" following verbs like "decide".
उदाहरण: "He decided to go."
[RULE] Infinitive after verb

---

[Q1522] She has been working at the firm ___ (1) five years.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) during
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"For" is used with a duration. Rule: Use "for" with a period of time.
उदाहरण: "I have lived here for ten years."
[RULE] Preposition for duration

---

[Q1523] Neither the manager nor the employees ___ (1) responsible for the delay.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) are
(b) is
(c) were
(d) be
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When a singular noun follows "neither...nor", the verb agrees with the nearer noun. Here "employees" is plural, but the subject is singular "manager". Rule: Subject-verb agreement with neither/nor.
उदाहरण: "Neither the teacher nor the students are here."
[RULE] Subject-verb agreement with neither/nor

---

[Q1524] The dog, which was ___ (1) in the yard, ran towards the park.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) sleeping
(b) slept
(c) sleeps
(d) sleep
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Sleeping" is a present participle used after a relative pronoun to describe an ongoing action. Rule: Use present participle after "which was".
उदाहरण: "The car, which was parked, was moved."
[RULE] Present participle in relative clause

---

[Q1525] If he ___ (1) earlier, he could have avoided the traffic.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had left
(b) left
(c) had leaving
(d) leaves
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Had left" is the past perfect form required for the third conditional. Rule: Use past perfect after "if" in a past unreal condition.
उदाहरण: "If she had studied, she would have passed."
[RULE] Third conditional – past perfect

---

[Q1526] The new policy will ___ (1) the company's efficiency by 20% next year.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) increase
(b) increased
(c) increasing
(d) to increase
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Increase" is the base form used after the modal "will". Rule: Use base verb after modal verbs.
उदाहरण: "She will go to the market."
[RULE] Modal verb + base form

---

[Q1527] The library is located ___ (1) the corner of Maple and Oak streets.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) on
(c) in
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"At" is used for a specific point or location. Rule: Use "at" for a corner or intersection.
उदाहरण: "Meet me at the station."
[RULE] Preposition for specific location

---

[Q1528] She has been ___ (1) to the museum every Sunday since she was a child.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) going
(b) go
(c) went
(d) gone
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Going" is the present participle used after "been" in a present perfect continuous construction. Rule: Use "been + present participle" for ongoing actions.
उदाहरण: "He has been studying for two hours."
[RULE] Present perfect continuous – been + present participle

[Q1529] The students will meet ___ (1) the auditorium at 10:00 AM.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) to
[ANS] c
[SOL]
"At" is used for a specific time point. Rule: Use "at" for exact times.
उदाहरण: "The meeting starts at 9 AM."
[RULE] Preposition for specific time

---

[Q1530] He is the only person ___ (1) can solve this problem.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) who
(b) whom
(c) whose
(d) which
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Who" is used as the subject of the relative clause. Rule: Use "who" for people as the subject.
उदाहरण: "I know the man who won the award."
[RULE] Relative pronoun for subject

[Q1531] The children were playing in the garden when suddenly a thunderstorm started to [1].

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) fall
(b) falling
(c) fell
(d) falling down
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use the present participle after a verb to indicate a continuous action.
उदा. हिंदी: बच्चे खेल रहे थे जब अचानक तूफ़ान गिरे।
[RULE] Present participle for continuous action

---

[Q1532] She has been studying for three hours, and she feels that her [1] is starting to fade.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) memory
(b) memorise
(c) memories
(d) memorising
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use the noun form after a verb when describing a state.
उदा. हिंदी: वह तीन घंटे से पढ़ रही है, और उसे लगता है कि उसकी स्मृति धुंधली हो रही है।
[RULE] Noun usage after verb

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a lot

(c) lots
(d) lots of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use the quantifier phrase 'a lot' with verbs.
उदा. हिंदी: अगर आप अंग्रेज़ी में सुधार करना चाहते हैं, तो आपको हर दिन बहुत अभ्यास करना चाहिए।
[RULE] Quantifier usage with verbs

---

[Q1534] The mayor announced that the new park would be opened on Sunday, but the [1] was postponed.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) opening
(b) opened
(c) opening up
(d) opened up
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use the noun form of a verb for events.
उदा. हिंदी: मेयर ने घोषणा की कि नया पार्क रविवार को खुला जाएगा, लेकिन उद्घाटन स्थगित कर दिया गया।
[RULE] Noun form of verb for events

[Q1535] He will be studying at the library [1] the exam dates are announced.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) until
(b) when
(c) as soon as
(d) by
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use 'as soon as' to indicate a time in the future.
उदा. हिंदी: जब ही परीक्षा की तिथियाँ घोषित होंगी, वह पुस्तकालय में पढ़ रहा होगा।
[RULE] Future time clauses with 'as soon as'

[Q1536] The old bridge, which had been built in 1950, was [1] in 2015 to make it safer.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) renovated

[ANS] a

Rule in English: Use the past participle of a verb for passive construction.
उदा. हिंदी: 1950 में बना पुराना पुल 2015 में सुरक्षित बनाने के लिए पुनर्निर्मित किया गया।

[Q1537] I am not sure which route to take, but I think the train will arrive [1] the city.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) in
(c) on
(d) to
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use 'in' after 'arrive' to indicate a location.
उदा. हिंदी: मैं नहीं जानता कि कौन सा रास्ता लेना है, लेकिन मुझे लगता है कि ट्रेन शहर में पहुँच जाएगी।
[RULE] Preposition after 'arrive'

---

[Q1538] She was the most [1] student in her class, earning top marks in every subject.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) intelligent

(c) intelligence
(d) Eloquent
[ANS] a

Rule in English: Use adjective form after 'most' for superlatives.

[Q1539] The committee will meet tomorrow to discuss the new project, but the final decision will be made [1].

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) later
(b) later on
(c) later so
(d) later in
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use 'later on' to refer to a future time.

[Q1540] The museum contains thousands of artifacts, many of which were [1] in the 19th century.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) collected
(b) collect
(c) collecting
(d) collectedly
[ANS] a

Rule in English: Use the past participle after 'were' for passive structure.

[Q1541] You should not forget to [1] the door before you leave.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) lock
(b) locking
(c) locked
(d) locks
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use base verb after modal 'should' for obligation.

[Q1542] The scientist explained that the experiment was successful because the catalyst [1] the reaction.

(a) accelerated
(b) accelerating
(c) accelerate
(d) acceleratedly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use past tense verb to describe completed action.

[Q1543] Every morning, I wake up at 6 a.m. and [1] a cup of tea before starting my day.

(a) bake
(b) drink
(c) my
(d) cook
[ANS] b

The blank requires a verb in simple present to indicate a habitual action.

[RULE] Verb in Simple Present

[Q1544] She has been [1] for 3 years and is now an expert in photography.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) working
(b) worked
(c) work
(d) workings
[ANS] a

The structure "has been + present participle" expresses an action that started in the past and continues to the present.

[Q1545] The team will finish the project by next week, but they [1] already started the first phase.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had
(b) has
(c) have
(d) having
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The past perfect is needed to show an action completed before another past action.
उदाहरण: "They had finished the report before the meeting."
[RULE] Present Perfect

---

[Q1546] It is essential that each student [1] their assignments on time.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) submit
(b) submits
(c) submitting
(d) submitted
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The subjunctive mood uses the base form of the verb after "that" for a necessity.
उदाहरण: "It is important that he be on time."
[RULE] Subjunctive Mood

---

[Q1547] There are few people who can [1] this type of complex problem.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) solve
(b) solving
(c) solved
(d) solves
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After a relative pronoun, the infinitive form is used.
उदाहरण: "She is the only one who can help."
[RULE] Infinitive after Relative Pronoun

---

[Q1548] Despite the rain, the match [1] on.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was held
(b) has been held
(c) is held
(d) will be held
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The past simple tense indicates a completed action in the past.
उदाहरण: "They played yesterday."
[RULE] Past Simple

---

[Q1549] The children were playing in the garden when suddenly it [1].
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) started to rain
(b) started raining
(c) started to rained
(d) started rains
[ANS] b
[SOL]
After "started" we use a gerund to show the action that was in progress.
उदाहरण: "She started dancing."
[RULE] Past Continuous with Gerund

---

[Q1550] If I [1] earlier, I would have caught the bus.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) leave
(b) left
(c) had left
(d) leaving
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Second conditional uses past simple in the if‑clause.
उदाहरण: "If it rained, we would stay indoors."
[RULE] Second Conditional

---

[Q1551] The book, which was published in 1990, has [1] many editions.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) been
(b) been been
(c) Futile
(d) Gregarious
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The past participle "been" is used after "has" to form the present perfect passive.
उदाहरण: "The story has been told many times."
[RULE] Past Participle

[Q1552] I have never seen such a beautiful sunset [1] this city.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition "in" is used for places or locations.
उदाहरण: "She lives in New York."
[RULE] Preposition Usage

[Q1553] The committee members were _______________ to attend the meeting even though they were exhausted.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) prepared
(b) preparing
(c) prepares
(d) preparedly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correct form is the past participle used with the auxiliary verb *were* to form the passive. Rule: Use the past participle after *were* for passive voice. 
उदा. हिंदी: सही उत्तर *prepared* है क्योंकि *were* के साथ भूतकालिक रूप का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Passive Voice – Past Participle
