[Q1554] He has been studying French _______________ he can travel across Europe next summer.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) so that
(b) because
(c) although
(d) unless
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The clause expresses purpose, so *so that* is appropriate. Rule: Use *so that* to indicate purpose. 
उदाहरण: सही उत्तर *so that* है क्योंकि यह उद्देश्य दर्शाता है।
[RULE] Purpose Clauses – So that

---

[Q1555] If you _______________ the dead line you will lose the opportunity to apply.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) miss
(b) missed
(c) misses
(d) missing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The simple present tense is used in the if‑clause of a first conditional. Rule: Use simple present for real future conditions. 
हिंदी: सही उत्तर *miss* है क्योंकि यह शर्तीय वाक्य में वर्तमान काल का प्रयोग है।
[RULE] First Conditional – Simple Present

---

[Q1556] The chef added a pinch of salt, but the dish still tasted _______________.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) bitter
(b) bitterly
(c) bitterness
(d) Impeccable
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Adjective form *bitter* is needed to describe taste. Rule: Use adjectives to modify nouns; adverbs modify verbs. 
उदाहरण: सही उत्तर *bitter* है क्योंकि यह स्वाद का विशेषण है।
[RULE] Adjective vs Adverb

---

[Q1557] She has been working at the firm _______________ five years.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) during
(d) before
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *for* is used with a duration of time. Rule: Use *for* with a period; use *since* with a starting point. 
हिंदी: सही उत्तर *for* है क्योंकि यह अवधि को दर्शाता है।
[RULE] Prepositions of Time – For/Since

---

[Q1558] The students were applauded by the teacher, and the principal _______________ the whole event.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) covered
(b) covering
(c) covers
(d) cove
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past tense *covered* matches the past simple structure. Rule: Use past tense for events in the past. 
उदाहरण: सही उत्तर *covered* है क्योंकि यह भूतकाल में है।
[RULE] Past Simple Tense

---

[Q1559] The novel is so ____________ that it has been translated into ten different languages.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) popular
(b) popularly
(c) popularity
(d) popularness
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Adjective *popular* modifies *novel*. Rule: Use adjectives to describe nouns. 
हिंदी: सही उत्तर *popular* है क्योंकि यह विशेषण है।
[RULE] Adjective Usage

---

[Q1560] By the time the ceremony started, the crowd had _______________ in the hall.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) arrived
(b) arrives
(c) arriving
(d) arriveded
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The perfect aspect *has arrived* fits the sequence of events. Rule: Use perfect tense to indicate an action completed before another. 
उदाहरण: सही उत्तर *arrived* है क्योंकि यह पूर्ण भूतकाल है।
[RULE] Present Perfect

---

[Q1561] If it _______________ tomorrow, the picnic will be postponed.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) rain
(b) rains
(c) rained
(d) raining
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The simple present tense *rains* is used in a second conditional for real future possibilities. Rule: Use simple present in if‑clauses for real future. 
हिंदी: सही उत्तर *rains* है क्योंकि यह शर्तीय वाक्य में वर्तमान काल है।
[RULE] Second Conditional – Simple Present

---

[Q1562] The evidence presented in court was so convincing that the jury _______________.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) convince
(b) convinced
(c) convincing
(d) Skeptical
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The past simple *convinced* is correct. Rule: Use past simple for completed actions. 
उदाहरण: सही उत्तर *convinced* है क्योंकि यह भूतकाल में है।
[RULE] Past Simple Tense

---

[Q1563] The manager asked the employees to submit their reports _______________.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by tomorrow
(b) tomorrow by
(c) by tomorrow's
(d) Eloquent
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correct prepositional phrase is *by tomorrow*. Rule: Use *by* to indicate deadline. 
हिंदी: सही उत्तर *by tomorrow* है क्योंकि यह समय सीमा दर्शाता है।
[RULE] Prepositions of Time – By

[Q1564] During the meeting, the manager emphasized that the new policy would [1] the company's growth.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) hamper
(b) facilitate
(c) neglect
(d) ignore
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The blank requires a verb in the base form following a modal verb 'would'.
उदा. "would facilitate" – मॉडेल के बाद क्रिया का मूल रूप प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Modal verb + Base verb

[Q1565] She was [1] when the news of her promotion reached her.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) excited
(b) exciting
(c) excitement
(d) excites
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The blank needs an adjective to describe her state of mind.

[Q1566] The committee has decided to [1] the proposal by the end of this month.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reviewed
(b) review
(c) reviewing
(d) reviews
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb must be in base form after modal 'to' as part of the infinitive.

[Q1567] He has been working here for ten years, and he will certainly [1] a senior manager soon.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) become
(b) became
(c) becoming
(d) becomes
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Will" followed by base form verb indicates future simple.
उदा. "will become" – भविष्य में क्रिया के लिए मूल रूप।

[Q1568] The data we collected last quarter is likely to [1] a significant trend.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) show
(b) shows
(c) showed
(d) showing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Likely to" requires the base form of the verb.
उदा. "likely to show" – संभावना व्यक्त करने के लिये मूल रूप।
[RULE] Modal + Base verb (likely to)

[Q1569] The research paper, which was published in 2020, has [1] many citations.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) earned
(b) earning
(c) earns
(d) earn
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Has" + past participle forms the present perfect tense.
उदा. "has earned" – वर्तमान पूर्ण काल।
[RULE] Present Perfect Tense

[Q1570] The museum's new exhibit is expected to [1] visitors from around the world.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) attract
(b) attracted
(c) attracting
(d) attracts
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb after 'expected to' is in base form.
उदा. "expected to attract" – अपेक्षा व्यक्त करने के लिये मूल रूप।
[RULE] Modal + Base verb (expected to)

[Q1571] By next year, the company will have [1] a new branch in Bangalore.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) opened
(b) opens
(c) open
(d) opening
[ANS] c
[SOL]
"Will have" + base verb forms the future perfect tense.
उदा. "will have opened" – भविष्य पूर्ण काल के लिये मूल रूप।
[RULE] Future Perfect Tense

[Q1572] The students were asked to [1] the questionnaire before the exam.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) fill
(b) filled
(c) filling
(d) fills
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Were asked to" requires an infinitive in base form.
उदा. "asked to fill" – आदेश वाक्य में मूल रूप।

[Q1573] The ___ (1) of the river has been diverted to create a reservoir.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) bank
(b) banking
(c) banks
(d) banked
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Here we need a noun to refer to the side of the river. "Bank" is a noun, whereas "banking" is a verb and "banked" is a past participle adjective. In Hindi: नदी का ___ (1) बाँध बना दिया गया।

[Q1574] The committee has decided to ___ (1) the policy after the feedback.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) revise
(b) revision
(c) revisting
(d) revised
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb form "revise" is required after "decided to". "Revision" is a noun, "revisting" is not a word, and "revised" is a misspelling. Hindi: समिति ने नीति ___ (1) करने का निर्णय लिया।
[RULE] Verb form after modal/infinitive

[Q1575] She has been working here for five years, during which time she has gained ___ (1) experience.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) rich
(b) richness
(c) richening
(d) richly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective "rich" is needed to describe the type of experience. "Richness" is a noun, "richening" is incorrect, "richly" is an adverb. Hindi: उसने पाँच वर्षों में ___ (1) अनुभव प्राप्त किया।
[RULE] Adjective to describe noun

---

[Q1576] The ___ (1) of the project was delayed due to unforeseen circumstances.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) delivery
(b) delivering
(c) delivered
(d) delivers
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Here a noun is required; "delivery" fits. "Delivering" is a gerund, "delivered" is past participle, "delivers" is a verb. Hindi: परियोजना की ___ (1) में देरी हुई।
[RULE] Noun vs. Gerund/Verb

---

[Q1577] The team was ___ (1) by the sudden announcement of the new manager.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) surprised
(b) surprise
(c) surprising
(d) surpising
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The past participle "surprised" is used after "was" to form a passive. "Surprise" is a noun, "surprising" is not standard, "surpising" is misspelled. Hindi: टीम को नए प्रबंधक की अचानक घोषणा से ___ (1) किया गया।
[RULE] Past participle after "was"

[Q1578] The city’s ___ (1) has increased significantly due to tourism.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) population
(b) populated
(c) populating
(d) populace
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Population" is a noun denoting the number of people; "populated" is a past participle adjective, "populating" is a gerund, and "populace" is a noun but less common. Hindi: शहर की ___ (1) पर्यटन के कारण बढ़ी है।
[RULE] Appropriate noun usage

---

[Q1579] The teacher asked the students to ___ (1) their home work before the dead line
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) submit
(b) submission
(c) submitting
(d) submited
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Submit" is the infinitive form needed after "to". "Submission" is a noun, "submitting" is a gerund, "submited" is misspelled. Hindi: शिक्षक ने छात्रों से ___ (1) होमवर्क जमा करने को कहा।
[RULE] Infinitive after "to"

[Q1580] The machine is ___ (1) efficient after the latest upgrade.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) more
(b) much
(c) most
(d) mostly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"More" is used to compare efficiency between before and after. "Much" is an adverb, "most" is superlative, "mostly" is not a standard word. Hindi: मशीन ___ (1) कुशल है।
[RULE] Comparative adjective usage

---

[Q1581] The doctor advised him to ___ (1) from smoking.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) quit
(b) quits
(c) quitting
(d) quitted
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Quit" is the infinitive required after "to". "Quits" is third‑person singular verb, "quitting" is a gerund, "quitted" is archaic. Hindi: डॉक्टर ने उसे धूम्रपान ___ (1) की सलाह दी।
[RULE] Infinitive after "to"

---

[Q1582] The ___ (1) of the book was praised for its vivid illustrations.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) cover
(b) covered
(c) covering
(d) covers
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Cover" is a noun needed here. "Covered" is past participle adjective, "covering" is gerund, "covers" is verb. Hindi: किताब का ___ (1) चित्रों के लिए प्रशंसा की गई।
[RULE] Noun vs. Verb/Participle

---

[Q1583] The committee had decided to postpone the meeting, but the chair person insisted that it should be held on ___.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) tomorrow
(b) today
(c) yesterday
(d) last week
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The sentence requires a future tense time expression to match the conditional mood. "Tomorrow" fits best.
वाक्य में भविष्य के समय का प्रयोग आवश्यक है, इसलिए "tomorrow" सही विकल्प है।
[RULE] Future Tense with Time Expression

[Q1584] She will have finished the report by the time we ___ there.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) arrive
(b) arrived
(c) arrives
(d) arriving
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The future perfect tense is used with a time clause introduced by "by the time". The verb in the time clause should be in the base form: "arrive".
भविष्य पूर्ण काल के साथ "by the time" वाले वाक्य में क्रिया मूल रूप में होनी चाहिए: "arrive".

[Q1585] The book, ___ on the shelf, has been read by many students.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) which
(b) who
(c) that
(d) whom
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The relative pronoun refers to an inanimate object (the book), so "which" is appropriate.
संज्ञा (पुस्तक) के लिए "which" सही है, "who" या "whom" नहीं।

[Q1586] The results indicate that the new policy has made the process ___ efficient.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) more
(b) most
(c) much
(d) muchly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Comparatives are formed with "more" + adjective. "More efficient" is correct.

[Q1587] I would have gone to the party, ___ I had known about it earlier.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) as
(c) because
(d) though
[ANS] c
[SOL]
A past unreal conditional clause uses "because" to explain the reason for the past hypothetical action.
पिछले काल की अनिश्चित शर्त में कारण के लिये "because" प्रयोग होता है।

[Q1588] The students were praised ___ they had completed the assignment on time.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) because
(c) although
(d) if
[ANS] b
[SOL]
"Because" introduces the reason for the praise, matching the structure "were praised because they had completed".
कृतज्ञता का कारण बताने के लिये "because" सही है।

[Q1589] He can ___ to the meeting if he finishes his work.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) attend

(d) attends

[Q1590] The river, ___ had flooded last year, is now dry.

(a) that
(b) which

(d) whom

The non‑restrictive relative clause uses "which" for an inanimate noun (river).

[Q1591] It is essential that the manager ___ the report before the dead line

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) submit
(b) submitted
(c) submits
(d) submitting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The subjunctive mood after "essential that" requires the base form: "submit".
सुब्ज़ंक्तिव मोड में "essential that" के बाद मूल रूप "submit" प्रयोग होता है।

[Q1592] The children, ___ were playing outside, were asked to come inside.

(a) who
(b) which
(c) whom
(d) that
[ANS] a

[Q1593] During the 18th century, the __________ of the British Empire was a major factor in the global trade network.

(a) expansion
(b) collapse
(c) curtailment
(d) abandonment
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correct word is a noun that fits the context of growth.

[Q1603] She has been working at the company for 10 years and has [1] many projects completed. Despite the challenges, she has always maintained a positive attitude. Her colleagues often say she is the most reliable employee in the department, which is why she was [2] for promotion.

(a) a) a number of
(b) b) many

(d) d) much
[ANS] a

Use 'a number of' with countable nouns in plural.

[Q1604] If you want to improve your English, you should read books and [1] watch movies in English. The more you expose your self to the language, the better your listening and speaking skills will become. Even small daily practice can lead to significant progress over time.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a) also
(b) b) too
(c) c) as well
(d) d) in addition
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Both 'also' and 'too' are acceptable, but 'as well' is more formal and fits the context better. 
उदाहरण: "I will go as well."
[RULE] Adverbs of addition

[Q1605] The new policy will be effective from next month. Employees who [1] the guidelines will receive a warning; those who fail to comply will be [2] from the company. The management hopes to maintain a fair and transparent process.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a) follow
(b) b) following
(c) c) follows
(d) d) followed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use base form after 'who' in relative clauses. 
उदाहरण: "People who work hard succeed."
[RULE] Relative clauses with base form

[Q1606] By the time the conference ends, we will have completed all the sessions and will have [1] enough time to evaluate the outcomes. It is essential that we keep track of every detail to ensure success.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a) have
(b) b) will have
(c) c) had
(d) d) have had
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In a future perfect tense, use 'will have' in main clause and 'have' in subordinate clause. 
उदाहरण: "By next year, I will have finished the project."

[Q1607] The scientist explained that the results were inconclusive, so the research team decided to [1] the study for a longer duration. This approach would help gather more data and clarify the findings.

(a) a) extend
(b) b) extended
(c) c) extending
(d) d) extends
[ANS] a

[Q1608] The committee will consider the proposal, but if it is rejected, the organization will [1] to search for alternative solutions. They intend to maintain momentum in the project.

[Q1609] She had been volunteering at the shelter for three years, but the recent policy changes [1] her to stop. Despite the set back she plans to find another way to contribute to the community.

(a) a) forced
(b) b) forced to
(c) c) forcing
(d) d) force

[Q1611] The manager praised the team's efforts, saying that the project was completed ahead of schedule, which [1] the company to gain a competitive edge in the industry.

(a) a) has given

[Q1612] The teacher advised the students that if they study diligently, they [1] pass the exam with flying colors. She emphasized the importance of consistent practice.

(a) a) will

[ANS] a

'Will' is used for a future prediction after 'if'.

(a) has stood

(a) will discuss

(c) discuss

[Q1626] If he (1) ____ earlier, the train would have arrived on time.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had left
(b) leaves
(c) leave
(d) leaving
[ANS] a
[SOL]

उदाहरण: अगर वह पहले निकल गया होता, तो ट्रेन समय पर पहुँच जाती।

(a) analyzed

(c) analysis
(d) Pensive
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The main verb of the sentence is in simple past tense. The correct past tense of *analyze* is "analyzed".
उदाहरण: वैज्ञानिकों ने परिणामों का विश्लेषण किया।

(a) continues
(b) continued
(c) continue

[ANS] b

The clause uses a past tense verb after a past‑time adverbial phrase. Hence "continued" is the correct form.
उदाहरण: भारी बारिश के बावजूद, मैराथन बिना किसी रुकावट के जारी रहा।
[RULE] Simple Past – Past Time Context

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) started

(c) starts
(d) starting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The narrative is in simple past tense. The correct past form of *start* is "started".
उदाहरण: उसने 2005 में अपना करियर शुरू किया और तब से काम कर रही है।

[Q1630] By the time the guests arrived, the chef (1) ____ the main course.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had prepared
(b) prepares
(c) preparing
(d) prepare
[ANS] a
[SOL]
This is a past perfect construction because the action happened before another past event (guests arriving). Hence "had prepared".
उदाहरण: अतिथियों के आने से पहले, शेफ ने मुख्य व्यंजन तैयार कर लिया था।

[Q1631] The book, which was written by a renowned author, (1) ____ in the top 10 bestseller list.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) remains
(b) remain
(c) remained
(d) remaining
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The relative clause describes a present state, so the verb must be in the present simple form "remains".
उदाहरण: यह पुस्तक सबसे अधिक बिकने वाली पुस्तकों की सूची में बनी हुई है।

[Q1632] If you (1) ____ the instructions carefully, you will not make any mistake.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) follow
(b) following
(c) follows
(d) followed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In a zero conditional, the verb in the if‑clause is in the simple present. Hence "follow".
उदाहरण: यदि आप निर्देशों का ध्यानपूर्वक पालन करते हैं, तो कोई गलती नहीं होगी।
[RULE] Zero Conditional – Simple Present

---

[Q1633] The students (1) ____ the assignment by the due date.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) completed
(b) complete
(c) completes
(d) completing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The main clause is in simple past tense. Hence "completed" is correct.
उदाहरण: छात्रों ने समय पर असाइनमेंट पूरा किया।
[RULE] Simple Past Tense – Regular Verb

[Q1634] The manager (1) ____ the project on schedule.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) completed
(b) complete
(c) completes
(d) completing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The sentence refers to a finished action in the past. The simple past form "completed" is appropriate.
उदाहरण: प्रबंधक ने परियोजना समय पर पूर्ण की।

[Q1635] The committee has decided to ________ the new policy.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) implement
(b) implementing
(c) implementation
(d) implemented
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: After the phrase 'has decided to', the base form of the verb is required.

[Q1636] He was known for his ________ skills, which helped the team win.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) leadership
(b) leading
(c) leader
(d) lead
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: The phrase 'for his' is followed by a noun.

[RULE] Noun usage after 'for his'

[Q1637] The museum, along with the gallery, ________ open to the public.

(a) is

[SOL]

उदा. Hindi explanation: विषय एकवचन है, इसलिए 'is' का उपयोग करें।

[Q1638] Not only did she finish the report, but she also ______ to the committee.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) presented
(b) present
(c) presenting
(d) to present
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: After 'did', the base form of the verb is used for the past simple.

[Q1639] The students who were absent ________ given extra time to complete the exam.

(a) was
(b) were

(d) had
[ANS] b
[SOL]

[Q1640] If the rain stops, we will ______ the picnic.

(a) cancel

(d) cancelling
[ANS] a

[Q1641] She has always been a person who ______ himself.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]

[ANS] a

[Q1642] The book, which was written by a famous author, _______ a bestseller.

(d) become
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: The present perfect construction uses 'has been' + past participle.
उदा. Hindi explanation: वर्तमान पूर्ण काल में 'has been' + past participle। उदाहरण: वह एक बेस्टसेलर रहा है।
[RULE] Present Perfect Tense with 'has been'

---

[Q1643] By the time the train arrived, the passengers ________ already left.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had
(b) has
(c) have
(d) having
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Past perfect is formed with 'had' + past participle to indicate an action completed before another past event.
उदा. Hindi explanation: भूतकाल पूर्ण काल 'had' से बनता है। उदाहरण: वे पहले ही निकल चुके थे।
[RULE] Past Perfect Tense

---

[Q1644] The new policy will ________ the number of employees who work over time.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reduce
(b) reduces
(c) reducing
(d) reduced
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: After 'will', the base form of the verb is used for future simple.
उदा. Hindi explanation: will के बाद मूल क्रिया। उदाहरण: नीति कमी करेगी।
[RULE] Future Simple Verb Form after 'will'

---

[Q1645] The committee [1] ______ decided to postpone the meeting.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) has
(b) have
(c) were
(d) was
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Subject‑verb agreement: In the present simple tense, a singular subject such as ‘committee’ requires a singular verb form. Example: वह स्कूल जाता है।
[RULE] Subject‑Verb Agreement (Singular)

---

[Q1646] He was born ______ 1990.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Preposition for years: Use ‘in’ for years. Example: वह 1990 में पैदा हुआ।
[RULE] Prepositions – Year

---

[Q1647] She adopted ______ dog from the shelter.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a
(b) an
(c) the
(d) no
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Indefinite article before a consonant sound: Use ‘a’. Example: उसने एक कुत्ता गोद लिया।
[RULE] Indefinite Article – a/an

---

[Q1648] You ______ wear a seat belt while driving.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) must
(b) may
(c) might
(d) could
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Modal verb for obligation: ‘must’ expresses a strong requirement. Example: आपको ड्राइव करते समय सीट बेल्ट लगाना चाहिए।
[RULE] Modal Verb – Must

---

[Q1649] Her cooking is ______ than his.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) better
(b) good
(c) best
(d) more good
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Comparative form of ‘good’: use ‘better’. Example: उसकी खाना उसकी तुलना में बेहतर है।
[RULE] Comparatives – Good/Better/Best

---

[Q1650] Both Rahul and Sita brought ______ books.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) their
(b) his
(c) her
(d) ours
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Pronoun for two people: use ‘their’. Example: राहुल और सीता दोनों ने अपने किताबें लाई।
[RULE] Possessive Pronouns – Their

---

[Q1651] He bought a ______ house.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) new beautiful red
(b) beautiful new red
(c) red beautiful new
(d) red new beautiful
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Adjective order: size, age, color, origin, purpose. Correct order: new, beautiful, red. Example: उसने एक नया सुंदर लाल घर खरीदा।
[RULE] Adjective Order

---

[Q1652] The new policy ______ implemented next month.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) will be
(b) has
(c) is
(d) were
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Future passive construction: ‘will be’ + past participle. Example: नई नीति अगले महीने लागू की जाएगी।
[RULE] Future Passive Voice

---

[Q1653] The manager asked for an ______ of the report.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) explanation
(b) explanations
(c) explain
(d) explanatory
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Singular noun required after ‘an’. Example: प्रबंधक ने रिपोर्ट का एक स्पष्टीकरण मांगा।
[RULE] Singular Noun after ‘an’

---

[Q1654] He studied hard, ______ he passed the exam.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) and
(b) but
(c) or
(d) so
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Contrasting conjunction: ‘but’ shows contrast between effort and result. Example: उसने कड़ी मेहनत की, लेकिन परीक्षा पास नहीं हुई।
[RULE] Conjunction – But

---

[Q1655] She ______ to the market yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) go
(b) went
(c) gone
(d) going
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Past Simple Tense is used to describe a completed action in the past.
उदाहरण: वह कल बाज़ार गई।
[RULE] Past Simple Tense

---

[Q1656] If I ______ you, I would not do that.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) am
(b) was
(c) were
(d) be
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Subjunctive mood uses ‘were’ in hypothetical statements.
उदाहरण: अगर मैं तुम्हारे स्थान पर होता, तो मैं ऐसा नहीं करता।
[RULE] Subjunctive Mood

---

[Q1657] The cake, along with the cookies, ______ on the table.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) is
(b) are
(c) were
(d) been
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Subject-verb agreement: the singular noun ‘cake’ requires the singular verb ‘is’.
उदाहरण: केक पर कुकीज़ के साथ, वह मेज़ पर है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1658] It is important that he ______ to the meeting on time.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) arrives
(b) arrive
(c) arrived
(d) arriving
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Subjunctive mood after ‘It is important that’ requires base form.
उदाहरण: यह ज़रूरी है कि वह समय पर बैठक में पहुँचे।
[RULE] Subjunctive Mood

---

[Q1659] Neither the manager nor the employees ______ willing to compromise.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) are
(d) is
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When the subject is a compound, use plural verb with the subject nearest the verb.
उदाहरण: न तो प्रबंधक और न ही कर्मचारी समझौता करने के लिए तैयार हैं।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement with Compound Subjects

---

[Q1660] She has been studying for two hours, ______ she still feels tired.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) however
(b) although
(c) but
(d) yet
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Conjunction ‘yet’ is used to show contrast.
उदाहरण: वह दो घंटे से पढ़ रही है, फिर भी वह थकी हुई महसूस करती है।
[RULE] Conjunctions for Contrast

---

[Q1661] The book, which was written by a famous author, ______ on the bestseller list.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) is
(b) was
(c) has
(d) were
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Present simple is used for facts that are always true.
उदाहरण: वह पुस्तक, जिसे एक प्रसिद्ध लेखक ने लिखा, बेस्टसेलर सूची पर है।
[RULE] Present Simple for Facts

---

[Q1662] He had already left when I ______ him.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) saw
(b) see
(c) seeing
(d) seen
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past simple after a past perfect clause.
उदाहरण: वह पहले ही चला गया था जब मैंने उसे देखा।
[RULE] Past Simple after Past Perfect

---

[Q1663] They will finish the project by tomorrow, ______?
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) right?
(b) correctly?
(c) exactly?
(d) already?
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Tag question for a future statement uses “right?”.
उदाहरण: वे परियोजना कल तक समाप्त करेंगे, सही है?
[RULE] Tag Questions with Future

---

[Q1664] The students were asked to submit the report ______ a week.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) by
(d) for
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Preposition ‘by’ indicates deadline.
उदाहरण: छात्रों से रिपोर्ट एक हफ़्ते के भीतर जमा करने को कहा गया।
[RULE] Prepositions for Deadlines

---

[Q1665] During the annual conference, the chair man emphasized that the company's ___ (1) would be reviewed in the next quarter.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) performance
(b) performances
(c) perfomance
(d) perform
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The possessive noun "the company's" requires a singular noun to describe its attribute. Hence, ‘performance’ is the correct choice.

उदाहरण: कंपनी का प्रदर्शन।
[RULE] Singular noun usage after possessive

---

[Q1666] The new policy will ___ (1) employees who work over time.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reward
(b) rewards
(c) rewarded
(d) rewarding
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After the modal verb ‘will’, the base form of the verb is used to express a future action. Therefore, ‘reward’ is correct.

उदाहरण: वह कर्मचारियों को पुरस्कृत करेगा।
[RULE] Infinitive after modal 'will'

---

[Q1667] She has been working here ___ (1) since 2010.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) long
(b) longer
(c) longest
(d) longer than
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adverb ‘long’ is used to describe duration; comparative forms are not needed here. Hence, ‘long’ fits.

उदाहरण: वह यहाँ लंबे समय से काम कर रही है।
[RULE] Adverbial use of long

---

[Q1668] If he ___ (1) earlier, he could have avoided the accident.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had arrived
(b) has arrived
(c) had to arrive
(d) arrive
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The third conditional uses the past perfect in the if‑clause. Thus, ‘had arrived’ is correct.

उदाहरण: अगर वह पहले पहुँच गया होता, तो दुर्घटना से बचा जा सकता था।
[RULE] Third conditional – past perfect

---

[Q1669] The committee will decide ___ (1) the proposal by next week.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) about
(b) on
(c) in
(d) to
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb ‘decide’ is followed by the preposition ‘on’ when referring to a subject. Hence, ‘on’ is correct.

उदाहरण: समिति प्रस्ताव पर निर्णय लेगी।
[RULE] Preposition after decide

---

[Q1670] The students were asked ___ (1) their home work before the class.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to submit
(b) to submitting
(c) submit
(d) submitting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After the verb ‘asked’, the infinitive form ‘to submit’ is required. Therefore, ‘to submit’ fits.

उदाहरण: उन्हें अपना होमवर्क जमा करने के लिए कहा गया।
[RULE] Infinitive after asked

---

[Q1671] He is considered one of the most ___ (1) writers in the genre.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) popular
(b) popularly
(c) popularity
(d) populars
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective ‘popular’ is used in a superlative construction; the adverb ‘popularly’ is incorrect here. Thus, ‘popular’ is correct.

उदाहरण: सबसे लोकप्रिय लेखक।
[RULE] Adjective usage with superlative

---

[Q1672] The project will be completed ___ (1) the end of the year.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) in
(c) at
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
A deadline expressed as a time period uses the preposition ‘by’. Hence, ‘by’ is correct.

उदाहरण: वर्ष के अंत तक पूरा होगा।
[RULE] Preposition by with deadline

---

[Q1673] It is essential that the report be submitted ___ (1) sooner.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) earlier
(b) earlier than
(c) earlier on
(d) earlier to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The comparative adverb ‘earlier’ describes a relative time; the prepositional phrases are unnecessary. Therefore, ‘earlier’ is correct.

उदाहरण: रिपोर्ट पहले सबमिट करना आवश्यक है।
[RULE] Comparative adverb earlier

---

[Q1674] The direct or said that the project would be successful ___ (1) strong leader ship.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) if
(b) than
(c) by
(d) with
[ANS] d
[SOL]
The noun phrase ‘strong leadership’ is linked with the preposition ‘with’ to indicate the condition for success. Thus, ‘with’ is correct.

उदाहरण: मजबूत नेतृत्व के साथ परियोजना सफल होगी।
[RULE] Preposition with noun phrase

---

[Q1675] The students [1] to the library every Sunday.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) go
(b) gone
(c) goes
(d) going
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The sentence talks about a habitual action in the present. The correct form of the verb is the base form 'go'.
उदाहरण: विद्यार्थी हर रविवार पुस्तकालय जाते हैं।
[RULE] Present Simple Tense for habitual actions

[Q1676] While I [1] the book, the phone rang.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) read
(b) was reading
(c) reading
(d) readed
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The action in the past was ongoing when another event occurred. We use the past continuous form 'was reading'.
उदाहरण: मैं पुस्तक पढ़ रहा था, तभी फोन बजा।
[RULE] Past Continuous Tense

---

[Q1677] The cake [1] by the time we arrived.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was made
(b) was being made
(c) made
(d) made by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The sentence describes a completed action in the past. The correct passive form is 'was made'.
उदाहरण: जब हम पहुंचे, केक बना हुआ था।
[RULE] Passive Voice (Past Simple)

---

[Q1678] You [1] finish your home work before watching TV.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) should
(b) must
(c) might
(d) would
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The sentence gives advice. The modal verb 'should' is used for suggestions or advice.
उदाहरण: तुम्हें टीवी देखने से पहले होमवर्क करना चाहिए।
[RULE] Modal Verb for advice (Should)

---

[Q1679] If it [1] tomorrow, we will cancel the picnic.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) rains
(b) rained
(c) raining
(d) rain
[ANS] a
[SOL]
This is a first conditional sentence. The present simple 'rains' is used in the if‑clause.
उदाहरण: अगर कल बारिश होती है तो हम पिकनिक रद्द कर देंगे।
[RULE] First Conditional (If + Present Simple)

---

[Q1680] She has been learning French [1] since she was ten.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) during
(d) by
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The phrase 'since' is used to indicate the starting point of an ongoing action. 'For' indicates duration.
उदाहरण: वह दस साल से फ्रेंच सीख रही है।
[RULE] Use of 'since' for starting point

---

[Q1681] I would have gone to the party if I [1] invited.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) am
(d) are
[ANS] b
[SOL]
In a third conditional sentence, the subjunctive 'were' is used after 'if' when the condition is contrary to reality.
उदाहरण: अगर मुझे आमंत्रित किया गया होता, तो मैं पार्टी में गया होता।
[RULE] Third Conditional (If + Past Perfect)

---

[Q1682] He [1] a lot of books during his holidays.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) uses
(b) used
(c) using
(d) use
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The sentence refers to a habit in the past. The simple past 'used' is correct.
उदाहरण: उसने छुट्टियों में बहुत सारी किताबें पढ़ीं।
[RULE] Past Simple for past habits

---

[Q1683] The manager will give a bonus to employees who [1] their targets.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) meet
(b) meeted
(c) meeting
(d) met
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The clause after 'who' requires the base form of the verb in a relative clause.
उदाहरण: प्रबंधक उन कर्मचारियों को बोनस देगा जो अपने लक्ष्यों को पूरा करते हैं।
[RULE] Relative clause with base form

---

[Q1684] The train [1] at 9:15 pm yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) departed
(b) depart
(c) has departed
(d) departing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
We use the simple past tense to describe a specific event that happened in the past.
उदाहरण: ट्रेन 9:15 बजे रात को रवाना हुई।
[RULE] Simple Past Tense for specific past events

[Q1685] The city council decided to (1) a new park in the down town area.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) establish
(b) established
(c) establishing
(d) establishment
[ANS] a

Use of infinitive after a verb to express purpose.
उदाहरण: "The council decided to establish a new park."
[RULE] Infinitive Usage

[Q1686] She has always been known as a person who (1) extraordinary ideas.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) generates
(b) generated
(c) generating
(d) generation
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Present simple tense for habitual action in third person singular.
उदाहरण: "He generates great ideas every day."
[RULE] Present Simple Tense

[Q1687] The committee will convene tomorrow, provided that all members (1) present.

(a) are
(b) was
(c) be
(d) being
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Present simple plural form of the verb 'to be'.
उदाहरण: "All members are present."

[Q1688] He promised to (1) his home work before the dead line

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) complete
(b) completed
(c) completing
(d) completion
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Infinitive form after a verb of promise.

[RULE] Infinitive After Promise

[Q1689] Unlike most reptiles, the basilisk lizard (1) to run quickly.

(a) can
(b) could
(c) will
(d) has
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use of modal verb 'can' to indicate ability.
उदाहरण: "The basilisk lizard can run quickly."
[RULE] Modal Verb for Ability

[Q1690] The manager, along with his team, (1) the project last year.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) completed
(b) complete
(c) completing
(d) completion
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past simple tense for completed action.
उदाहरण: "They completed the project."
[RULE] Past Simple Tense

[Q1691] It is essential for students to (1) regular practice of mathematics.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) require
(b) requires
(c) requiring
(d) required
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Infinitive after a verb of necessity.
उदाहरण: "Students need to practice regularly."
[RULE] Infinitive After Necessity

[Q1692] The book, which was published in 1998, (1) a bestseller.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) has become
(b) became
(c) become
(d) becoming
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Present perfect tense to show a state from past to present.
उदाहरण: "The book has become popular."
[RULE] Present Perfect Tense

[Q1693] The documentary (1) the life of the endangered species.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) tells
(b) told
(c) telling
(d) to tell
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Present simple tense for general facts.
उदाहरण: "The documentary tells the story."
[RULE] Present Simple Tense

[Q1694] They were astonished to find that the solution (1) the problem.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) solved
(b) solving
(c) solve
(d) solves
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past simple tense for completed action.
उदाहरण: "The solution solved the problem."
[RULE] Past Simple Tense

[Q1695] She has been working here for five years and she ________ a promotion last month.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) received
(b) receive
(c) receives
(d) receiving
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb after a past event in the past tense is in the simple past form.
उदाहरण: उसने कल खाना खाया।
[RULE] Simple Past tense after a past event

[Q1696] By the time the meeting started, all the participants had already ________ their reports.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) submitted
(b) submitting
(c) submits
(d) submittal
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The past perfect is used to show an action completed before another past action.
उदाहरण: उसने पहले ही खाना खा लिया था।
[RULE] Past Perfect tense

---

[Q1697] If I ________ earlier, I would have caught the train.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had left
(b) leave
(c) leaving
(d) left
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Conditionals with ‘would have’ use the past perfect in the ‘if’ clause.
उदाहरण: अगर मैं पहले निकल जाता, तो मैं ट्रेन पकड़ लेता।
[RULE] Third conditional (past perfect in if-clause)

(d) encourages
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After ‘will’ we use the base form of the verb.

[RULE] Future simple (will + base verb)

[Q1699] The professor asked the students to write a paper ________ the topic of climate change.

The preposition ‘on’ is the most natural choice after ‘paper’.

The simple past form ‘abandoned’ is required after a past event.

[Q1701] When the lights went out, the crowd ________ in panic.

(a) trembled

(a) take

After ‘will likely’ we use the base form of the verb.

[Q1703] The team had been working tirelessly, yet they ________ the dead line

Present perfect ‘has not’ is required here; the blank holds ‘has’.

[Q1705] The recent data shows a steady rise in global temperatures. Scientists say that human activities have contributed to this trend. Governments around the world are now working to [3] the rate of emissions.

(a) decrease
(b) increase
(c) stability
(d) fluctuation
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb *increase* is the correct choice because it expresses the growth in temperature. In Hindi: "तापमान में वृद्धि".

Rule: Use the verb that indicates a change in quantity.

(a) light

(a) discovered

[Q1740] The festival's main attraction is the grand parade, which attracts thousands of visitors, _1_.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) bringing joy to the community
(b) which brings joy to the community
(c) bringing joy to the communities
(d) that brings joy to the community
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The clause needs to be a relative clause in present tense to describe the parade. Rule: Use 'which brings' for a relative clause referring to a singular noun.
उदाहरण: "त्योहार की मुख्य आकर्षण बड़ी परेड है, **जो समुदाय को खुशी लाती है**।
[RULE] Relative Clause (Present Tense)

---

[Q1741] The scientist proposed a hypothesis that could _1_ the current understanding of quantum mechanics.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) challenge
(b) challenged
(c) challenging
(d) Hostile
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb must be in base form after 'could'. Rule: Use base form after modal verbs like could.
उदाहरण: "वैज्ञानिक ने एक परिकल्पना प्रस्तुत की जो **चुनौती** दे सकती है।
[RULE] Base Form after Modal Verb

---

[Q1742] The library's new digital catalog will allow users to _1_ books more efficiently.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) search
(b) searching
(c) searched
(d) searches
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The infinitive form is required after 'allow'. Rule: Use base form after verbs like allow, encourage, etc.
उदाहरण: "ग्रंथालय का नया डिजिटल कैटलॉग उपयोगकर्ताओं को पुस्तकें अधिक कुशलता से **खोजने** की अनुमति देगा।
[RULE] Infinitive after Allow

---

[Q1743] During the lecture, the professor emphasized the importance of peer review, _1_ for ensuring research quality.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) which is vital
(b) which are vital
(c) Venerable
(d) Whimsical
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The relative clause refers to 'peer review' (singular), so singular verb 'is' is needed. Rule: Match verb number with subject in relative clauses.
उदाहरण: "कक्षा में, प्रोफेसर ने सहकर्मी समीक्षा के महत्व पर जोर दिया, **जो महत्वपूर्ण है**।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement in Relative Clause

---

[Q1744] The team celebrated their victory, _1_ by cheering loudly in the stadium.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) which they did
(b) which they did so
(c) and they did
(d) and they did so
[ANS] d
[SOL]
The participial phrase needs a conjunction. Rule: Use 'and they did so' to link actions in past tense.
उदाहरण: "टीम ने अपनी जीत का जश्न मनाया, **और उन्होंने ऐसा करते हुए स्टेडियम में जोर से चीयर किया**।
[RULE] Conjunction in Participial Phrase

---

[Q1745] The new policy aims to reduce carbon emissions, _1_ by encouraging electric vehicle usage.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by doing so
(b) by doing that
(c) by doing
(d) by doing this
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The phrase 'by doing' is the correct gerund usage to explain the means. Rule: Use gerund after 'by' to indicate method.
उदाहरण: "नई नीति का उद्देश्य कार्बन उत्सर्जन को कम करना है, **करते हुए** इलेक्ट्रिक वाहन उपयोग को प्रोत्साहित करना।
[RULE] Gerund After 'By'

---

[Q1746] The manuscript, written in an archaic script, has been _1_ by a team of linguists for over a decade.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) translated
(b) translating
(c) translate
(d) translation
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Passive construction requires past participle. Rule: Use past participle with 'been' in passive voice.
उदाहरण: "पुराने लिपि में लिखी गई पांडुलिपि को **अनुवादित** किया गया है।
[RULE] Past Participle in Passive

---

[Q1747] The team of scientists (1) ________ a new theory about climate change.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) has
(b) have
(c) were
(d) is
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The subject "The team" is singular, so the verb must be singular.
उदाहरण: टीम एकवचन है, इसलिए क्रिया भी एकवचन होनी चाहिए।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1748] I have been working here for (1) ________ years.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) five
(b) fives
(c) fifth
(d) fiver
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When expressing a duration in years, a cardinal number is used as an adjective.
उदाहरण: "five years" में "five" एक विशेषण है।
[RULE] Use of Numbers as Adjectives

---

[Q1749] She was the first person (1) ________ to arrive at the station.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) arrived
(b) arrives
(c) arriving
(d) arrival
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"First" requires a past tense verb to indicate the order of arrival.
उदाहरण: "first person arrived" में "arrived" भूतकाल है।
[RULE] Past Tense After 'First'

---

[Q1750] The museum's collection includes artifacts from the (1) ________ civilization.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) ancient
(b) ancients
(c) anciently
(d) ancientness
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Ancient" is an adjective describing the civilization.
उदाहरण: "ancient civilization" में "ancient" विशेषण है।
[RULE] Adjective Usage

---

[Q1751] He has been waiting (1) ________ the bus for over an hour.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Wait for" is the correct prepositional phrase.
उदाहरण: "waiting for the bus" में "for" सही है।
[RULE] Preposition Usage

---

[Q1752] Although it was raining, we (1) ________ decided to go hiking.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) still
(b) stillly
(c) stills
(d) stillness
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Still" is an adverb meaning "however".
उदाहरण: "we still decided" में "still" क्रिया का संशोधक है।
[RULE] Adverb Usage

---

[Q1753] The report was presented (1) ________ the board yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) at
(c) on
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Present to" indicates the recipient of the report.
उदाहरण: "presented to the board" में "to" सही पूर्वसर्ग है।
[RULE] Preposition Usage

---

[Q1754] She has a habit of (1) ________ loudly during meetings.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) talk
(b) talking
(c) talked
(d) talks
[ANS] b
[SOL]
"Habit of" is followed by a gerund.
उदाहरण: "habit of talking" में "talking" जेरंड है।
[RULE] Gerund After Noun

[Q1755] The (1) ________ of the book was written in 1920.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) publication
(b) publish
(c) published
(d) publishing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Publication" is the noun form of the verb.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) implement
(b) implemented
(c) implementing
(d) implementation
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Will implement" is the correct infinitive form after modal verb.
उदाहरण: "will implement" में "implement" क्रिया का मूल रूप है।
[RULE] Infinitive After Modal Verb

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) so
(b) therefore
(c) however
(d) consequently
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adverb "so" indicates the result of her study. "Therefore" and "consequently" are also possible, but "so" fits the informal tone.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) arranged
(b) arranging
(c) arrangedly
(d) to arrange
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Arranged" is the correct past tense verb to complete the sentence.
उदाहरण: उन्होंने जल्दी से कार्यक्रम को पुनः निर्धारित किया।

---

[Q1760] The company’s profits have been steadily increasing; this trend is expected to [1] in the next fiscal year.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) continue
(b) continues
(c) continuing
(d) continued
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Continue" is the infinitive form needed after the verb "to" from the phrase "is expected to".
उदाहरण: लाभ अगले वर्ष भी बढ़ते रहेंगे।
[RULE] Infinitive after modal verb

[Q1761] When choosing a laptop, it’s important to consider both the processor speed and the amount of [1] memory.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) RAM

(c) Hard
(d) Flash
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"RAM" (Random Access Memory) is the type of memory referred to in this context.

[RULE] Correct terminology

[Q1762] If you want to improve your public speaking skills, you should practice speaking in front of a [1] audience.

(a) small

[ANS] a

[Q1766] The new policy aims to reduce traffic accidents by encouraging commuters to [1] alternative modes of transport.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) adopt
(b) adopts
(c) adopting
(d) adopted
[ANS] a

"Adopt" is the infinitive form after "to".
उदाहरण: वैकल्पिक परिवहन को अपनाने के लिए प्रोत्साहित करें।

[Q1767] The scientist, who had spent years studying the effects of climate change, presented his findings at the conference. He claimed that the warming trend was not only accelerating but also affecting the migration patterns of various species. Consequently, many habitats are now at risk, and urgent measures must be taken to protect these vulnerable ecosystems.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had spent
(b) spent
(c) had spending
(d) spend
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past perfect is used to indicate an action completed before another past action. 
उदा. वह **had spent** years studying, before presenting the findings.
[RULE] Past Perfect

[Q1768] If the company continues to ignore the safety protocols, it will likely face legal consequences. The employees who have been regularly violating the rules should be reminded that their actions are not only dangerous but also **[1]** to the organization.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a)  detrimental
(b) beneficial
(c) neutral
(d) irrelevant
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Adjectives must agree in meaning with the context. The word should reflect the negative impact.
उदा. यह **detrimental** है.
[RULE] Adjective Usage

[Q1769] The committee, along with its chair person, has decided to postpone the meeting until a more suitable time. This decision was reached after a thorough discussion, and it reflects the **[1]** of the organization.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) collective spirit
(b) individual opinion
(c) random choice
(d) immediate action
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase should convey unity and joint decision. 'Collective spirit' fits the context.
उदा. यह **collective spirit** को दर्शाता है.
[RULE] Collocations

---

[Q1770] The new policy will **[1]** all employees to submit their expense reports by the end of the month. Failure to comply may result in delayed reimbursements.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) require
(b) requires
(c) required
(d) recommending
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Subject-verb agreement: singular subject 'policy' requires singular verb 'requires'.
उदा. नीति **requires** रिपोर्ट जमा करने की।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

[Q1771] During the audit, the accountant discovered that the ledger had been **[1]** for months, leading to discrepancies in the financial statements.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) overlooked
(b) overlooking
(c) overlook
(d) overlooked by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past participle is used with 'had been' to indicate an action completed earlier.
उदा. लेज़र **overlooked** गया था.

[Q1772] The committee will appoint a new chair person **[1]** the upcoming meeting. The chosen individual must have a strong back ground in project management.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) in
(c) for
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Preposition 'at' is used with 'meeting' to indicate location/time.
उदा. बैठक **at** अगले सप्ताह.

[Q1773] The research, which was funded by the government, **[1]** promising results that could revolutionize renewable energy technologies.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) has yielded
(b) yielded

Simple past tense 'yielded' matches the past event of funding and results.
उदा. शोध **yielded** अच्छे परिणाम।

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) share
(b) sharing
(c) shared

[ANS] a

Infinitive form 'share' follows the verb 'encouraged to'.
उदा. उन्हें **share** अपने विचार प्रोत्साहित किया जाता है।

[Q1775] The new regulations will **[1]** the use of single use plastics across all public facilities, aiming to reduce environmental waste.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) ban
(b) banned

Present simple 'ban' is used to describe a future or ongoing policy.
उदा. वे **ban** एकल-उपयोग प्लास्टिक को प्रतिबंधित करेंगे।

[Q1776] During the meeting, the direct or **[1]** a new project proposal that could potentially increase the company's market share.

(a) presented

(c) present
(d) presented by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past simple 'presented' is used for an action completed in the past.
उदा. निर्देशक ने एक नया प्रस्ताव **presented** किया।
[RULE] Past Simple Tense

[Q1777] The committee [1] __________ to hold the meeting on Saturday.

[Q1788] She has been studying English ________ she was nine years old.

उदाहरण: वह नौ वर्ष की उम्र से अंग्रेज़ी पढ़ रही है।
[RULE] Use of 'since' with a specific point

---

[Q1789] If you ________ the instructions, you will get the job done correctly.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) follow
(b) followed
(c) are following
(d) following
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In a conditional sentence with 'if', use the simple present tense.
उदाहरण: यदि आप निर्देशों का पालन करते हैं, तो आप काम सही ढंग से कर लेंगे।
[RULE] Simple Present in First Conditional

---

[Q1790] The committee will decide ________ the proposal next week.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) at
(c) by
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Decide on' is the correct preposition to use with 'decide'.
उदाहरण: समिति अगले हफ्ते प्रस्ताव पर निर्णय करेगी।
[RULE] Preposition 'on' with 'decide on'

---

[Q1791] He was the first person ________ the rescue team successfully.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) who led
(b) who leads
(c) who will lead
(d) who had led
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Past perfect tense is used because the action of leading occurred before the rescue team’s success.
उदाहरण: वह पहला व्यक्ति था जिसने बचाव दल को सफलतापूर्वक नेतृत्व किया।
[RULE] Past Perfect in Relative Clause

---

[Q1792] The children were excited ________ the new play ground opened.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) when
(b) as
(c) while
(d) by
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'As' is used to indicate simultaneous actions in the past.
उदाहरण: बच्चे नए खेल के मैदान के खुलने पर उत्साहित थे।
[RULE] Use of 'as' for simultaneous actions

---

[Q1793] By the time the train arrived, the passengers ________ their tickets.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had collected
(b) collected
(c) are collecting
(d) will collect
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past perfect is appropriate because the collecting happened before the arrival.
उदाहरण: जब ट्रेन आई, तो यात्रियों ने अपने टिकट इकट्ठा कर लिए थे।
[RULE] Past Perfect for earlier action

---

[Q1794] She will start her new job ________ Monday.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) at
(c) by
(d) in
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use 'at' with specific days of the week.
उदाहरण: वह सोमवार को अपनी नई नौकरी शुरू करेगी।
[RULE] Preposition 'at' with days of the week

---

[Q1795] If the company could ________ the costs, it would expand internationally.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reduce
(b) reduced
(c) reducing
(d) has reduced
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In a second conditional, use the base form of the verb.
उदाहरण: यदि कंपनी लागतें घटा सके तो वह अंतरराष्ट्रीय स्तर पर विस्तार करेगी।
[RULE] Base verb form in Second Conditional

---

[Q1796] They were praised ________ their innovative design.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) with
(d) of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Praised for' is the correct collocation.
उदाहरण: उन्हें उनके नवाचारी डिज़ाइन के लिए प्रशंसा मिली।
[RULE] Collocation 'praised for'

---

[Q1797] The young scientist had always been very curious and was especially fascinated by the mysteries of the deep sea. One day, while studying the behavior of a rare species, he felt a strong sense of [1] in his research.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) inspired
(b) inspire
(c) inspiring
(d) inspiredly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Past tense of the verb ‘to inspire’ is ‘inspired’.
उदा. क्रिया का भूतकाल ‘inspired’ होता है।
[RULE] Past Tense of Regular Verb

---

[Q1798] During the exhibition, the artist's work was widely [1] by critics and visitors alike, and it quickly became the high light of the event.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) appreciated
(b) appreciates
(c) appreciating
(d) appreciation
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Past participle used as an adjective.
उदा. ‘appreciated’ यहाँ एक विशेषण के रूप में प्रयोग हो रहा है।
[RULE] Past Participle as Adjective

---

[Q1799] If she had left earlier, she would have avoided the [1] that caused the traffic to gridlock for hours.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) traffic jam
(b) traffic jams
(c) jam traffic
(d) traffic jammed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Singular noun phrase; ‘traffic jam’ is the idiomatic expression.
उदा. ‘traffic jam’ का अर्थ है ट्रैफ़िक का जाम।
[RULE] Idiomatic Noun Phrase

---

[Q1800] The committee's decision was made after extensive [1] among its members, resulting in a comprehensive proposal.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) debate
(b) debated
(c) debating
(d) debates
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Noun form of the verb ‘to debate’.
उदा. ‘debate’ यहाँ एक संज्ञा के रूप में प्रयोग हुआ है।

[Q1801] She brought a [1] of freshly sliced vegetables to the picnic, which every one appreciated for its freshness.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) pile
(b) piled
(c) piles
(d) piling
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Noun form ‘pile’ meaning a heap.
उदा. ‘pile’ एक संज्ञा है जिसका अर्थ है ढेर।

[Q1802] The new policy will [1] the efficiency of the system by reducing redundant steps.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) improve
(b) improved
(c) improvement
(d) improving
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Base form of the verb used after modal-like expressions.
उदा. ‘improve’ क्रिया का मूल रूप है।
[RULE] Base Form of Verb

[Q1803] By the time we arrived, the show had already [1] when the crowd started cheering.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) started
(b) starting
(c) start
(d) startled
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Past simple tense of the verb ‘to start’.
उदा. ‘started’ भूतकाल में प्रयोग हुआ है।

[Q1804] He was known for his [1] of the book, which provided fresh insights into classical literature.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) interpretation
(b) interpreter
(c) interpret
(d) interpreting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Noun form ‘interpretation’ used to denote an act of interpreting.
उदा. ‘interpretation’ एक संज्ञा है।

[Q1805] The company announced a new initiative to [1] employee engagement across all departments.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) boost
(b) boosted
(c) boosting
(d) boosts
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Base form of the verb used in an infinitive-like construction.

[Q1806] The film, which was shot in Paris, has already [1] world wide acclaim for its artistic direct i on.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) earned
(b) earning
(c) earn
(d) earnings
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Past simple tense of ‘to earn’.
उदा. ‘earned’ भूतकाल में प्रयोग हुआ है।

[Q1807] By the time the train arrived, the passengers (1) already ________ their luggage.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) paid
(b) have paid
(c) had paid
(d) will pay
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The sentence refers to an action completed *before* another past action (the train’s arrival). Therefore, the past perfect tense is required.
उदाहरण: जब ट्रेन पहुंची, तो यात्रियों ने अपना सामान पहले ही रख लिया था.

[Q1808] She (1) reading the book when the phone rang.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was reading
(b) is reading
(c) reads
(d) have been reading
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The action of reading was ongoing at a specific past moment when another event happened. The past continuous tense is used.
उदाहरण: जब फोन बजा, वह किताब पढ़ रही थी.
[RULE] Past Continuous Tense

[Q1809] The documents (1) ________ by the committee yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) are signed
(b) was signed
(c) were signed
(d) have signed
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The sentence is in the passive voice. The correct form uses the past simple of 'to be' + past participle.
उदाहरण: दस्तावेज़ कल समिति द्वारा हस्ताक्षरित किए गए थे.
[RULE] Passive Voice (Past Simple)

[Q1810] You (1) ________ the door if you want to keep the room warm.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) must close
(b) should close
(c) will close
(d) have closed
[ANS] b
[SOL]
A recommendation or advice is expressed using the modal verb 'should'.
उदाहरण: अगर आप कमरे को गर्म रखना चाहते हैं तो आपको दरवाज़ा बंद करना चाहिए.
[RULE] Modal Verb – Should (Advice)

---

[Q1811] Her performance was (1) ________ than his, but he was still praised for his efforts.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) better
(b) best
(c) good
(d) well
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Comparatives join two nouns; the correct comparative form of 'good' is 'better'.
उदाहरण: उसका प्रदर्शन उसके मुकाबले बेहतर था, पर फिर भी उसकी प्रशंसा की गई.
[RULE] Comparative Adjectives

---

[Q1812] He arrived home (1) ________ the storm had already started.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) because
(b) although
(c) since
(d) before
[ANS] d
[SOL]
The clause indicates a prior event; the correct preposition is 'before'.
उदाहरण: तूफान शुरू होने से पहले वह घर लौट आया.
[RULE] Prepositional Phrase (Before)

---

[Q1813] If it (1) ________ tomorrow, the picnic will be cancelled.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) rain
(b) rains
(c) raining
(d) rained
[ANS] b
[SOL]
A first conditional uses the present simple in the ‘if’ clause. Thus, 'rains' is required.
उदाहरण: अगर कल बारिश हुई, तो पिकनिक रद्द कर दी जाएगी.
[RULE] First Conditional (Present Simple)

---

[Q1814] They say that the new policy (1) ________ not only reduce costs but also improve safety.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) will
(b) does
(c) have
(d) has
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Reported speech uses the future simple in the main clause. Hence 'will' is correct.
उदाहरण: कहा जाता है कि नई नीति न केवल लागत घटाएगी बल्कि सुरक्षा भी बढ़ाएगी.
[RULE] Reported Speech – Future Simple

[Q1815] She enjoys (1) ________ novels in her free time.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reading
(b) to read
(c) read
(d) reads
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After the verb 'enjoys', a gerund is required.
उदाहरण: वह अपनी फुर्सत में पढ़ने का आनंद लेती है.
[RULE] Gerund after Enjoys

[Q1816] ______ can be found at the end of the sentence to indicate an indefinite reference.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) A pronoun

(d) A preposition
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The sentence describes an indefinite pronoun. Hence, 'A pronoun' is correct.
उदाहरण: एक सर्वनाम वाक्य के अंत में पाया जा सकता है.
[RULE] Indefinite Pronoun

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) after

(d) before
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The announcement will happen following the conclusion of the meeting; 'after' is the appropriate preposition.
उदाहरण: बैठक समाप्त होने के बाद समिति परिणाम घोषित करेगी.

[Q1818] The company announced that it would start the new project in the next quarter, provided that all the necessary approvals are secured. The board members were confident that their efforts would pay off, as the project was expected to generate a significant _____ for the organization.

(a) profit
(b) profitability
(c) profitable
(d) profitably
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The noun ‘profit’ is the correct answer because the blank requires a noun that refers to the financial benefit. 
उदाहरण: "यह परियोजना कंपनी के लिए बड़ा मुनाफा ला सकती है।"

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) showed
(b) showed that
(c) showing
(d) shows
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The clause requires a verb phrase that introduces a subordinate clause. ‘Showed that’ correctly conveys the meaning. 
उदाहरण: "अध्ययन ने दिखाया कि दवा प्रभावी है।"
[RULE] Past tense verb + that-clause

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) asset
(b) assets
(c) assets’
(d) asset’s
[ANS] a
[SOL]
‘Asset’ is singular noun referring to an individual’s valuable quality. 
उदाहरण: "उनकी समयबद्धता एक मूल्यवान संपत्ति है।"

[Q1821] Despite the heavy rain, the marathon runners continued their training; they believed that the _____ of perseverance would lead to success.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) power
(b) powerful
(c) powers
(d) powerfulness
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The blank requires a noun that denotes a quality; ‘power’ fits best. 
उदाहरण: "संकल्प की शक्ति सफलता लाती है।"

[Q1822] The new policy will _____ the process of approving travel expenses, making it quicker for employees to get reimbursed.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) simplify
(b) simplifies
(c) simplified
(d) simplifying
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The subject ‘policy’ is singular; therefore the verb should be in the present simple tense: ‘simplifies’. 
उदाहरण: "नीति अनुमोदन प्रक्रिया को सरल बनाती है।"

(a) reaffirms
(b) reaffirm
(c) reaffirmed
(d) reaffirming
[ANS] d

The present participle ‘reaffirming’ correctly links the two clauses, indicating simultaneous action. 
उदाहरण: "प्रदर्शनी इस युग के महत्व को स्पष्ट करती है।"

[Q1824] The committee’s decision was finalized after a lengthy discussion; however, the chief executive was still _____ about the potential risks involved.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) uncertain
(b) uncertainty
(c) unconfident
(d) unconfidently
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The blank requires an adjective describing the chief executive’s state of mind. ‘Uncertain’ fits best. 
उदाहरण: "वह जोखिमों के बारे में अनिश्चित थे।"

[Q1825] The city’s population has been growing steadily, which _____ the need for improved public transportation infrastructure.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) implies
(b) implied
(c) implying
(d) imply
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The present tense verb ‘implies’ matches the present progressive clause.

[Q1826] The scientist’s meticulous experiments _____ that the hypothesis could be incorrect, prompting a re-evaluation of the data.

(a) indicated
(b) indicates
(c) indicating
(d) indication

[Q1827] The new software upgrade will _____ the user experience by reducing loading times and enhancing security features.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) improve
(b) improving
(c) improved
(d) improves
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The infinitive ‘improve’ correctly follows ‘will’ to indicate future action.

[Q1828] After the meeting, the team was very (1) about the new project.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) excited
(b) excitedly
(c) exciting
(d) excitement
[ANS] a

The blank requires an adjective to describe the team's feeling. "Excited" fits the sentence context.
उदाहरण: टीम बहुत excited है।

[Q1829] She (1) to the market every Saturday to buy fresh vegetables.

(a) goes
(b) went
(c) going
(d) gone
[ANS] a

[Q1830] If it (1) tomorrow, we will postpone the picnic.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) rains
(b) rain
(c) raining
(d) rained
[ANS] a
[SOL]
A first conditional requires the simple present in the if‑clause. "Rains" is correct.
उदाहरण: अगर कल बारिश होती है तो हम पिकनिक टाल देंगे।

[Q1831] The scientist, who (1) the experiment last night, was exhausted.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) conducted
(b) conduct
(c) conducting
(d) Dubious
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The clause is a past participle describing the scientist. "Conducted" is the correct past tense form.
उदाहरण: वैज्ञानिक, जिसने कल रात प्रयोग किया, थका हुआ था।
[RULE] Past participle in relative clause

[Q1832] By the time the train (1), the passengers had already boarded.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) arriving
(b) arrived
(c) arrives
(d) Kinetic
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The sentence uses a past perfect in the main clause; the if‑clause needs simple past. "Arrived" is correct.
उदाहरण: जब ट्रेन पहुंची, तो यात्री पहले ही सवार हो चुके थे।
[RULE] Past perfect vs. simple past

---

[Q1833] The book that I (1) yesterday was very interesting.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) read
(b) reads
(c) reading
(d) readed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The relative clause requires simple past; "read" is the correct form.
उदाहरण: वह किताब जिसे मैंने कल पढ़ा, बहुत रोचक थी।
[RULE] Simple past in relative clause

---

[Q1834] We will start the discussion only after every one has (1).
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) arrived
(b) arrives
(c) arriving
(d) Pragmatic
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The clause uses the perfect infinitive; "arrived" is the correct form.
उदाहरण: सभी के आने के बाद ही हम चर्चा शुरू करेंगे।
