(a) critical
(b) important
(c) minor
(d) irrelevant
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Choosing adjectives that reflect significance. 'Important' best fits. 
उदाहरण: The discussion focused on *important* issues.
[RULE] Adjective for importance

---

[Q1884] The newly renovated highway features a series of (1) bridges that span the wide river, providing a smoother commute for residents.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) steel
(b) arch
(c) concrete
(d) wooden
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Selection of an adjective that describes the structural design. 'Arch' is commonly used for aesthetically pleasing bridges. 
उदाहरण: The highway has *arch* bridges.
[RULE] Adjective for design

---

[Q1885] The chef explained that the secret to a perfect soufflé lies in the careful whisking of the eggs and the use of (1) ingredients that do not interfere with the rising process.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) spicy
(b) flavorful
(c) fresh
(d) bitter
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The adjective should denote quality suitable for the dish. 'Fresh' is best. 
उदाहरण: The soufflé was made with *fresh* ingredients.
[RULE] Adjective for quality

---

[Q1886] The research paper concluded that the new algorithm significantly reduced the computational time by 30%, making it a (1) solution for large datasets.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) efficient
(b) inefficient
(c) complex
(d) simple
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Adjective indicating performance. 'Efficient' describes a desirable trait. 
उदाहरण: The algorithm is an *efficient* solution.
[RULE] Adjective for performance

---

[Q1887] During the debate, the candidate highlighted the need for stricter environmental regulations to protect the fragile ecosystems of the (1) wetlands.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) dry
(b) wet
(c) desert
(d) arid
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Adjective must match the noun phrase. 'Wet' is correct. 
उदाहरण: The wetlands are *wet* and support diverse life.
[RULE] Adjective for environment

---

[Q1888] The artist’s latest exhibition features abstract paintings that evoke feelings of calmness and (1) serenity, capturing the essence of nature.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) deep
(b) peaceful
(c) chaotic
(d) turbulent
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Adjective that conveys tranquility. 'Peaceful' is appropriate. 
उदाहरण: The art brings *peaceful* serenity.
[RULE] Adjective for emotion

---

[Q1889] Every student in the class ________ eager to learn.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) is
(b) are
(c) am
(d) be
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Subject-verb agreement – a singular subject requires a singular verb.
उदाहरण: "हर छात्र खुश है।"
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1890] She had finished her work, and then she ________ to the market.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) goes
(b) went
(c) go
(d) going
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule: Use past simple after past perfect to show completion of a past action.
उदाहरण: "वह स्कूल गई थी, फिर उसने खाना खाया।"
[RULE] Tense Consistency

---

[Q1891] You ________ careful while handling the chemicals.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) must
(b) could
(c) may
(d) will
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Modal ‘must’ expresses necessity or obligation.
उदाहरण: "आपको समय पर आना चाहिए।"
[RULE] Modal Verbs

---

[Q1892] The teacher gave the students a book, and they read ________.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) it
(b) them
(c) their
(d) its
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Pronoun must agree in number with its antecedent.
उदाहरण: "किताब पढ़ी गई।"
[RULE] Pronoun Reference

---

[Q1893] The picture is hanging ________ the wall.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Preposition ‘on’ is used for surfaces.
उदाहरण: "चित्र दीवार पर लटका हुआ है।"
[RULE] Prepositions

---

[Q1894] He bought ________ car yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a
(b) an
(c) the
(d) no article
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Use ‘a’ before a singular noun not specified.
उदाहरण: "मैंने एक किताब खरीदी।"
[RULE] Article Usage

---

[Q1895] She wore a ________ dress.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) red beautiful summer
(b) beautiful red summer
(c) summer red beautiful
(d) beautiful summer red
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule: Order of adjectives – descriptive, color, type.
उदाहरण: "एक सुंदर लाल गर्मियों का परिधान।"
[RULE] Adjective Order

---

[Q1896] She sang ________ well.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) so
(b) very
(c) quite
(d) well
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Adverb of degree comes before the adjective/adverb it modifies.
उदाहरण: "उसने बहुत अच्छा गाया।"
[RULE] Adverb Placement

---

[Q1897] The new policy ________ next month.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) will be implemented
(b) has implemented
(c) will implement
(d) was implemented
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Passive voice with future tense – ‘will be + past participle’.
उदाहरण: "नीति लागू की जाएगी।"
[RULE] Passive Voice

---

[Q1898] My brother is ________ than me.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) taller
(b) tallest
(c) tall
(d) taller than
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Comparative form of an adjective ends in ‘-er’.
उदाहरण: "वह मुझसे ऊँचा है।"
[RULE] Comparatives

---

[Q1899] Every morning, the school bus [1] to the school at 7:30 a.m. It is very punctual and never [1] late.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) goes
(b) go
(c) went
(d) gone
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Present simple tense is used to describe habitual actions. 
उदा. हर रोज़, वह स्कूल जाता है।
[RULE] Present Simple Habitual

---

[Q1900] She had already finished her home work [1] when the teacher called her to the desk.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) before
(b) after
(c) by
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past perfect tense is used to show an action completed before another past action. 
उदा. उसने पहले अपना काम पूरा कर लिया था।
[RULE] Past Perfect

---

[Q1901] If I [1] you, I would apologize immediately.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) were
(b) was
(c) am
(d) be
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Second conditional uses was/were for unreal situations in the present. 
उदा. अगर मैं तुम्हारी जगह होता, तो मैं तुरंत माफी मांगता।
[RULE] Second Conditional

---

[Q1902] The book [1] on the table is my favourite novel.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) which
(b) that
(c) who
(d) whom
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Relative pronoun 'that' is used for defining noun clauses. 
उदा. वह पुस्तक जो टेबल पर है, मेरी पसंदीदा है।
[RULE] Defining Relative Clause

---

[Q1903] I would have liked [1] you to join us for the reunion.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) seeing
(b) to see
(c) to seeed
(d) to seeing
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Infinitive form after verbs of wish: 'would have liked to see'. 
उदा. मैं चाहूँगा कि आप हमें मिलें।
[RULE] Infinitive after Wish

---

[Q1904] The committee has decided to postpone the meeting, so that [1] every one can attend.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) they
(b) all
(c) anyone
(d) each
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The pronoun 'everyone' is used with singular verb 'can'. 
उदा. ताकि सभी उपस्थित हो सकें।
[RULE] Everyone + Singular Verb

---

[Q1905] He didn't realize that the key had been misplaced [1] a few days ago.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) until
(b) since
(c) when
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The word 'until' is used to indicate the point up to which something was true. 
उदा. जब तक वह यह नहीं जानते थे।
[RULE] Until for Time

---

[Q1906] The children, [1] were playing in the garden, shouted loudly when the dog barked.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) who
(b) that
(c) which
(d) when
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Relative pronoun 'who' refers to people. 
उदा. बच्चे, जो खेल रहे थे।
[RULE] Relative Pronoun for People

---

[Q1907] It is essential that every one [1] the guidelines before starting the project.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) follow
(b) follows
(c) followsed
(d) following
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After 'that' in a subjunctive clause, use base form of the verb. 
उदा. यह आवश्यक है कि सभी दिशानिर्देशों का पालन करें।
[RULE] Subjunctive Mood

---

[Q1908] The festival was celebrated with great enthusiasm, and the streets were filled with [1] people.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a lot of
(b) lots of
(c) many
(d) much
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'Many' is used with countable nouns such as 'people'. 
उदा. बहुत से लोग।
[RULE] Many with Countable Nouns

---

[Q1909] The new policy has been implemented ___ (1) many employees have reported increased satisfaction. The management believes that this change will ___ (2) the overall productivity. However, some staff are concerned that it might ___ (3) the work life balance.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) 1) since  2) enhance  3) affect
(b) 1) because  2) enhance  3) affect
(c) 1) since  2) affect  3) enhance
(d) 1) because  2) affect  3) enhance
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Present perfect tense is used for actions that started in the past and continue to the present.
उदा. The new policy has been implemented.

Grammatical Order: Present Perfect + since + adverbial clause.

Correct option: 1) since 2) enhance 3) affect.
[RULE] Present Perfect Tense with since + verb

---

[Q1910] If the committee ___ (1) the proposal earlier, they would have avoided the delay. Instead, the decision was postponed until the next meeting, which ___ (2) the project schedule.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) 1) had reviewed  2) disrupted
(b) 1) reviewed  2) disrupted
(c) 1) had reviewed  2) had disrupted
(d) 1) had been reviewing  2) disrupts
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Third conditional: 'If + past perfect, would have + past participle'.
उदा. If the committee had reviewed the proposal earlier,

The second clause uses simple past to describe a factual consequence.

Correct option: 1) had reviewed 2) disrupted.
[RULE] Third Conditional

---

[Q1911] The committee, ___ (1) the decision was influenced by several factors, announced the new guidelines. The guidelines ___ (2) stricter than before, aiming to improve safety standards.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) 1) where  2) are
(b) 1) where  2) were
(c) 1) that  2) are
(d) 1) that  2) were
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Relative clause: 'which' or 'that' introduces a clause. Here we need a relative pronoun to refer to 'the decision'.
उदा. ...the decision, which was influenced by several factors,...

Use 'that' for restrictive clauses.

Correct option: 3) that 2) are.
[RULE] Restrictive Relative Clause

---

[Q1912] The committee will launch the project ___ (1) the approval comes through. The team is ready, but they ___ (2) the final report before the launch.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) 1) once  2) must submit
(b) 1) until  2) must submit
(c) 1) once  2) will submit
(d) 1) until  2) will submit
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Conditional 'once' is used for future real condition. The second clause uses modal 'must' for obligation.
उदा. We will start once you give the nod.

Correct option: 1) once 2) must submit.
[RULE] Future Real Conditional

---

[Q1913] The report was ___ (1) by the auditors, and the findings were ___ (2) to the management. The audit team ___ (3) a comprehensive review during the last quarter.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) 1) prepared  2) presented  3) conducted
(b) 1) prepared  2) presented  3) was conducted
(c) 1) prepared by  2) presented to  3) conducted
(d) 1) prepared by  2) presented to  3) was conducted
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Passive voice: 'The report was prepared by the auditors.' The second clause uses passive with preposition 'to'. The third clause uses passive with 'was conducted'.
उदा. The audit team was conducted by the auditors.

Correct option: 4th.
[RULE] Passive Voice

---

[Q1914] He suggested that the team ___ (1) the data immediately. Although the suggest i on was reasonable, they ___ (2) to follow it due to budget constraints.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) 1) analyse  2) decided
(b) 1) analyze  2) decided
(c) 1) analyse  2) decided to
(d) 1) analyze  2) decided to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Subjunctive mood after 'suggested that': verb in base form. The second clause uses simple past tense.
उदा. He suggested that they analyze the data.

Correct option: 1) analyse 2) decided.
[RULE] Subjunctive Mood

---

[Q1915] The new software is expected to ___ (1) the efficiency of the process. However, it may ___ (2) some existing features, so users are advised to ___ (3) their settings before installation.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) 1) increase  2) remove  3) adjust
(b) Pensive
(c) Quaint
(d) Redundant
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The blanks require a verb in base form after modal verbs. The pattern is consistent: increase, remove, adjust.
उदा. The new software will increase efficiency.

Correct option: 1) increase 2) remove 3) adjust.
[RULE] Modal Verb Followed by Base Verb

---

[Q1916] The manager said that the project ___ (1) completed by next week, but the team ___ (2) the dead line due to unforeseen issues. The client was ___ (3) about the delay.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) 1) will be  2) missed  3) disappointed
(b) Whimsical
(c) Zealous
(d) Pragmatic
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Future simple in reported speech. The past simple 'missed' follows the reporting verb. The adjective 'disappointed' describes the client's feeling.
उदा. The manager said the project would be completed.

Correct option: 1) will be 2) missed 3) disappointed.
[RULE] Reported Speech with Future Simple

---

[Q1917] The committee decided to ___ (1) the proposal after reviewing the feedback. The proposal will ___ (2) the current policy and ___ (3) the organization’s long-term goals.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) 1) approve  2) align  3) support
(b) 1) approve  2) aligns  3) supports
(c) 1) approve  2) align  3) supports
(d) 1) approve  2) aligns  3) support
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Subject-verb agreement: 'The proposal will align' (singular). The third verb is base form after 'and'.
उदा. The proposal will align with the policy.

Correct option: 1) approve 2) align 3) support.
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1918] The new guidelines ___ (1) the public widely. The guidelines ___ (2) the new regulations, which ___ (3) stricter than the previous ones.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Ephemeral
(b) 1) were adopted  2) aligns  3) are
(c) 1) were adopted  2) align  3) are
(d) Futile
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Passive past tense 'were adopted' followed by present simple 'align' for present relevance, and 'are' for present state.
उदा. The guidelines are stricter.

Correct option: 3rd.
[RULE] Passive Past Tense

---

[Q1919] The new training program ___ (1) offered to all employees. The program ___ (2) the skill gaps identified during the last audit. It is expected to ___ (3) the overall performance.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) 1) has been  2) address  3) improve
(b) 1) has been  2) addresses  3) improve
(c) 1) has been  2) address  3) improves
(d) 1) has been  2) addresses  3) improves
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Present perfect passive 'has been offered', present simple 'address', base form 'improve'.
उदा. The program has been offered.

Correct option: 1) has been 2) address 3) improve.
[RULE] Present Perfect Passive

---

[Q1920] The city council decided to renovate the old library, but the plan was postponed because of [1] funding issues. The mayor announced that the budget would be increased by 10% next year, which would finally allow the project to [2] move forward. However, some residents are concerned that the new design might [3] alter the historic character of the neighborhood.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) lack of
(b) lack
(c) lacking
(d) without
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use the noun phrase ‘lack of’ to indicate absence of something.
उदा. ‘कमी’ के लिये ‘lack of’ उपयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Noun phrase + of

---

[Q1921] In the early nineteenth century, the industrial revolution had a profound effect on society. Many factories were built in the cities, which caused the population to [1] and the environment to [2] rapidly. As a result, governments began to implement regulations to [3] pollution levels.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) grow
(b) growed
(c) growing
(d) Eloquent
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The simple present tense ‘grow’ is used for general truth.
उदाहरण: ‘जनसंख्या बढ़ती है’ में ‘grow’ सही है।
[RULE] Simple present tense

---

[Q1922] The research paper concluded that the new drug was effective in treating the disease, provided that patients adhered to the [1] regimen. Failure to follow the instructions could lead to [2] complications and an overall decrease in efficacy. The authors therefore recommended a [3] monitoring system.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) daily
(b) dailyy
(c) dailying
(d) Affluent
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Adjectives used to describe frequency are in the form ‘daily’.
उदा. ‘दैनिक नियम’ के लिये ‘daily’ सही है।
[RULE] Adjective for frequency

---

[Q1923] During the meeting, the team leader emphasized that the project would be successful only if every one shared responsibility. She asked each member to write a brief report on their tasks by the end of the week, so that the progress could be [1] and any obstacles could be addressed promptly.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) monitored
(b) monitoring
(c) Hostile
(d) Impeccable
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The past participle ‘monitored’ is used after ‘be’ to form passive voice.
उदाहरण: ‘प्रगति की निगरानी की जाती है’ में ‘monitored’ सही है।
[RULE] Passive voice with past participle

---

[Q1924] The museum’s latest exhibit showcases artifacts from the Renaissance period. Visitors can expect to see paintings, sculptures, and manuscripts that illustrate the era’s [1] innovation. The curator mentioned that the collection includes pieces that were [2] discovered in a coastal village last year.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) technological
(b) techno-logical
(c) Obsolete
(d) Pensive
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective ‘technological’ is correctly spelled and used to describe innovation.
उदाहरण: ‘प्रौद्योगिक नवाचार’ के लिये ‘technological’ सही है।
[RULE] Adjective formation

---

[Q1925] The teacher asked the students to write an essay about the benefits of reading. She then instructed them to submit their work either on paper or via the online portal. The dead line was set for next Friday, and the teacher promised that the essays would be evaluated on [1] quality, originality, and clarity.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) the
(b) a
(c) an
(d) our
[ANS] a

Use the definite article ‘the’ before a noun when the noun is specific.
उदाहरण: ‘विशिष्ट गुणवत्ता’ के लिये ‘the’ उपयोग होता है।
[RULE] Definite article usage

---

[Q1926] After the storm, the town's water supply was contaminated, leading to a health crisis. The local authorities decided to set up a temporary water treatment plant. They also announced that emergency services would be available 24 hours a day to assist residents with [1].

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) survival
(b) survivals
(c) survived
(d) survival’s
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use the singular noun ‘survival’ as an object of assistance.
उदाहरण: ‘सहायता’ के लिये ‘survival’ सही है।
[RULE] Singular noun usage

---

[Q1927] The new policy aims to reduce carbon emissions by encouraging the use of renewable energy sources. Both households and businesses are urged to adopt solar panels, wind turbines, or other alternatives. This initiative is expected to [1] the nation’s dependence on fossil fuels.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) decrease
(b) decreasing
(c) decreased
(d) decreaseing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use the simple present tense ‘decrease’ for a future action.
उदाहरण: ‘निर्भरत को घटाना’ में ‘decrease’ सही है।
[RULE] Simple present tense for future

---

[Q1928] The expedition team reached the summit after a grueling 12‑hour climb. Upon arrival, they recorded the coordinates and captured photographs. They also noted that the weather had changed from clear to [1] within a short period.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) stormy
(b) stormiest
(c) storm
(d) storms
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective ‘stormy’ describes the weather condition.
उदाहरण: ‘तूफानी मौसम’ के लिये ‘stormy’ सही है।
[RULE] Adjective for weather

---

[Q1929] In order to improve road safety, the government has introduced stricter speed limits on highways. It also plans to install additional traffic signals at key intersections. The new regulations will be enforced by [1] officers on the road.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) police
(b) police’s
(c) police's
(d) Redundant
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use the noun ‘police’ as an attributive noun without an apostrophe.

[Q1980] The committee (1) ________ the new policy last week.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) adopted
(b) adopt
(c) adopts
(d) adopting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English.
वाक्य में विषय एकवचन है इसलिए क्रिया को भूतकाल में एकवचन रूप दिया जाता है।
[RULE] Subject-verb agreement in simple past tense

---

[Q1981] If I (1) ________ enough time, I would have visited the museum.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had
(b) have
(c) having
(d) has
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English.
दूसरी शर्त में भूतकाल पर आधारित क्रिया के लिए भूतकाल पूर्ण (past perfect) का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Second conditional: past perfect + would have

---

[Q1982] She is (1) ________ a teacher.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a
(b) an
(c) the
(d) no
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule in English.
स्वर ध्वनि से पहले 'an' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Indefinite article usage before vowel

---

[Q1983] We went to the beach, but it was (1) ________ because of the storm.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) empty
(b) emptied
(c) emptily
(d) emptiness
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English.
यहाँ 'empty' एक विशेषण है जो स्थिति बताता है।
[RULE] Adjectives describing nouns

[Q1984] The students were (1) ________ to finish the assignment before the dead line
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) eager
(b) eagerly
(c) eagerness
(d) eagered
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English.
'eager' एक विशेषण है।
[RULE] Adjective as complement after linking verb

[Q1985] He (1) ________ the book yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) read
(b) reads
(c) reading
(d) readed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English.
'read' का भूतकाल भी 'read' लिखा जाता है।
[RULE] Irregular verb: read/read

[Q1986] The (1) ________ was more expensive than the cheap one.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) expensive
(b) expensiver
(c) most expensive
(d) expensivly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English.
'expensive' का तुलनात्मक रूप 'more expensive' होता है।
[RULE] Comparative adjectives with 'more'

---

[Q1987] After finishing the work, she (1) ________ home.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) went
(b) go
(c) going
(d) goes
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English.
क्रिया का भूतकाल 'went' प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Past simple tense

[Q1988] They were (1) ________ for their efforts.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) proud
(b) proudly
(c) proudness
(d) prouded
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule in English.
'proudly' एक क्रिया विशेषण है।
[RULE] Adverb usage

---

[Q1989] I will call you as soon as I (1) ________.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) arrive
(b) arrived
(c) arriving
(d) arrives
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English.
'will' के साथ शर्त में वर्तमान काल का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Future with 'will' + present simple in if clauses

[Q1990] During the annual meeting, the manager announced that the company would expand its operations to three new countries, and the employees were very ___(1)___ about the opportunity. The CEO also mentioned that the financial results for the previous quarter were ___(2)___, which would allow for additional investment in research and development.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) elated
(b) doubted
(c) apologetic
(d) indifferent
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: The adjective should describe the employees' feelings. The word "elated" (उत्साहित) correctly fits the context of excitement.
उदा. Employees were elated about the new opportunity.
[RULE] Adjective usage

---

[Q1991] The ancient manuscript, which had been preserved for over five centuries, was finally opened by the archaeologists. They found that the ink used for the inscriptions was ___(1)___, indicating that the text had been written during the medieval period. The discovery was considered ___(2)___ for the study of early scripts.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) faded
(b) bright
(c) new
(d) blurred
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: The word describing the ink should indicate age. "Faded" (मंद) indicates old age.
उदा. The ink was faded, confirming its antiquity.
[RULE] Adjective describing condition

---

[Q1992] When the teacher asked the students to solve the complex equation, only a few could ___(1)___ the required steps, but most were unable to ___(2)___ the final answer. The teacher decided to provide additional practice worksheets to help them improve.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) understand
(b) solve
(c) forget
(d) ignore
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule: The verb should refer to performing the calculation. "Solve" (हल करना) is the appropriate choice.
उदा. They were able to solve the equation.
[RULE] Verb selection for mathematical context

---

[Q1993] The new policy requires all employees to submit their monthly reports by the first working day of the month. Failure to comply will result in a ___(1)___ deduction from the employee's salary, as stipulated in the company's handbook. Employees are advised to treat this matter ___(2)___ to avoid any inconvenience.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) significant
(b) insignificant
(c) minor
(d) negligible
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: The deduction is likely to be large. "Significant" (महत्वपूर्ण) fits the context.
उदा. A significant deduction will be applied.
[RULE] Adjective describing impact

---

[Q1994] In the experiment, the scientist added a small amount of catalyst to the reaction mixture. The reaction rate increased dramatically, indicating that the catalyst was ___(1)___ effective. However, the temperature of the mixture rose only slightly, suggesting that the catalyst was ___(2)___ in terms of thermal stability.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) highly
(b) moderately
(c) slightly
(d) negligibly
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule: The first blank requires an adverb describing effectiveness. "Moderately" (मध्यम) is suitable. The second blank needs an adverb describing stability. "Slightly" (थोड़ा) fits.
उदा. The catalyst was moderately effective and slightly stable.
[RULE] Adverb usage for degree

---

[Q1995] The government announced a new initiative aimed at reducing air pollution. Under the program, vehicles emitting more than ___(1)___ grams of pollutants per kilometer will be required to undergo mandatory inspections. The aim is to ensure that all vehicles meet the ___(2)___ standards set by the environmental agency.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) 120
(b) 80
(c) 200
(d) 50
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule: The blank refers to a numeric threshold. "80" (८०) is the standard limit for many countries.
उदा. Vehicles emitting more than 80 grams will be inspected.
[RULE] Numeric quantifier in policy context

---

[Q1996] During the archaeological dig, the team discovered a pottery shard with a unique pattern. The shard was found at a depth of ___(1)___ meters below the surface, indicating that the site had been buried for centuries. The find was considered ___(2)___ and could provide valuable insights into ancient trade routes.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) five
(b) ten
(c) twenty
(d) thirty
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Rule: The depth is significant. "Twenty" (बीस) meters suggests an ancient burial.
उदा. The shard was found 20 meters deep.
[RULE] Numeric depth description

---

[Q1997] The novel's protagonist, a young engineer, had to decide whether to accept a lucrative offer from a multinational corporation or stay with the local start up He ultimately chose the latter, believing that it would provide him with a sense of ___(1)___ and the chance to work on ___(2)___ projects that could benefit his community.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) achievement
(b) social media
(c) financial
(d) artistic
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: The blank should reflect personal fulfillment. "Achievement" (सफलता) is appropriate.
उदा. He sought a sense of achievement.
[RULE] Noun selection for motivation

---

[Q1998] The committee reviewed the proposal and found that the budget estimates were ___(1)___, but the time line was unrealistic. Consequently, the project was approved with the condition that the team would ___(2)___ the schedule to align with the available resources.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) accurate
(b) overestimated
(c) underestimated
(d) unreliable
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: The budget estimates should be correct. "Accurate" (सही) is the suitable choice.
उदा. The budget estimates were accurate.
[RULE] Adjective describing correctness

---

[Q1999] In the garden, the gardener planted a row of tulips, dahlias, and roses. Each flower required a different amount of sun light The roses needed the ___(1)___ amount of sun, whereas the dahlias could thrive in partial shade. The gardener was pleased with the ___(2)___ arrangement.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) least
(b) greatest
(c) average
(d) medium
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule: The roses need the most sunlight. "Greatest" (सबसे) fits.
उदा. The roses needed the greatest amount of sun.
[RULE] Adjective comparative for quantity

---

[Q2000] She has been working here _______ two years.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) during
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use 'for' with duration of time.
उदाहरण: She has been working here for two years.
[RULE] Preposition for duration

---

[Q2001] The book, which was written by a famous author, __________ published last year.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) has
(b) were
(c) was
(d) be
[ANS] c
[SOL]
To describe a past action, use simple past 'was'.
उदाहरण: The book was published last year.
[RULE] Simple Past for completed action

---

[Q2002] If I had known, I ___________ the mistake.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) would have avoided
(b) will have avoided
(c) had avoided
(d) would avoid
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Third conditional uses 'would have + past participle'.
उदाहरण: If I had known, I would have avoided the mistake.
[RULE] Third Conditional

---

[Q2003] The committee decided to postpone the meeting ________ the unexpected announcement.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) because of
(b) due to
(c) at the
(d) in spite of
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use 'due to' to indicate reason.
उदाहरण: The meeting was postponed due to the announcement.
[RULE] Preposition 'due to' for reason

---

[Q2004] Neither the manager nor the employees _______ willing to accept the new policy.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) is
(b) are
(c) was
(d) were
[ANS] b
[SOL]
In 'neither...nor', verb agrees with the nearer subject, plural.
उदाहरण: Neither the manager nor the employees are willing.
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement with Neither...Nor

---

[Q2005] The children, along with the teachers, ________ at the field trip.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was going
(b) were going
(c) is going
(d) have gone
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Subject is plural due to 'children', verb 'were'.
उदाहरण: The children were going to the field trip.
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement with plural subject

---

[Q2006] He will attend the conference only if the tickets ________ sold by Friday.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) have
(b) has
(c) had
(d) were
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Conditional clause uses simple past 'were' for unreal present.
उदाहरण: He will attend only if the tickets were sold by Friday.
[RULE] Second Conditional

---

[Q2007] The painting, which was stolen _______ last month, has been recovered.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) during
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Use 'during' with time period.
उदाहरण: The painting was stolen during last month.
[RULE] Preposition 'during' for time periods

---

[Q2008] She is someone who can _______ any problem with confidence.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) solve
(b) solving
(c) solved
(d) solvingly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After 'can', use base form verb.
उदाहरण: She can solve any problem.
[RULE] Modal verb + base form

---

[Q2009] Despite the rain, the game _______ as scheduled.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was played
(b) had been played
(c) will be played
(d) is played
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past simple 'was played' because event completed.
उदाहरण: The game was played as scheduled.
[RULE] Past simple for completed action

---

[Q2010] The project, which faced many challenges, _______ successfully after months of effort.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) has been completed
(b) was completed
(c) will be completed
(d) is completed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use present perfect when action is relevant to present.
उदाहरण: The project has been completed successfully.
[RULE] Present Perfect for recent relevance
